### Task 5: `pc.<id>` fields (`ui`)

**Files:**
- Create: `components/ui/include/ui_pc.h`, `components/ui/ui_pc.c`, `test/host/test_ui_pc.c`
- Modify: `components/pcstats/include/pc_msg.h`, `components/pcstats/pc_msg.c` (`pc_id_valid()`), `components/ui/CMakeLists.txt`, `components/ui/include/ui_fields.h`, `components/ui/include/ui_preset.h`, `components/ui/ui_fields.c`, `components/ui/ui_internal.h`, `components/ui/ui_preset.c`, `components/ui/ui_preset_json.c`, `components/ui/ui_widget.c`, `components/locale/include/lang.h`, `components/locale/lang_en.c`, `components/locale/lang_cs.c`, `components/webui/include/webui.h`, `main/app_ui.c`, `main/app_web.c`, `assets/icons/icons.txt`, `test/host/CMakeLists.txt`, `test/host/test_ui_preset.c`
- Regenerate: `components/gfx/icons/gfx_icons.c`, `components/gfx/include/gfx_icons.h` (`tools/gen_icons.sh`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pc_catalog_t`, `pc_values_t`, `pc_catalog_find()` (Task 1); `ui_set_num()`, `ui_set_range()` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST` (128), `UI_PC_FIELDS` (32), `bool ui_field_is_pc(ui_field_id_t)`; `ui_field_info()` of a PC field is one shared entry (`"pc"`, a number);
  - in `ui_context_t`: `const ui_pc_t *pc` and `const char (*pc_ids)[PC_ID_LEN]`;
  - `ui_pc.h`: `struct ui_pc` {`session`, `catalog`, `values`, `age_ms`, `interval_ms`} and `UI_PC_FRESH_INTERVALS` (3);
  - in `ui_presets_t`: `char pc_ids[UI_PC_FIELDS][PC_ID_LEN]`; `ui_presets_pc_field()`, `ui_presets_field_by_name()`, `ui_presets_field_name()`;
  - `bool pc_id_valid(const char *id)` (`pcstats`), shared by the messages and the file;
  - `UI_PRESETS_JSON_MAX` 64 KB, `WEBUI_BODY_MAX` and `WEBUI_REPLY_MAX` 72 KB;
  - the icon `gfx_icon_pc_16/24/48` (Material `computer`), and `LS_PC`.

A slot holds a field byte. A PC reading is field 128 + n, n its entry in a table of 32 ids kept with the presets, so a slot keeps its reading whatever the catalogue's order, and one the PC no longer sends shows its old id with "—" (spec §12a.3). The file names it `pc.<id>`; each parse builds the table afresh, in the order the file names the ids. The resolver takes the label, the unit, the decimals and the range from the catalogue, the value from the last message: fresh for three intervals, stale with its age after that, missing without a session, without a message or for a `null`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** The fields as the PC's catalogue and messages make them, in English and in Czech, and the presets that name them:

`test/host/test_ui_pc.c`:

```c
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L /* setenv() in fixture_zone() */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "context_fixtures.h"
#include "pc_msg.h"
#include "ui_fields.h"
#include "ui_pc.h"
#include "ui_preset.h"
#include "unity.h"

/* The PC's readings as fields (spec §5.1, §12a, D44): `pc.<id>` resolved from the session's last message, named in
 * presets.json, and kept in the presets' own table of reading ids. */

static ui_context_t s_ctx;
static ui_presets_t s_p;
static pc_catalog_t s_cat;
static pc_values_t s_vals;
static ui_pc_t s_pc;
static char s_err[UI_PRESET_ERR_LEN];
static char s_json[UI_PRESETS_JSON_MAX];

static void reading(int i, const char *id, const char *label, const char *unit, int decimals, float value)
{
    pc_reading_t *r = &s_cat.r[i];
    snprintf(r->id, sizeof(r->id), "%s", id);
    snprintf(r->label, sizeof(r->label), "%s", label);
    snprintf(r->unit, sizeof(r->unit), "%s", unit);
    r->decimals = (uint8_t)decimals;
    s_cat.count = (uint8_t)(i + 1);
    s_vals.has[i] = true;
    s_vals.v[i] = value;
}

void setUp(void)
{
    s_ctx = fixture_context();
    memset(&s_p, 0, sizeof(s_p));
    memset(&s_cat, 0, sizeof(s_cat));
    memset(&s_vals, 0, sizeof(s_vals));
    reading(0, "cpu_load", "CPU load", "%", 0, 37.4f);
    s_cat.r[0].has_min = s_cat.r[0].has_max = true;
    s_cat.r[0].max = 100.0f;
    reading(1, "cpu_temp", "CPU", "\xc2\xb0" "C", 1, 61.25f);
    reading(2, "ram_used", "RAM", "GB", 1, 21.34f);
    s_pc = (ui_pc_t){ .session = true, .catalog = &s_cat, .values = &s_vals, .age_ms = 400, .interval_ms = 1000 };
    s_ctx.pc = &s_pc;
    s_ctx.pc_ids = s_p.pc_ids;
}

void tearDown(void) {}

static ui_value_t pc(const char *id)
{
    ui_field_id_t f = ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, id);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, f);
    ui_value_t v;
    ui_resolve(&s_ctx, f, &v);
    return v;
}

static void test_a_reading_shows_with_its_label_unit_and_decimals(void)
{
    ui_value_t v = pc("cpu_temp");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_FRESH, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FK_NUMBER, v.kind);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("CPU", v.label);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("61.3", v.text); /* rounded half away from zero */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("61", v.short_text);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("\xc2\xb0" "C", v.unit);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(v.has_num);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(61.25f, v.num);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(v.has_range); /* the app gave none */
    v = pc("cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("37", v.text);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(v.has_range);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, v.range_min);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(100.0f, v.range_max);
    s_vals.v[2] = -0.05f;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("-0.1", pc("ram_used").text);
}

static void test_czech_writes_its_comma(void)
{
    s_ctx.lang = lang_get("cs");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("61,3", pc("cpu_temp").text);
}

static void test_a_huge_value_drops_its_decimals_and_still_shows(void)
{
    s_vals.v[2] = 3.5e9f; /* past what a 32-bit long holds in tenths */
    ui_value_t v = pc("ram_used");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_FRESH, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("3500000000", v.text);
    s_vals.v[2] = -1e30f;
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(strlen(pc("ram_used").text) > 0);
}

static void test_no_value_no_session_or_no_message_is_missing_with_the_label_kept(void)
{
    s_vals.has[1] = false; /* HWiNFO sends none */
    ui_value_t v = pc("cpu_temp");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_MISSING, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("CPU", v.label);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(v.has_num);
    s_pc.values = NULL; /* before the first message */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_MISSING, pc("cpu_load").state);
    s_pc.values = &s_vals;
    s_pc.session = false; /* the cached catalogue still names it */
    v = pc("cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_MISSING, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("CPU load", v.label);
    s_ctx.pc = NULL; /* no view at all */
    v = pc("cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_MISSING, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("cpu_load", v.label);
}

static void test_a_value_goes_stale_after_three_intervals(void)
{
    s_pc.age_ms = 3000;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_FRESH, pc("cpu_load").state);
    s_pc.age_ms = 3001;
    ui_value_t v = pc("cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_STALE, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT32(3, v.age_s);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("37", v.text); /* the value stays, marked */
    s_pc.interval_ms = 250;
    s_pc.age_ms = 800;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_STALE, pc("cpu_load").state);
}

static void test_a_renamed_reading_shows_its_old_id(void)
{
    ui_value_t v = pc("gpu_temp"); /* not in the catalogue: the owner renamed it on the PC */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VALUE_MISSING, v.state);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("gpu_temp", v.label);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FK_NUMBER, v.kind);
}

static void test_the_presets_keep_one_entry_a_reading(void)
{
    ui_field_id_t load = ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST, load);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_field_is_pc(load));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_field_is_pc(UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + 1, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "cpu_temp"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(load, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "cpu_load"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("cpu_load", s_p.pc_ids[0]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "CPU"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, ""));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "a_very_long_identifier"));
    for (int i = 2; i < UI_PC_FIELDS; i++) {
        char id[8];
        snprintf(id, sizeof(id), "r%d", i);
        TEST_ASSERT_NOT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, id));
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "one_more")); /* the table is full */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(load, ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "cpu_load"));       /* a known one still resolves */
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(ui_field_info(load));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FK_NUMBER, ui_field_info(load)->kind);
}

static void test_fields_are_named_as_the_file_names_them(void)
{
    ui_field_id_t load = ui_presets_pc_field(&s_p, "cpu_load");
    char name[24];
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(11, ui_presets_field_name(&s_p, load, name, sizeof(name)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc.cpu_load", name);
    ui_presets_field_name(&s_p, UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP, name, sizeof(name));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("env.temp", name);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, ui_presets_field_name(&s_p, UI_FIELD_NONE, name, sizeof(name)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", name);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, ui_presets_field_name(&s_p, (ui_field_id_t)(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + 5), name, sizeof(name)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(load, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "pc.cpu_load"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + 1, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "pc.ram_used"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "env.temp"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "pc.CPU"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "pc."));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, ui_presets_field_by_name(&s_p, "env.nope"));
}

static const char k_file[] =
    "{\"schema\":1,\"active\":\"pc\",\"presets\":["
    "{\"id\":\"pc\",\"name\":\"PC\",\"layout\":\"grid\",\"slots\":{\"g1\":\"pc.cpu_load\",\"g2\":\"pc.cpu_temp\","
    "\"g3\":\"env.temp\",\"g4\":\"pc.cpu_load\"}},"
    "{\"id\":\"cells\",\"name\":\"Cells\",\"layout\":\"split\",\"split\":{\"split\":\"columns\",\"ratio\":\"1/2\","
    "\"a\":{\"field\":\"pc.ram_used\"},\"b\":{\"field\":\"pc.gone\"}}}]}";

static void test_presets_json_names_pc_readings_and_keeps_them(void)
{
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(k_file, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST, s_p.presets[0].slots[0]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + 1, s_p.presets[0].slots[1]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP, s_p.presets[0].slots[2]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST, s_p.presets[0].slots[3]); /* one entry for both */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + 2, s_p.presets[1].slots[0]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("gone", s_p.pc_ids[3]); /* a reading the PC no longer sends stays named */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", s_p.pc_ids[4]);
    size_t n = ui_presets_to_json(&s_p, s_json, sizeof(s_json));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(n > 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(strstr(s_json, "\"g1\":\"pc.cpu_load\""));
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(strstr(s_json, "\"field\":\"pc.gone\""));
    ui_presets_t back;
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(s_json, &back, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(&s_p, &back, sizeof(back));
}

static void test_presets_json_refuses_a_bad_pc_reading(void)
{
    static const char k_bad[] = "{\"schema\":1,\"presets\":[{\"id\":\"pc\",\"name\":\"PC\",\"layout\":\"grid\","
                                "\"slots\":{\"g1\":\"pc.CPU\"}}]}";
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_from_json(k_bad, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("preset \"pc\": unknown field \"pc.CPU\"", s_err);
    /* six grid presets of six readings each: 36 names, 33 of them different */
    char *const end = s_json + sizeof(s_json);
    char *p = s_json + snprintf(s_json, sizeof(s_json), "{\"schema\":1,\"presets\":[");
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "%s{\"id\":\"p%d\",\"name\":\"P\",\"layout\":\"grid\",\"slots\":{", i ? "," : "", i);
        for (int g = 0; g < 6; g++) {
            int r = i * 6 + g < 33 ? i * 6 + g : 0;
            p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "%s\"g%d\":\"pc.r%d\"", g ? "," : "", g + 1, r);
        }
        p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "}}");
    }
    strcpy(p, "]}");
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_from_json(s_json, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("preset \"p5\": the presets name more than 32 PC readings", s_err);
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_a_reading_shows_with_its_label_unit_and_decimals);
    RUN_TEST(test_czech_writes_its_comma);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_huge_value_drops_its_decimals_and_still_shows);
    RUN_TEST(test_no_value_no_session_or_no_message_is_missing_with_the_label_kept);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_value_goes_stale_after_three_intervals);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_renamed_reading_shows_its_old_id);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_presets_keep_one_entry_a_reading);
    RUN_TEST(test_fields_are_named_as_the_file_names_them);
    RUN_TEST(test_presets_json_names_pc_readings_and_keeps_them);
    RUN_TEST(test_presets_json_refuses_a_bad_pc_reading);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The fields and their view.**

`components/ui/include/ui_pc.h`:

```c
#pragma once

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "pc_msg.h"
#include "ui_fields.h"

/*
 * The PC view (spec §12a, M7, D44): what the app gathers for the pc.* fields. Pure C, host-buildable.
 */

#define UI_PC_FRESH_INTERVALS 3 /* a reading is fresh for three intervals after its message (spec §5.1) */

struct ui_pc {
    bool session;                /* a session runs (spec §12a.4); without one every pc.* field is missing */
    const pc_catalog_t *catalog; /* the session's catalogue, or the cached one (/fs/state/pc.json); NULL for none */
    const pc_values_t *values;   /* the last readings message's values, by the catalogue's order; NULL before one */
    uint32_t age_ms;             /* since that message */
    uint32_t interval_ms;        /* the session's interval */
};
```


`components/ui/ui_pc.c`:

```c
#include "ui_pc.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "ui_internal.h"

/* The PC's readings as fields (spec §5.1, §12a, D44). */

#define TEXT_LIMIT 2e9 /* past this a scaled value leaves a 32-bit long (lang_format_decimal()) */

/* `v` with `decimals` in the language's form; a value too large for them drops them, and then prints whole. */
static void number_text(const lang_t *lang, float v, int decimals, char *out, size_t size)
{
    double scaled = (double)v;
    for (int i = 0; i < decimals; i++) {
        scaled *= 10.0;
    }
    if (fabs(scaled) < TEXT_LIMIT) {
        lang_format_decimal(lang, lround(scaled), decimals, out, size);
    } else if (fabs((double)v) < TEXT_LIMIT) {
        lang_format_decimal(lang, lround((double)v), 0, out, size);
    } else {
        snprintf(out, size, "%.0f", (double)v);
    }
}

bool ui_resolve_pc(const ui_context_t *ctx, ui_field_id_t field, ui_value_t *out)
{
    if (!ui_field_is_pc(field)) {
        return false;
    }
    const char *id = ctx->pc_ids != NULL ? ctx->pc_ids[field - UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST] : "";
    if (id[0]) {
        out->label = id; /* a reading the catalogue doesn't name: the PC no longer sends it */
    }
    const ui_pc_t *pc = ctx->pc;
    int i = pc != NULL && pc->catalog != NULL && id[0] ? pc_catalog_find(pc->catalog, id) : -1;
    if (i < 0) {
        return true;
    }
    const pc_reading_t *r = &pc->catalog->r[i];
    out->label = r->label[0] ? r->label : id;
    snprintf(out->unit, sizeof(out->unit), "%s", r->unit);
    if (r->has_min && r->has_max && r->min < r->max) {
        ui_set_range(out, r->min, r->max);
    }
    if (!pc->session || pc->values == NULL || !pc->values->has[i]) {
        return true;
    }
    float v = pc->values->v[i];
    ui_set_num(out, v);
    number_text(ctx->lang, v, r->decimals, out->text, sizeof(out->text));
    if (r->decimals > 0) {
        number_text(ctx->lang, v, 0, out->short_text, sizeof(out->short_text));
    }
    bool fresh = pc->age_ms <= UI_PC_FRESH_INTERVALS * pc->interval_ms;
    out->state = fresh ? UI_VALUE_FRESH : UI_VALUE_STALE;
    out->age_s = pc->age_ms / 1000;
    return true;
}
```

`components/ui/ui_preset.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/ui/ui_preset.c
+++ b/components/ui/ui_preset.c
@@ -142,6 +142,44 @@ static size_t title_bytes(const char *text)
     return n;

+ui_field_id_t ui_presets_pc_field(ui_presets_t *p, const char *id)
+{
+    if (!pc_id_valid(id)) {
+        return UI_FIELD_NONE;
+    }
+    for (int n = 0; n < UI_PC_FIELDS; n++) {
+        if (p->pc_ids[n][0] == '\0') {
+            snprintf(p->pc_ids[n], sizeof(p->pc_ids[n]), "%s", id); /* a new entry */
+            return (ui_field_id_t)(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + n);
+        }
+        if (strcmp(p->pc_ids[n], id) == 0) {
+            return (ui_field_id_t)(UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST + n);
+        }
+    }
+    return UI_FIELD_NONE;
+}
+
+ui_field_id_t ui_presets_field_by_name(ui_presets_t *p, const char *name)
+{
+    if (name != NULL && strncmp(name, "pc.", 3) == 0) {
+        return ui_presets_pc_field(p, name + 3);
+    }
+    return ui_field_by_name(name);
+}
+
+size_t ui_presets_field_name(const ui_presets_t *p, ui_field_id_t field, char *out, size_t size)
+{
+    int n = 0;
+    if (ui_field_is_pc(field)) {
+        const char *id = p->pc_ids[field - UI_FIELD_PC_FIRST];
+        n = id[0] ? snprintf(out, size, "pc.%s", id) : snprintf(out, size, "%s", "");
+    } else {
+        const ui_field_info_t *info = ui_field_info(field);
+        n = snprintf(out, size, "%s", info != NULL ? info->id : "");
+    }
+    return n > 0 && (size_t)n < size ? (size_t)n : 0;
+}
+
 const char *ui_presets_title(const ui_presets_t *p, int preset, int slot)
 {
     int n;
```

### Task 6: The charts' history (`ui`)

**Files:**
- Create: `components/ui/include/ui_history.h`, `components/ui/ui_history.c`, `test/host/test_ui_history.c`
- Modify: `components/ui/CMakeLists.txt`, `test/host/CMakeLists.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`ui_history.h`): `UI_HIST_SERIES` (16), `UI_HIST_VALUES` (3 600), `UI_HIST_MINUTES` (1 440), `UI_HIST_NAME_LEN` (20); `ui_series_t`, `ui_history_t` (`struct ui_history`, from Task 8), `ui_hist_column_t` {`n`, `brk`, `min`, `mean`, `max`};
  - `void ui_history_init(ui_history_t *, ui_series_t *mem)`;
  - `int ui_history_track(ui_history_t *, const char *const names[], int count)`;
  - `ui_series_t *ui_history_find(const ui_history_t *, const char *name)`;
  - `void ui_series_push(ui_series_t *, uint32_t now_ms, float v)`, `void ui_series_break(ui_series_t *, uint32_t now_ms)`;
  - `int ui_series_columns(const ui_series_t *, uint32_t now_ms, uint32_t span_ms, int width, int first, int count, ui_hist_column_t *cols)`.

Spec §5.3's History: for each field a chart shows, its last 3 600 values at their own rate and 24 h of one-minute minimum, mean and maximum, in PSRAM, by the field's name in `presets.json` (a field byte can go to another reading at the next save). Each value keeps its time on the caller's monotonic clock in ms, which may wrap, so a gap stays a gap; an age is the unsigned difference to now, and a time up to 60 s ahead of now counts as now, so a value weeks old (a board on USB with the PC off) reads as old, never as new; a break (a reconnect, a `null`, a value no number holds) keeps the line from joining across it. A chart's columns come from the values while they cover its span (their oldest's age and one interval), else from the minutes; a day's silence clears the minutes. A chart takes its columns a few at a time, as `ui` has no PSRAM of its own for 400 of them.

About 47 KB a series, where the spec estimated 32 KB: the times are what the estimate left out.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`test/host/test_ui_history.c`:

```c
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "ui_history.h"
#include "unity.h"

/* The history behind the charts (spec §5.3, D44): each series' last 3 600 values at their own rate and 24 h of
 * one-minute minimum, mean and maximum, and the pixel columns a chart draws from them. RAM only: the app keeps it
 * in PSRAM. */

static ui_series_t *s_mem;
static ui_history_t s_h;
static ui_series_t *s;
static ui_hist_column_t s_cols[UI_HIST_VALUES];

void setUp(void)
{
    s_mem = calloc(UI_HIST_SERIES, sizeof(ui_series_t));
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(s_mem);
    ui_history_init(&s_h, s_mem);
    const char *const names[] = { "pc.cpu_load" };
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, ui_history_track(&s_h, names, 1));
    s = ui_history_find(&s_h, "pc.cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(s);
}

void tearDown(void)
{
    free(s_mem);
}

/* `minutes` minutes of a value every 250 ms from t 0, each the index of its minute; the last push's time. */
static uint32_t quarter_seconds(int minutes)
{
    uint32_t t = 0;
    for (int k = 0; k < minutes * 240; k++) {
        t = (uint32_t)k * 250;
        ui_series_push(s, t, (float)(k / 240));
    }
    return t;
}

static void test_one_value_a_column_newest_at_the_right(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, 1000 + i * 1000, (float)i);
    }
    uint32_t now = 1000 + 59 * 1000;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(60, ui_series_columns(s, now, 60000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols));
    for (int c = 0; c < 60; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, s_cols[c].n);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)c, s_cols[c].mean);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)c, s_cols[c].min);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)c, s_cols[c].max);
        TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(s_cols[c].brk);
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(30, ui_series_columns(s, now, 30000, 30, 0, 30, s_cols)); /* half the span: the newer half */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(30.0f, s_cols[0].mean);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(59.0f, s_cols[29].mean);
}

static void test_a_column_of_many_values_keeps_their_range_and_mean(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 3600; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, i * 1000, (float)(i % 20)); /* 0..19 in every 20 s */
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(180, ui_series_columns(s, 3599 * 1000, 3600000, 180, 0, 180, s_cols)); /* an hour at 1 s */
    for (int c = 0; c < 180; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(20, s_cols[c].n);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, s_cols[c].min);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(19.0f, s_cols[c].max);
        TEST_ASSERT_FLOAT_WITHIN(0.001f, 9.5f, s_cols[c].mean);
    }
}

static void test_columns_without_values_are_empty(void)
{
    ui_series_push(s, 10000, 5.0f);
    ui_series_push(s, 70000, 7.0f);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(2, ui_series_columns(s, 70000, 120000, 120, 0, 120, s_cols));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, s_cols[59].n); /* 60 s before now */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(5.0f, s_cols[59].mean);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, s_cols[119].n);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s_cols[0].n);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s_cols[100].n);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, ui_series_columns(s, 270000, 60000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols)); /* all of them older than the span */
}

static void test_the_ring_keeps_the_last_3600_values(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 3700; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, i * 1000, (float)i);
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_HIST_VALUES, s->count);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(3600, ui_series_columns(s, 3699 * 1000, 3600000, 3600, 0, 3600, s_cols));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(100.0f, s_cols[0].mean);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(3699.0f, s_cols[3599].mean);
}

static void test_a_span_past_the_values_draws_from_the_minutes(void)
{
    uint32_t now = quarter_seconds(20); /* 4 800 values: the last 3 600 cover 15 min */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(20, ui_series_columns(s, now, 3600000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols));
    for (int c = 0; c < 40; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s_cols[c].n);
    }
    for (int m = 0; m < 20; m++) { /* minute m in column 40 + m, the one in progress last */
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MESSAGE(1, s_cols[40 + m].n, "one minute a column");
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)m, s_cols[40 + m].mean);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)m, s_cols[40 + m].min);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)m, s_cols[40 + m].max);
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(15, ui_series_columns(s, now, 900000, 15, 0, 15, s_cols)); /* 15 min: still the values */
}

static void test_24_hours_of_minutes(void)
{
    uint32_t now = quarter_seconds(25 * 60);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_HIST_MINUTES, s->mcount);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(144, ui_series_columns(s, now, 24u * 3600000u, 144, 0, 144, s_cols)); /* 10 min a column */
    for (int c = 0; c < 144; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(10, s_cols[c].n);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)(60 + 10 * c), s_cols[c].min);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)(69 + 10 * c), s_cols[c].max);
        TEST_ASSERT_FLOAT_WITHIN(0.001f, 64.5f + 10.0f * (float)c, s_cols[c].mean);
    }
}

static void test_a_break_keeps_the_line_apart(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i <= 10; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, i * 1000, 1.0f);
    }
    ui_series_break(s, 10500); /* the session reconnected */
    for (uint32_t i = 11; i <= 20; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, i * 1000, 2.0f);
    }
    ui_series_columns(s, 20000, 30000, 30, 0, 30, s_cols);
    for (int c = 0; c < 30; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MESSAGE(c == 20, s_cols[c].brk, "only the first column after the break");
    }
    ui_series_push(s, 21000, NAN); /* a value no number holds is a break too */
    ui_series_push(s, 22000, 3.0f);
    ui_series_columns(s, 22000, 30000, 30, 0, 30, s_cols);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(s_cols[29].brk);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s_cols[28].n);
    ui_series_break(s, 23000); /* a break after the last value marks nothing */
    ui_series_columns(s, 23000, 30000, 30, 0, 30, s_cols);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(s_cols[28].brk); /* 22 s, as before */
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(s_cols[29].brk);
}

static void test_a_break_inside_a_minute_keeps_the_minutes_apart(void)
{
    uint32_t t = 0;
    for (int k = 0; k < 20 * 240; k++) {
        t = (uint32_t)k * 250;
        ui_series_push(s, t, 1.0f);
        if (k == 10 * 240) {
            ui_series_break(s, t + 125); /* within minute 10 */
        }
    }
    ui_series_columns(s, t, 3600000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols);
    for (int c = 0; c < 60; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MESSAGE(c == 50, s_cols[c].brk, "minute 10's column alone");
    }
}

static void test_a_long_silence_clears_the_minutes(void)
{
    uint32_t now = quarter_seconds(20);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(19, s->mcount);
    now += 25u * 3600000u;
    ui_series_push(s, now, 4.0f);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s->mcount);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, ui_series_columns(s, now, 24u * 3600000u, 144, 0, 144, s_cols));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(4.0f, s_cols[143].mean);
}

static void test_the_clock_may_wrap(void)
{
    uint32_t start = 0xFFFFFFFFu - 30000u;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, start + i * 1000, (float)i);
    }
    uint32_t now = start + 59 * 1000;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(60, ui_series_columns(s, now, 60000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols));
    for (int c = 0; c < 60; c++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT((float)c, s_cols[c].mean);
    }
}

static void test_a_few_columns_at_a_time_make_the_same_chart(void)
{
    static ui_hist_column_t part[50];
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 3000; i++) {
        if (i % 700 == 350) {
            ui_series_break(s, i * 1000 + 500);
        }
        ui_series_push(s, i * 1000, (float)((i * 7919) % 101));
    }
    uint32_t now = 2999 * 1000;
    int whole = ui_series_columns(s, now, 3600000, 390, 0, 390, s_cols);
    int sum = 0;
    for (int first = 0; first < 390; first += 50) {
        int n = first + 50 <= 390 ? 50 : 390 - first;
        sum += ui_series_columns(s, now, 3600000, 390, first, n, part);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(&s_cols[first], part, (size_t)n * sizeof(part[0]));
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(whole, sum);
}

/* A board on USB keeps its history while the PC stays off for weeks: those values are weeks old, not new. */
static void test_values_from_weeks_ago_stay_weeks_ago(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 600; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s, 1000 + i * 1000, (float)i);
    }
    uint32_t now = 1000 + 30u * 86400000u; /* 30 days on */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, ui_series_columns(s, now, 60000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, ui_series_columns(s, 1000 + 599 * 1000 - 500, 60000, 60, 0, 60, s_cols) > 0); /* a clock a
                                                                     moment behind the last value: it counts as now */
}

static void test_tracking_keeps_known_series_and_frees_the_rest(void)
{
    const char *const ab[] = { "env.temp", "pc.gpu_temp" };
    ui_history_track(&s_h, ab, 2);
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_history_find(&s_h, "pc.cpu_load")); /* no chart shows it any more */
    ui_series_t *temp = ui_history_find(&s_h, "env.temp");
    ui_series_push(temp, 1000, 21.5f);
    const char *const bc[] = { "env.temp", "pc.gpu_temp", "", "env.temp", "pc.cpu_load" };
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(3, ui_history_track(&s_h, bc, 5)); /* "" and the repeat count for nothing */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_PTR(temp, ui_history_find(&s_h, "env.temp"));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1, temp->count); /* kept with its values */
    ui_series_t *load = ui_history_find(&s_h, "pc.cpu_load");
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(load);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, load->count); /* back, but from nothing */
    char names[UI_HIST_SERIES + 1][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
    const char *list[UI_HIST_SERIES + 1];
    for (int i = 0; i <= UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        snprintf(names[i], sizeof(names[i]), "pc.r%d", i);
        list[i] = names[i];
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_HIST_SERIES, ui_history_track(&s_h, list, UI_HIST_SERIES + 1));
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(ui_history_find(&s_h, "pc.r15"));
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_history_find(&s_h, "pc.r16")); /* 16 series at most */
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_history_find(&s_h, ""));
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_one_value_a_column_newest_at_the_right);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_column_of_many_values_keeps_their_range_and_mean);
    RUN_TEST(test_columns_without_values_are_empty);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_ring_keeps_the_last_3600_values);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_span_past_the_values_draws_from_the_minutes);
    RUN_TEST(test_24_hours_of_minutes);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_break_keeps_the_line_apart);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_break_inside_a_minute_keeps_the_minutes_apart);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_long_silence_clears_the_minutes);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_clock_may_wrap);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_few_columns_at_a_time_make_the_same_chart);
    RUN_TEST(test_values_from_weeks_ago_stay_weeks_ago);
    RUN_TEST(test_tracking_keeps_known_series_and_frees_the_rest);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the history.**

`components/ui/include/ui_history.h`:

```c
#pragma once

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/*
 * The history behind the charts (spec §5.3, M7, D44): for each field a chart on any preset shows, its last values
 * at their own rate and 24 h of one-minute minimum, mean and maximum. RAM only: the app keeps the series in PSRAM,
 * and a restart or a deep sleep loses them. Times are the caller's monotonic clock in ms, which may wrap. Pure C,
 * host-buildable.
 */

#define UI_HIST_SERIES 16
#define UI_HIST_VALUES 3600 /* an hour at 1 s, 15 min at 250 ms */
#define UI_HIST_MINUTES 1440
#define UI_HIST_NAME_LEN 20 /* a field's name in presets.json: "pc." and a 15-character id */

typedef struct {
    char name[UI_HIST_NAME_LEN]; /* "" for a free series */
    uint16_t head, count;        /* the values' ring: the next goes at head */
    uint16_t mhead, mcount;      /* the finished minutes' ring, the same way */
    bool gathering;              /* a minute is being gathered, since minute_ms */
    bool cur_break;
    uint32_t minute_ms;
    uint32_t cur_n;
    float cur_min, cur_max, cur_sum;
    float v[UI_HIST_VALUES]; /* NaN: a break, where the line stops */
    uint32_t t_ms[UI_HIST_VALUES];
    float mmin[UI_HIST_MINUTES], mmean[UI_HIST_MINUTES], mmax[UI_HIST_MINUTES]; /* NaN mean: no value that minute */
    uint8_t mbreak[UI_HIST_MINUTES];
} ui_series_t;

typedef struct {
    ui_series_t *series; /* UI_HIST_SERIES of them */
} ui_history_t;

/* A chart's pixel column. */
typedef struct {
    uint16_t n;            /* the values in it, or the minutes; 0: none */
    bool brk;              /* a break since the column before with values: the line doesn't join them */
    float min, mean, max;
} ui_hist_column_t;

/* `mem` holds UI_HIST_SERIES series; all of them free. */
void ui_history_init(ui_history_t *h, ui_series_t *mem);
/* The series to keep, by field name: those already kept stay with their values, the others are freed, and new
 * names take free series, from nothing, until all 16 are taken. "" and repeats count for nothing. Returns how many
 * series are kept. */
int ui_history_track(ui_history_t *h, const char *const names[], int count);
/* The series kept for `name`, or NULL. */
ui_series_t *ui_history_find(const ui_history_t *h, const char *name);
/* A value at `now_ms`; one no number holds (NaN, an infinity) is a break. */
void ui_series_push(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, float v);
/* A break: the line stops here, as when a session reconnects. */
void ui_series_break(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms);
/* The last `span_ms` before `now_ms` in `width` columns, oldest at the left: from the values while they cover the
 * span (their oldest's age and one interval), else from the minutes. `cols` gets the `count` columns from `first`,
 * so a chart can take them a few at a time. Returns how many of those have values. */
int ui_series_columns(const ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, uint32_t span_ms, int width, int first, int count,
                      ui_hist_column_t *cols);
```


`components/ui/ui_history.c`:

```c
#include "ui_history.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <string.h>

/* The history behind the charts (spec §5.3, D44). */

#define MINUTE_MS 60000u

static void clear(ui_series_t *s, const char *name)
{
    memset(s, 0, sizeof(*s));
    strncpy(s->name, name, sizeof(s->name) - 1);
}

void ui_history_init(ui_history_t *h, ui_series_t *mem)
{
    h->series = mem;
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        clear(&mem[i], "");
    }
}

static bool named(const char *const names[], int count, const char *name)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (names[i] != NULL && strcmp(names[i], name) == 0) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

ui_series_t *ui_history_find(const ui_history_t *h, const char *name)
{
    for (int i = 0; name != NULL && name[0] && i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        if (strcmp(h->series[i].name, name) == 0) {
            return &h->series[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

int ui_history_track(ui_history_t *h, const char *const names[], int count)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        if (h->series[i].name[0] && !named(names, count, h->series[i].name)) {
            h->series[i].name[0] = '\0'; /* freed: cleared when it is taken again */
        }
    }
    for (int k = 0; k < count; k++) {
        const char *name = names[k];
        if (name == NULL || !name[0] || strlen(name) >= UI_HIST_NAME_LEN || ui_history_find(h, name) != NULL) {
            continue;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
            if (!h->series[i].name[0]) {
                clear(&h->series[i], name);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    int kept = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        kept += h->series[i].name[0] != '\0';
    }
    return kept;
}

/* The minute being gathered goes into the ring. */
static void finish_minute(ui_series_t *s)
{
    int i = s->mhead;
    bool any = s->cur_n > 0;
    s->mmin[i] = any ? s->cur_min : NAN;
    s->mmean[i] = any ? s->cur_sum / (float)s->cur_n : NAN;
    s->mmax[i] = any ? s->cur_max : NAN;
    s->mbreak[i] = s->cur_break;
    s->mhead = (uint16_t)((s->mhead + 1) % UI_HIST_MINUTES);
    if (s->mcount < UI_HIST_MINUTES) {
        s->mcount++;
    }
    s->cur_n = 0;
    s->cur_sum = 0;
    s->cur_break = false;
}

/* Brings the minutes up to `now_ms`: each minute passed is finished, those without values empty; past a day of
 * them, they all go. */
static void advance(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms)
{
    if (!s->gathering) {
        s->gathering = true;
        s->minute_ms = now_ms;
        return;
    }
    uint32_t steps = (now_ms - s->minute_ms) / MINUTE_MS;
    if (steps > UI_HIST_MINUTES) {
        s->mhead = s->mcount = 0;
        s->cur_n = 0;
        s->cur_sum = 0;
        s->cur_break = false;
        s->minute_ms = now_ms;
        return;
    }
    for (uint32_t k = 0; k < steps; k++) {
        finish_minute(s);
        s->minute_ms += MINUTE_MS;
    }
}

static void store(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, float v)
{
    s->v[s->head] = v;
    s->t_ms[s->head] = now_ms;
    s->head = (uint16_t)((s->head + 1) % UI_HIST_VALUES);
    if (s->count < UI_HIST_VALUES) {
        s->count++;
    }
}

void ui_series_break(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms)
{
    advance(s, now_ms);
    s->cur_break = true;
    store(s, now_ms, NAN);
}

void ui_series_push(ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, float v)
{
    if (!isfinite(v)) {
        ui_series_break(s, now_ms);
        return;
    }
    advance(s, now_ms);
    if (s->cur_n == 0 || v < s->cur_min) {
        s->cur_min = v;
    }
    if (s->cur_n == 0 || v > s->cur_max) {
        s->cur_max = v;
    }
    s->cur_sum += v;
    s->cur_n++;
    store(s, now_ms, v);
}

/* How long before `now_ms` `t_ms` was, the clock wrapping: a time up to a minute ahead is now (a clock read a moment
 * before the value), anything else that far back, weeks included, until the 49.7 days of a 32-bit clock. */
static uint32_t age(uint32_t now_ms, uint32_t t_ms)
{
    uint32_t a = now_ms - t_ms;
    return a > 0u - 60000u ? 0 : a;
}

typedef struct {
    ui_hist_column_t *cols; /* columns first .. first + count - 1 */
    int width, first, count;
    uint32_t span_ms;
    int last; /* the oldest column with values so far, walking from the newest, kept or not; -1: none */
    int filled;
} walk_t;

static void add(walk_t *w, uint32_t a, float lo, float mean, float hi)
{
    int c = w->width - 1 - (int)((uint64_t)a * (uint64_t)w->width / w->span_ms);
    w->last = c;
    if (c < w->first || c >= w->first + w->count) {
        return;
    }
    ui_hist_column_t *col = &w->cols[c - w->first];
    if (col->n == 0) {
        col->min = lo;
        col->max = hi;
        col->mean = 0;
        w->filled++;
    }
    col->min = lo < col->min ? lo : col->min;
    col->max = hi > col->max ? hi : col->max;
    col->mean += mean; /* a sum until the end */
    col->n++;
}

/* A break older than the values walked so far: the oldest of them doesn't join what comes before it. */
static void mark_break(walk_t *w)
{
    if (w->last >= w->first && w->last < w->first + w->count) {
        w->cols[w->last - w->first].brk = true;
    }
}

static void from_values(const ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, walk_t *w)
{
    for (int k = 0; k < s->count; k++) {
        int i = (s->head + UI_HIST_VALUES - 1 - k) % UI_HIST_VALUES;
        uint32_t a = age(now_ms, s->t_ms[i]);
        if (a >= w->span_ms) {
            break;
        }
        if (isnan(s->v[i])) {
            mark_break(w);
        } else {
            add(w, a, s->v[i], s->v[i], s->v[i]);
        }
    }
}

static void from_minutes(const ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, walk_t *w)
{
    if (!s->gathering) {
        return;
    }
    uint32_t a = age(now_ms, s->minute_ms + MINUTE_MS / 2); /* the minute being gathered, at its middle */
    if (a < w->span_ms) {
        if (s->cur_n > 0) {
            add(w, a, s->cur_min, s->cur_sum / (float)s->cur_n, s->cur_max);
        }
        if (s->cur_break) {
            mark_break(w);
        }
    }
    for (int k = 0; k < s->mcount; k++) {
        int i = (s->mhead + UI_HIST_MINUTES - 1 - k) % UI_HIST_MINUTES;
        a = age(now_ms, s->minute_ms - (uint32_t)(k + 1) * MINUTE_MS + MINUTE_MS / 2);
        if (a >= w->span_ms) {
            break;
        }
        if (!isnan(s->mmean[i])) {
            add(w, a, s->mmin[i], s->mmean[i], s->mmax[i]);
        }
        if (s->mbreak[i]) {
            mark_break(w);
        }
    }
}

/* Whether the values cover `span_ms`: all of them since the first, or their oldest's age and one interval. */
static bool values_cover(const ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, uint32_t span_ms)
{
    if (s->count < UI_HIST_VALUES) {
        return true;
    }
    int oldest = s->head, newest = (s->head + UI_HIST_VALUES - 1) % UI_HIST_VALUES;
    uint32_t interval = (s->t_ms[newest] - s->t_ms[oldest]) / (UI_HIST_VALUES - 1);
    return (uint64_t)age(now_ms, s->t_ms[oldest]) + interval >= span_ms;
}

int ui_series_columns(const ui_series_t *s, uint32_t now_ms, uint32_t span_ms, int width, int first, int count,
                      ui_hist_column_t *cols)
{
    if (count <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    memset(cols, 0, (size_t)count * sizeof(cols[0]));
    if (width <= 0 || span_ms == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    walk_t w = { .cols = cols, .width = width, .first = first, .count = count, .span_ms = span_ms, .last = -1 };
    if (values_cover(s, now_ms, span_ms)) {
        from_values(s, now_ms, &w);
    } else {
        from_minutes(s, now_ms, &w);
    }
    for (int c = 0; c < count; c++) {
        if (cols[c].n > 0) {
            cols[c].mean /= (float)cols[c].n;
        }
    }
    return w.filled;
}
```

### Task 7: The four visuals (`ui`)

**Files:**
- Create: `components/ui/include/ui_visual.h`, `components/ui/ui_visual.c`, `test/host/test_ui_visual.c`, `test/host/visual_fixtures.h`, `test/host/test_ui_visual_golden.c`, `test/host/render_visual.c`, `test/host/golden/vis_*.pbm` (10)
- Modify: `components/ui/CMakeLists.txt`, `test/host/CMakeLists.txt`, `tools/render.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ui_value_t`'s number and range (Task 4); `ui_series_t`, `ui_series_columns()` (Task 6).
- Produces (`ui_visual.h`): `ui_vis_as_t` (`UI_VIS_VALUE`, `_CHART`, `_BAR`, `_RING`, `_GAUGE`), `ui_vis_limit_t` (`UI_LIMIT_FIELD`, `_VALUE`, `_AUTO`), the spans' limits (`UI_SPAN_MIN_S` 10, `UI_SPAN_VALUES_MAX_S` 3 600, `UI_SPAN_HOURS_MIN_S` 7 200, `UI_SPAN_MAX_S` 86 400, `UI_SPAN_DEFAULT_S` 60), `ui_visual_t` {`as`, `min_mode`, `max_mode`, `numbers`, `span_s`, `min`, `max`};
  - `bool ui_visual_fits(ui_vis_as_t, int w, int h)`, `bool ui_visual_span_ok(uint32_t)`, `void ui_visual_span_text(uint32_t, char *, size_t)`;
  - `void ui_visual_range(const ui_visual_t *, const ui_value_t *, bool have_view, float view_lo, float view_hi, float *lo, float *hi)`;
  - `void ui_visual_draw(gfx_fb_t *, gfx_rect_t, const ui_value_t *, const ui_visual_t *, const ui_series_t *, uint32_t now_ms, const lang_t *)`;
  - the golden sheets `fixture_visual(name, fb)` and `k_visual_fixtures`, and `build-host/render_visual <sheet> <out.pbm>`.

The looks are the spike's (branch `spike/m7`, its renders in `captures/spike-m7/`), approved by the owner on 2026-10-19, now drawn from a resolved value, in its language, and a real history:
- **Chart**: a 1 px line over a light dither, newest at the right; a column holding several values draws their range half grey with their mean as the line; the line runs straight across the columns between values and stops at a break. With numbers: the latest value over the top left with a white halo, the range's top at the right of the label's line, its bottom and the span in the corners; without: the value on the label's line. An automatic range is the values in view with a tenth's room, on a tenth of a round step. Neither a value nor a history in view: the label and the baseline alone.
- **Bar**: the label and value on a line, the bar under them; one line under 44 px; upright in a tall cell.
- **Ring**: from 12 o'clock, the value inside, else beside a smaller ring, a pie under 30 px.
- **Gauge**: a 240° arc, the value inside, the range's ends under the arc's ends from 70 px.
- Room: 80×40 for a chart, 60×40 a gauge, 40×40 a ring, 60×16 a bar. Bounds: the visual's own, else the field's range, else automatic (for a bar, a ring or a gauge, 0 to the round number past the value). A number gives up its unit before its decimals, as the spike's did.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests:** which visual has room, the spans and their words, the ranges, and that none draws outside its cell:

`test/host/test_ui_visual.c`:

```c
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "gfx.h"
#include "lang.h"
#include "ui_visual.h"
#include "unity.h"

/* Show as (spec §5.3, D44): which visuals have room, a chart's spans, the range each draws between, and that none
 * draws outside its cell. Their looks are pinned by the goldens (test_ui_visual_golden.c). */

static uint8_t s_buf[400 * 300 / 8];
static gfx_fb_t s_fb;
static ui_series_t *s_series;

void setUp(void)
{
    gfx_fb_init(&s_fb, s_buf, 400, 300);
    s_series = calloc(1, sizeof(ui_series_t));
}

void tearDown(void)
{
    free(s_series);
}

static ui_value_t value(const char *label, const char *text, const char *unit, float num)
{
    ui_value_t v;
    memset(&v, 0, sizeof(v));
    v.kind = UI_FK_NUMBER;
    v.state = UI_VALUE_FRESH;
    v.label = label;
    snprintf(v.text, sizeof(v.text), "%s", text);
    snprintf(v.unit, sizeof(v.unit), "%s", unit);
    v.has_num = true;
    v.num = num;
    return v;
}

static void test_each_visual_needs_its_room(void)
{
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_CHART, 80, 40));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_CHART, 79, 40));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_CHART, 80, 39));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_GAUGE, 60, 40));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_GAUGE, 59, 40));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_RING, 40, 40));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_RING, 40, 39));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_BAR, 60, 16));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_BAR, 60, 15));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_VALUE, 1, 1));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_COUNT, 400, 300));
}

static void test_a_charts_span_and_its_words(void)
{
    static const struct {
        uint32_t s;
        bool ok;
        const char *text;
    } k[] = {
        { 9, false, "9 s" },        { 10, true, "10 s" },    { 60, true, "60 s" },     { 90, true, "90 s" },
        { 120, true, "2 min" },     { 300, true, "5 min" },  { 150, true, "150 s" },   { 3600, true, "1 h" },
        { 5400, false, "90 min" },   { 3601, false, "3601 s" }, { 7200, true, "2 h" }, { 7300, false, "7300 s" },
        { 86400, true, "24 h" },    { 90000, false, "25 h" },
    };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(k) / sizeof(k[0]); i++) {
        char text[16];
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MESSAGE(k[i].ok, ui_visual_span_ok(k[i].s), k[i].text);
        ui_visual_span_text(k[i].s, text, sizeof(text));
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING(k[i].text, text);
    }
}

static void test_the_range_takes_its_own_bounds_then_the_fields_then_automatic(void)
{
    ui_value_t v = value("CPU", "61", "%", 61.0f);
    v.has_range = true;
    v.range_min = 0.0f;
    v.range_max = 100.0f;
    ui_visual_t bar = { .as = UI_VIS_BAR };
    float lo, hi;
    ui_visual_range(&bar, &v, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, lo);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(100.0f, hi);
    bar.max_mode = UI_LIMIT_VALUE;
    bar.max = 80.0f;
    ui_visual_range(&bar, &v, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(80.0f, hi);
    /* a chart's automatic bounds: the values in view, a tenth's room, rounded */
    ui_visual_t chart = { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .min_mode = UI_LIMIT_AUTO, .max_mode = UI_LIMIT_AUTO };
    ui_visual_range(&chart, &v, true, 52.0f, 72.0f, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(50.0f, lo);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(75.0f, hi);
    ui_visual_range(&chart, &v, true, 1.21f, 1.25f, &lo, &hi); /* a small range keeps its decimals */
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(lo <= 1.21f && lo >= 1.19f);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(hi >= 1.25f && hi <= 1.27f);
    chart.min_mode = UI_LIMIT_FIELD; /* the field's minimum, an automatic maximum */
    ui_visual_range(&chart, &v, true, 52.0f, 72.0f, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, lo);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(75.0f, hi);
    /* without the field's range: 0 to a round number past the value */
    ui_value_t t = value("Indoor", "21.3", "\xc2\xb0" "C", 21.3f);
    ui_visual_t gauge = { .as = UI_VIS_GAUGE };
    ui_visual_range(&gauge, &t, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, lo);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(50.0f, hi);
    t.num = -12.0f;
    ui_visual_range(&gauge, &t, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(-20.0f, lo);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(0.0f, hi);
    t.has_num = false; /* no value and no range: still a range to draw in */
    ui_visual_range(&gauge, &t, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(lo < hi);
    ui_visual_range(&chart, &t, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(lo < hi);
    /* bounds the wrong way round still leave a range */
    gauge.min_mode = gauge.max_mode = UI_LIMIT_VALUE;
    gauge.min = gauge.max = 5.0f;
    ui_visual_range(&gauge, &t, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(lo < hi);
}

/* Every pixel outside `r` still white. */
static void assert_inside(gfx_rect_t r, const char *what)
{
    for (int y = 0; y < 300; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 400; x++) {
            bool in = x >= r.x && x < r.x + r.w && y >= r.y && y < r.y + r.h;
            if (!in && gfx_get_pixel(&s_fb, x, y)) {
                char msg[96];
                snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg), "%s drew at %d,%d outside %dx%d", what, x, y, r.w, r.h);
                TEST_FAIL_MESSAGE(msg);
            }
        }
    }
}

static void test_no_visual_draws_outside_its_cell(void)
{
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 600; i++) {
        ui_series_push(s_series, i * 1000, (float)((i * 37) % 150) - 20.0f); /* past the range both ways */
    }
    static const int k_sizes[][2] = { { 400, 279 }, { 199, 99 }, { 132, 77 }, { 100, 69 }, { 80, 40 }, { 60, 40 },
                                      { 40, 40 },   { 60, 16 },  { 66, 279 }, { 400, 34 }, { 41, 20 } };
    ui_value_t big = value("Network download rate", "123456.7", "MB/s", 123456.7f);
    ui_value_t none = value("GPU", "", "%", 0);
    none.state = UI_VALUE_MISSING;
    none.has_num = false;
    const ui_value_t *values[] = { &big, &none };
    for (int as = UI_VIS_CHART; as < UI_VIS_COUNT; as++) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < sizeof(k_sizes) / sizeof(k_sizes[0]); k++) {
            int w = k_sizes[k][0], h = k_sizes[k][1];
            if (!ui_visual_fits((ui_vis_as_t)as, w, h)) {
                continue;
            }
            for (int n = 0; n < 2; n++) {
                for (int numbers = 0; numbers < 2; numbers++) {
                    gfx_fill_rect(&s_fb, (gfx_rect_t){ 0, 0, 400, 300 }, GFX_WHITE);
                    gfx_rect_t r = { (int16_t)((400 - w) / 2), (int16_t)((300 - h) / 2), (int16_t)w, (int16_t)h };
                    ui_visual_t vis = { .as = (uint8_t)as, .numbers = numbers, .span_s = 300,
                                        .min_mode = UI_LIMIT_VALUE, .max_mode = UI_LIMIT_VALUE, .min = 0, .max = 100 };
                    ui_visual_draw(&s_fb, r, values[n], &vis, s_series, 599 * 1000, lang_get("en"));
                    char what[48];
                    snprintf(what, sizeof(what), "visual %d at %dx%d", as, w, h);
                    assert_inside(r, what);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

static void test_a_chart_without_history_draws_its_frame(void)
{
    ui_value_t v = value("CPU", "61", "%", 61.0f);
    ui_visual_t chart = { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = 60, .numbers = true };
    gfx_rect_t r = { 10, 10, 199, 99 };
    ui_visual_draw(&s_fb, r, &v, &chart, NULL, 0, lang_get("en"));
    int black = 0;
    for (int y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
        for (int x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
            black += gfx_get_pixel(&s_fb, x, y);
        }
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(black > 100); /* the label, the value and the baseline */
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_each_visual_needs_its_room);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_charts_span_and_its_words);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_range_takes_its_own_bounds_then_the_fields_then_automatic);
    RUN_TEST(test_no_visual_draws_outside_its_cell);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_chart_without_history_draws_its_frame);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```

`components/ui/include/ui_visual.h`:

```c
#pragma once

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "gfx.h"
#include "lang.h"
#include "ui_fields.h"
#include "ui_history.h"

/*
 * Show as (spec §5.3, M7, D44): a numeric field drawn as its value, or as a history chart, a bar, a ring or an arc
 * gauge between a minimum and a maximum. The looks are the spike's, approved on 2026-10-19. Pure C, host-buildable.
 */

typedef enum {
    UI_VIS_VALUE, /* the field's own widget */
    UI_VIS_CHART,
    UI_VIS_BAR,
    UI_VIS_RING,
    UI_VIS_GAUGE,
    UI_VIS_COUNT,
} ui_vis_as_t;

typedef enum {
    UI_LIMIT_FIELD, /* the field's own range; automatic without one */
    UI_LIMIT_VALUE, /* the visual's own number */
    UI_LIMIT_AUTO,  /* a chart's: from the values in view */
} ui_vis_limit_t;

#define UI_SPAN_MIN_S 10
#define UI_SPAN_VALUES_MAX_S 3600 /* from each value the field takes */
#define UI_SPAN_HOURS_MIN_S 7200  /* from the minutes, in whole hours */
#define UI_SPAN_MAX_S 86400
#define UI_SPAN_DEFAULT_S 60

typedef struct {
    uint8_t as;                 /* ui_vis_as_t */
    uint8_t min_mode, max_mode; /* ui_vis_limit_t */
    bool numbers;               /* a chart's latest value over its top left, its range's ends and span in the corners */
    uint32_t span_s;            /* a chart's span (ui_visual_span_ok()) */
    float min, max;             /* for UI_LIMIT_VALUE, in the field's unit */
} ui_visual_t;

/* Whether `as` has room in w×h (spec §5.3): about 80×40 for a chart, 60×40 for a gauge, 40×40 for a ring and
 * 60×16 for a bar; the value always has. */
bool ui_visual_fits(ui_vis_as_t as, int w, int h);
/* A chart's span: 10-3 600 s, or whole hours from 2 to 24. */
bool ui_visual_span_ok(uint32_t span_s);
/* The span as the chart writes it: "60 s", "5 min", "1 h". */
void ui_visual_span_text(uint32_t span_s, char *out, size_t size);
/* The range `vis` draws `v` between (spec §5.3): each bound its own number, or the field's range, or automatic: a
 * chart's from the values in view, `view_lo`..`view_hi` (`have_view` false: none), with a tenth's room, rounded; a
 * bar's, a ring's or a gauge's from 0 to a round number past the value. */
void ui_visual_range(const ui_visual_t *vis, const ui_value_t *v, bool have_view, float view_lo, float view_hi,
                     float *lo, float *hi);
/* `v` as `vis` in `r`, nothing outside it; a chart draws `series` up to `now_ms` (NULL: no history yet). A value
 * missing draws the visual empty with "—". */
void ui_visual_draw(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, const ui_visual_t *vis, const ui_series_t *series,
                    uint32_t now_ms, const lang_t *lang);
```


`components/ui/ui_visual.c`:

```c
#include "ui_visual.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "gfx_fonts.h"
#include "ui_internal.h"

/* Show as (spec §5.3, D44): the four visuals as the spike drew them (approved 2026-10-19), from the resolved value
 * and the chart's history. */

#define CHUNK 50 /* a chart's columns taken at a time, on the stack */

static const gfx_font_t *const k_faces[] = { &gfx_font_num_cb_48, &gfx_font_sans_bold_28, &gfx_font_sans_bold_20,
                                             &gfx_font_sans_bold_16, &gfx_font_sans_12 };

bool ui_visual_fits(ui_vis_as_t as, int w, int h)
{
    switch (as) {
    case UI_VIS_VALUE:
        return true;
    case UI_VIS_CHART:
        return w >= 80 && h >= 40;
    case UI_VIS_GAUGE:
        return w >= 60 && h >= 40;
    case UI_VIS_RING:
        return w >= 40 && h >= 40;
    case UI_VIS_BAR:
        return w >= 60 && h >= 16;
    default:
        return false;
    }
}

bool ui_visual_span_ok(uint32_t span_s)
{
    return (span_s >= UI_SPAN_MIN_S && span_s <= UI_SPAN_VALUES_MAX_S) ||
           (span_s >= UI_SPAN_HOURS_MIN_S && span_s <= UI_SPAN_MAX_S && span_s % 3600 == 0);
}

void ui_visual_span_text(uint32_t span_s, char *out, size_t size)
{
    if (span_s >= 3600 && span_s % 3600 == 0) {
        snprintf(out, size, "%lu h", (unsigned long)(span_s / 3600));
    } else if (span_s >= 120 && span_s % 60 == 0) {
        snprintf(out, size, "%lu min", (unsigned long)(span_s / 60));
    } else {
        snprintf(out, size, "%lu s", (unsigned long)span_s);
    }
}

/* The round number (1, 2 or 5 times a power of ten) at or past `x` > 0. */
static float nice_up(float x)
{
    float p = powf(10.0f, floorf(log10f(x)));
    float m = x / p;
    return (m <= 1.0f ? 1.0f : m <= 2.0f ? 2.0f : m <= 5.0f ? 5.0f : 10.0f) * p;
}

/* A chart's automatic bounds: the values in view with a tenth's room, on a tenth of a round step. */
static void auto_view(float lo, float hi, float *out_lo, float *out_hi)
{
    float pad = hi > lo ? (hi - lo) * 0.1f : fabsf(hi) > 0.0f ? fabsf(hi) * 0.1f : 1.0f;
    lo -= pad;
    hi += pad;
    float step = nice_up(hi - lo) / 10.0f;
    *out_lo = floorf(lo / step) * step;
    *out_hi = ceilf(hi / step) * step;
}

void ui_visual_range(const ui_visual_t *vis, const ui_value_t *v, bool have_view, float view_lo, float view_hi,
                     float *lo, float *hi)
{
    float alo = 0.0f, ahi = 1.0f; /* automatic */
    if (vis->as == UI_VIS_CHART) {
        if (have_view) {
            auto_view(view_lo, view_hi, &alo, &ahi);
        } else if (v->has_num) {
            auto_view(v->num, v->num, &alo, &ahi);
        }
    } else if (v->has_num && v->num != 0.0f) {
        alo = v->num < 0.0f ? -nice_up(-v->num) : 0.0f;
        ahi = v->num > 0.0f ? nice_up(v->num) : 0.0f;
    }
    *lo = vis->min_mode == UI_LIMIT_VALUE                    ? vis->min
          : vis->min_mode == UI_LIMIT_FIELD && v->has_range ? v->range_min
                                                            : alo;
    *hi = vis->max_mode == UI_LIMIT_VALUE                    ? vis->max
          : vis->max_mode == UI_LIMIT_FIELD && v->has_range ? v->range_max
                                                            : ahi;
    if (!(*hi > *lo)) {
        *hi = *lo + 1.0f;
    }
}

/* ---- text ---- */

static int ascent(const gfx_font_t *f)
{
    return f->ascent;
}

static bool face_has(const gfx_font_t *f, const char *s)
{
    for (const char *p = s; *p;) {
        uint32_t c = gfx_utf8_next(&p);
        if (c != ' ' && !gfx_font_has_glyph(f, c)) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

/* Text with a 1 px white halo, so it reads over a chart. Returns where it ends. */
static int text_halo(gfx_fb_t *fb, const gfx_font_t *f, int x, int base, const char *s, bool halo)
{
    if (halo) {
        for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
            for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
                if (dx != 0 || dy != 0) {
                    gfx_text(fb, f, x + dx, base + dy, s, GFX_WHITE);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return gfx_text(fb, f, x, base, s, GFX_BLACK);
}

/* What a visual writes for its value: the text, its shorter form, and the unit; "—" without a value. */
typedef struct {
    const char *text, *short_text, *unit;
} number_t;

static number_t number_of(const ui_value_t *v)
{
    if (v->state == UI_VALUE_MISSING || !v->text[0]) {
        return (number_t){ "—", "—", "" };
    }
    return (number_t){ v->text, v->short_text[0] ? v->short_text : v->text, v->unit };
}

/* The value and its unit, as large as fits w×h (the unit never below bold 16), then without the unit, then without
 * its decimals, as the spike drew them; the width it takes, drawn when `draw`. 0: nothing fits. */
static int number_fit(gfx_fb_t *fb, int x, int y, int w, int h, const number_t *n, bool draw, gfx_align_t align,
                      bool halo)
{
    for (int pass = 0; pass < 3; pass++) {
        const char *num = pass < 2 ? n->text : n->short_text;
        const char *u = pass == 0 ? n->unit : "";
        if (pass == 2 && strcmp(n->text, n->short_text) == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(k_faces) / sizeof(k_faces[0]); i++) {
            const gfx_font_t *f = k_faces[i];
            if (!face_has(f, num) || ascent(f) > h || (u[0] && f == &gfx_font_sans_12)) {
                continue;
            }
            const gfx_font_t *uf = ascent(f) >= 28 ? &gfx_font_sans_16 : &gfx_font_sans_12;
            int nw = gfx_text_width(f, num), uw = u[0] ? gfx_text_width(uf, u) + 3 : 0;
            if (nw + uw > w) {
                continue;
            }
            if (draw) {
                int x0 = align == GFX_ALIGN_LEFT    ? x
                         : align == GFX_ALIGN_RIGHT ? x + w - nw - uw
                                                    : x + (w - nw - uw) / 2;
                int base = y + (h + ascent(f)) / 2;
                int end = text_halo(fb, f, x0, base, num, halo);
                if (u[0]) {
                    text_halo(fb, uf, end + 3, base, u, halo);
                }
            }
            return nw + uw;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

/* The label (the field's, or the slot's own title) at the top left, cut to `max_w`. */
static void label(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, const gfx_font_t *f, int max_w)
{
    const char *text = v->label;
    char fit[64];
    if (text == NULL || !text[0] || max_w <= 0 || gfx_text_ellipsize(f, text, max_w, fit, sizeof(fit)) <= 0) {
        return;
    }
    ui_label(v);
    gfx_text(fb, f, r.x + 4, r.y + 2 + ascent(f), fit, GFX_BLACK);
}

/* A bound as the visuals write it: whole, else with the decimals it needs (up to two). */
static void bound_text(const lang_t *lang, float x, char *out, size_t size)
{
    int decimals = fabsf(x - roundf(x)) < 1e-3f ? 0 : fabsf(x * 10.0f - roundf(x * 10.0f)) < 1e-2f ? 1 : 2;
    float scale = decimals == 0 ? 1.0f : decimals == 1 ? 10.0f : 100.0f;
    float scaled = x * scale;
    if (fabsf(scaled) < 2e9f) {
        lang_format_decimal(lang, lroundf(scaled), decimals, out, size);
    } else {
        snprintf(out, size, "%.0f", (double)x);
    }
}

static float frac(const ui_value_t *v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if (!v->has_num || v->state == UI_VALUE_MISSING) {
        return 0.0f;
    }
    float f = (v->num - lo) / (hi - lo);
    return f < 0.0f ? 0.0f : f > 1.0f ? 1.0f : f;
}

/* ---- the chart ---- */

static int y_of(gfx_rect_t p, float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    int y = p.y + p.h - 1 - (int)lroundf((v - lo) / (hi - lo) * (float)(p.h - 1));
    return y > p.y + p.h - 1 ? p.y + p.h - 1 : y < p.y ? p.y : y;
}

/* One column of the line at `y`, with the light fill under it, joined to the column before (`line_y`, -1: none). */
static void plot(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t p, int x, int y, int *line_y)
{
    for (int fy = y + 1; fy < p.y + p.h; fy++) {
        if (x % 2 == 0 && fy % 2 == 0) {
            gfx_pixel(fb, x, fy, GFX_BLACK);
        }
    }
    int a = *line_y >= 0 && *line_y < y ? *line_y : y, b = *line_y > y ? *line_y : y;
    gfx_vline(fb, x, a, b - a + 1, GFX_BLACK);
    *line_y = y;
}

static void chart(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, const ui_visual_t *vis, const ui_series_t *series,
                  uint32_t now_ms, const lang_t *lang)
{
    const gfx_font_t *lf = r.h >= 80 ? &gfx_font_sans_16 : &gfx_font_sans_12;
    const gfx_font_t *sf = &gfx_font_sans_12;
    number_t n = number_of(v);
    int top = r.y + 2 + ascent(lf) + 6; /* the label's line stays clear of the chart */
    gfx_rect_t p = { (int16_t)(r.x + 4), (int16_t)top, (int16_t)(r.w - 8), (int16_t)(r.y + r.h - 3 - top) };
    uint32_t span_ms = (ui_visual_span_ok(vis->span_s) ? vis->span_s : UI_SPAN_DEFAULT_S) * 1000u;
    ui_hist_column_t cols[CHUNK];
    bool have = false;
    float vlo = 0.0f, vhi = 0.0f;
    for (int first = 0; series != NULL && first < p.w; first += CHUNK) { /* the values in view, for an auto range */
        int count = p.w - first < CHUNK ? p.w - first : CHUNK;
        ui_series_columns(series, now_ms, span_ms, p.w, first, count, cols);
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            if (cols[i].n > 0) {
                vlo = !have || cols[i].min < vlo ? cols[i].min : vlo;
                vhi = !have || cols[i].max > vhi ? cols[i].max : vhi;
                have = true;
            }
        }
    }
    float lo, hi;
    ui_visual_range(vis, v, have, vlo, vhi, &lo, &hi);
    char lo_s[16], hi_s[16];
    bound_text(lang, lo, lo_s, sizeof(lo_s));
    bound_text(lang, hi, hi_s, sizeof(hi_s));
    int right = vis->numbers ? gfx_text_width(sf, hi_s) + 8 /* the range's top */
                             : number_fit(fb, 0, 0, r.w / 2, ascent(lf) + 4, &n, false, GFX_ALIGN_RIGHT, false) + 8;
    label(fb, r, v, lf, r.w - 8 - right);
    if (vis->numbers) {
        gfx_text(fb, sf, r.x + r.w - 4 - gfx_text_width(sf, hi_s), r.y + 2 + ascent(lf), hi_s, GFX_BLACK);
    } else { /* the value on the label's line, at the right */
        int hw = right - 8;
        number_fit(fb, r.x + r.w - 4 - hw, r.y + 1, hw, ascent(lf) + 4, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_RIGHT, false);
    }
    int prev_x = -1, prev_y = 0, line_y = -1;
    for (int first = 0; series != NULL && first < p.w; first += CHUNK) {
        int count = p.w - first < CHUNK ? p.w - first : CHUNK;
        ui_series_columns(series, now_ms, span_ms, p.w, first, count, cols);
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const ui_hist_column_t *c = &cols[i];
            if (c->n == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            int px = p.x + first + i;
            int ym = y_of(p, c->mean, lo, hi), ylo = y_of(p, c->min, lo, hi), yhi = y_of(p, c->max, lo, hi);
            if (c->brk) {
                line_y = -1; /* the line starts again */
            } else if (prev_x >= 0) {
                for (int x = prev_x + 1; x < px; x++) { /* across the columns without a value, straight */
                    plot(fb, p, x, prev_y + (ym - prev_y) * (x - prev_x) / (px - prev_x), &line_y);
                }
            }
            for (int y = yhi; y < ym; y++) { /* the column's range above the mean, half grey */
                if ((px + y) % 2 == 0) {
                    gfx_pixel(fb, px, y, GFX_BLACK);
                }
            }
            for (int y = ym + 1; y <= ylo; y++) { /* and below it */
                if ((px + y) % 2 == 0) {
                    gfx_pixel(fb, px, y, GFX_BLACK);
                }
            }
            plot(fb, p, px, ym, &line_y);
            prev_x = px;
            prev_y = ym;
        }
    }
    gfx_hline(fb, p.x, p.y + p.h, p.w, GFX_BLACK); /* the baseline */
    if (vis->numbers) { /* the latest value over the chart's top left, the range's bottom at the right */
        int nh = p.h >= 130 ? 52 : p.h >= 60 ? 30 : p.h >= 40 ? 22 : 18;
        number_fit(fb, p.x + 1, p.y + 1, p.w * 2 / 3, nh, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_LEFT, true);
        text_halo(fb, sf, p.x + p.w - gfx_text_width(sf, lo_s) - 1, p.y + p.h - 2, lo_s, true);
    }
    if (p.h >= 40) { /* the span, small, at the bottom left */
        char span[16];
        ui_visual_span_text(span_ms / 1000u, span, sizeof(span));
        text_halo(fb, sf, p.x + 1, p.y + p.h - 2, span, true);
    }
}

/* ---- the bar ---- */

static void bar(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, float lo, float hi)
{
    number_t n = number_of(v);
    float f = frac(v, lo, hi);
    if (r.h > r.w * 3 / 2) { /* upright in a tall cell: the value under it */
        label(fb, r, v, &gfx_font_sans_12, r.w - 8);
        int nh = 22, top = r.y + 4 + ascent(&gfx_font_sans_12) + 2;
        gfx_rect_t b = { (int16_t)(r.x + r.w / 2 - 10), (int16_t)top, 20, (int16_t)(r.y + r.h - nh - 4 - top) };
        gfx_rect(fb, b, GFX_BLACK);
        int fill = (int)lroundf(f * (float)(b.h - 2));
        gfx_fill_rect(fb, (gfx_rect_t){ (int16_t)(b.x + 1), (int16_t)(b.y + b.h - 1 - fill), (int16_t)(b.w - 2),
                                        (int16_t)fill },
                      GFX_BLACK);
        number_fit(fb, r.x + 2, r.y + r.h - nh - 2, r.w - 4, nh, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER, false);
        return;
    }
    gfx_rect_t b;
    if (r.h < 44) { /* one line: the bar, then the value */
        int nw = number_fit(fb, 0, 0, r.w / 2, r.h - 4, &n, false, GFX_ALIGN_LEFT, false);
        int bh = r.h - 8 > 12 ? 12 : r.h - 8;
        b = (gfx_rect_t){ (int16_t)(r.x + 4), (int16_t)(r.y + (r.h - bh) / 2), (int16_t)(r.w - 12 - nw), (int16_t)bh };
        number_fit(fb, b.x + b.w + 4, r.y + 2, nw, r.h - 4, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_LEFT, false);
    } else { /* the label and the value on a line, the bar under them */
        const gfx_font_t *lf = r.h >= 70 ? &gfx_font_sans_16 : &gfx_font_sans_12;
        int hh = r.h >= 70 ? 32 : 22;
        int lw = v->label != NULL ? gfx_text_width(lf, v->label) : 0; /* the label first, the value in the rest */
        lw = lw > (r.w - 8) * 3 / 5 ? (r.w - 8) * 3 / 5 : lw;
        label(fb, (gfx_rect_t){ r.x, (int16_t)(r.y + hh - ascent(lf) - 4), r.w, r.h }, v, lf, lw);
        lw += lw > 0 ? 12 : 0;
        number_fit(fb, r.x + lw, r.y + 2, r.w - 4 - lw, hh, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_RIGHT, false);
        int bh = r.y + r.h - 6 - (r.y + hh + 4);
        bh = bh > 18 ? 18 : bh;
        b = (gfx_rect_t){ (int16_t)(r.x + 4), (int16_t)(r.y + hh + 4 + (r.y + r.h - 6 - (r.y + hh + 4) - bh) / 2),
                          (int16_t)(r.w - 8), (int16_t)bh };
    }
    gfx_rect(fb, b, GFX_BLACK);
    int fill = (int)lroundf(f * (float)(b.w - 2));
    gfx_fill_rect(fb, (gfx_rect_t){ (int16_t)(b.x + 1), (int16_t)(b.y + 1), (int16_t)fill, (int16_t)(b.h - 2) },
                  GFX_BLACK);
}

/* ---- the ring and the gauge ---- */

/* Angles: 0 at 12 o'clock, clockwise, in degrees; a0 below -180 for a full ring. */
static void arc_band(gfx_fb_t *fb, int cx, int cy, float ro, float ri, float a0, float a1, float filled_to)
{
    for (int y = (int)((float)cy - ro - 1.0f); y <= (int)((float)cy + ro + 1.0f); y++) {
        for (int x = (int)((float)cx - ro - 1.0f); x <= (int)((float)cx + ro + 1.0f); x++) {
            float dx = (float)x + 0.5f - (float)cx, dy = (float)y + 0.5f - (float)cy;
            float d = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
            if (d > ro || d < ri) {
                continue;
            }
            float a = atan2f(dx, -dy) * 57.29578f; /* -180..180 */
            if (a0 >= -180.0f) {
                if (a < a0 || a > a1) {
                    continue;
                }
            } else if (a < 0.0f) {
                a += 360.0f;
            }
            bool edge = d > ro - 1.0f || d < ri + 1.0f;
            if (a <= filled_to || edge) {
                gfx_pixel(fb, x, y, GFX_BLACK);
            }
        }
    }
}

static void ring(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, float lo, float hi)
{
    number_t n = number_of(v);
    float f = frac(v, lo, hi);
    const gfx_font_t *lf = r.h >= 80 ? &gfx_font_sans_16 : &gfx_font_sans_12;
    int lh = ascent(lf) + 4;
    label(fb, r, v, lf, r.w - 8);
    int ah = r.h - lh - 4, aw = r.w - 8, ay = r.y + lh + 2;
    int d = ah < aw ? ah : aw;
    float ro = (float)d / 2.0f, t = ro / 5.0f < 4.0f ? 4.0f : ro / 5.0f;
    int inner = (int)((ro - t) * 1.35f);
    if (number_fit(fb, 0, 0, inner, inner * 2 / 3, &n, false, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER, false) > 0) {
        int cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = ay + ah / 2;
        arc_band(fb, cx, cy, ro, ro - t, -999.0f, 999.0f, f * 360.0f);
        number_fit(fb, cx - inner / 2, cy - inner / 3, inner, inner * 2 / 3, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER, false);
        return;
    }
    /* beside: a smaller ring at the left (a pie under 30 px), the value right of it */
    d = ah < aw / 2 ? ah : aw / 2;
    ro = (float)d / 2.0f;
    int cx = r.x + 4 + d / 2, cy = ay + ah / 2;
    if (d < 30) {
        gfx_circle(fb, cx, cy, d / 2, GFX_BLACK);
        arc_band(fb, cx, cy, ro, 0.0f, -999.0f, 999.0f, f * 360.0f);
    } else {
        t = ro / 4.0f < 4.0f ? 4.0f : ro / 4.0f;
        arc_band(fb, cx, cy, ro, ro - t, -999.0f, 999.0f, f * 360.0f);
    }
    number_fit(fb, cx + d / 2 + 4, cy - 15, r.x + r.w - (cx + d / 2 + 4) - 2, 30, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_LEFT, false);
}

static void gauge(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, float lo, float hi, const lang_t *lang)
{
    number_t n = number_of(v);
    float f = frac(v, lo, hi);
    const gfx_font_t *lf = r.h >= 80 ? &gfx_font_sans_16 : &gfx_font_sans_12;
    const gfx_font_t *sf = &gfx_font_sans_12;
    int lh = ascent(lf) + 4;
    label(fb, r, v, lf, r.w - 8);
    bool ends = r.h >= 70;
    int ah = r.h - lh - 4 - (ends ? 4 : 0), aw = r.w - 8;
    float ro = (float)aw / 2.0f;
    ro = ro * 1.5f > (float)ah ? (float)ah / 1.5f : ro;
    float t = ro / 5.0f < 4.0f ? 4.0f : ro / 5.0f;
    int spare = ah - (int)(ro * 1.5f); /* a tall cell: the arc in its middle */
    int cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + lh + 2 + (spare > 0 ? spare / 2 : 0) + (int)ro + 1;
    arc_band(fb, cx, cy, ro, ro - t, -120.0f, 120.0f, -120.0f + f * 240.0f);
    int iw = (int)((ro - t) * 1.6f), ih = (int)(ro * 0.85f);
    if (number_fit(fb, cx - iw / 2, cy - ih / 2, iw, ih, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER, false) == 0) {
        number_fit(fb, cx - (int)ro, cy + (int)(ro * 0.5f) - 18, (int)(2.0f * ro), 18, &n, true, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER,
                   false);
    }
    if (ends) { /* the range's ends under the arc's ends */
        char lo_s[16], hi_s[16];
        bound_text(lang, lo, lo_s, sizeof(lo_s));
        bound_text(lang, hi, hi_s, sizeof(hi_s));
        int ey = cy + (int)(ro * 0.5f) + ascent(sf) + 1;
        ey = ey > r.y + r.h - 2 ? r.y + r.h - 2 : ey;
        int lw = gfx_text_width(sf, lo_s), hw = gfx_text_width(sf, hi_s);
        int lx = cx - (int)(ro * 0.87f) - lw / 2, hx = cx + (int)(ro * 0.87f) - hw / 2;
        lx = lx < r.x + 1 ? r.x + 1 : lx; /* inside the cell, beside the arc's ends */
        hx = hx + hw > r.x + r.w - 1 ? r.x + r.w - 1 - hw : hx;
        gfx_text(fb, sf, lx, ey, lo_s, GFX_BLACK);
        gfx_text(fb, sf, hx, ey, hi_s, GFX_BLACK);
    }
}

void ui_visual_draw(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_value_t *v, const ui_visual_t *vis, const ui_series_t *series,
                    uint32_t now_ms, const lang_t *lang)
{
    if (!ui_visual_fits((ui_vis_as_t)vis->as, r.w, r.h) || vis->as == UI_VIS_VALUE) {
        return;
    }
    gfx_set_clip(fb, r);
    float lo, hi;
    ui_visual_range(vis, v, false, 0, 0, &lo, &hi);
    switch (vis->as) {
    case UI_VIS_CHART:
        chart(fb, r, v, vis, series, now_ms, lang);
        break;
    case UI_VIS_BAR:
        bar(fb, r, v, lo, hi);
        break;
    case UI_VIS_RING:
        ring(fb, r, v, lo, hi);
        break;
    case UI_VIS_GAUGE:
        gauge(fb, r, v, lo, hi, lang);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    gfx_reset_clip(fb);
}
```
