`test/host/visual_fixtures.h`:

```c
#pragma once

#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "dashboard_fixtures.h"
#include "ui_history.h"
#include "ui_split.h"
#include "ui_visual.h"

/* The visuals' golden sheets (spec §5.3, M7, D44; test_ui_visual_golden.c, render_visual.c): the spike's renders
 * that the owner approved, drawn by the real visuals into empty split cells, with histories made up the same way. */

#define VIS_NOW_MS 89999000u /* 25 h of the monotonic clock, at 1 s */
#define VIS_NONE NAN

enum { VIS_S_CPU, VIS_S_TEMP, VIS_S_GPU, VIS_S_GTEMP, VIS_S_ROOM, VIS_S_NET, VIS_S_GAP, VIS_S_SILENCE, VIS_S_COUNT };

static uint32_t s_vis_seed;
static inline float vis_rnd(void)
{
    s_vis_seed = s_vis_seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
    return (float)((s_vis_seed >> 8) & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0f;
}

/* The histories, made once: a day of CPU load at 1 s, the last minute of the others, the indoor temperature every
 * 5 min, a minute with a reconnect, and two hours with a silence. */
static inline const ui_series_t *vis_series(int which)
{
    static ui_series_t *s_all;
    if (s_all == NULL) {
        s_all = calloc(VIS_S_COUNT, sizeof(ui_series_t));
        s_vis_seed = 12345;
        for (uint32_t t = 0; t <= VIS_NOW_MS / 1000; t++) { /* CPU load */
            uint32_t a = VIS_NOW_MS / 1000 - t, m = (t / 60) % 1440;
            float v;
            if (a < 60) {
                float i = (float)(59 - a);
                v = 18.0f + 10.0f * sinf(i / 6.0f) + 8.0f * vis_rnd() + (i > 40.0f ? 35.0f : 0.0f);
            } else if (a < 3600) {
                v = 8.0f + 6.0f * vis_rnd();
                if ((a > 550 && a < 1000) || (a > 2300 && a < 2700)) {
                    v = 70.0f + 25.0f * vis_rnd();
                }
                v = vis_rnd() > 0.97f ? 55.0f + 40.0f * vis_rnd() : v;
            } else {
                bool day = m > 8 * 60 && m < 19 * 60;
                v = day ? 25.0f + 30.0f * vis_rnd() + (vis_rnd() > 0.9f ? 40.0f : 0.0f) : 4.0f + 3.0f * vis_rnd();
            }
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_CPU], t * 1000, v);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            uint32_t t = VIS_NOW_MS - (uint32_t)(59 - i) * 1000;
            float f = (float)i;
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_TEMP], t, 52.0f + 6.0f * sinf(f / 9.0f) + 2.0f * vis_rnd() + (i > 40 ? 14.0f : 0));
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_GPU], t, i < 20 ? 3.0f + 2.0f * vis_rnd() : 88.0f + 10.0f * vis_rnd());
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_GTEMP], t, i < 20 ? 41.0f + vis_rnd() : 41.0f + (f - 20.0f) * 0.6f + vis_rnd());
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_NET], t, 6.0f + 8.0f * vis_rnd() + (i > 30 && i < 45 ? 30.0f : 0.0f));
            if (i == 26) {
                ui_series_break(&s_all[VIS_S_GAP], t); /* the session reconnected after ten silent seconds */
            }
            if (i <= 25 || i >= 36) {
                ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_GAP], t, 40.0f + 15.0f * sinf(f / 5.0f) + 4.0f * vis_rnd());
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) { /* two hours of the indoor temperature */
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_ROOM], VIS_NOW_MS - (uint32_t)(24 - i) * 300000,
                           22.1f + 0.08f * (float)i + 0.2f * vis_rnd());
        }
        bool quiet = false;
        for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 2 * 3600 * 4; k++) { /* two hours at 250 ms, quiet for 20 min in the middle */
            uint32_t t = VIS_NOW_MS - 2 * 3600 * 1000 + k * 250;
            uint32_t a = (VIS_NOW_MS - t) / 60000;
            if (a >= 50 && a < 70) {
                quiet = true;
                continue;
            }
            if (quiet) { /* the next session's first message */
                ui_series_break(&s_all[VIS_S_SILENCE], t);
                quiet = false;
            }
            ui_series_push(&s_all[VIS_S_SILENCE], t, 30.0f + 20.0f * sinf((float)k / 2000.0f) + 10.0f * vis_rnd());
        }
    }
    return which >= 0 && which < VIS_S_COUNT ? &s_all[which] : NULL;
}

/* One cell: how it shows, what it shows. */
typedef struct {
    ui_visual_t vis;
    const char *label, *unit;
    int decimals;
    float value; /* VIS_NONE: no value */
    float lo, hi; /* the field's own range; lo == hi: none */
    int series;   /* VIS_S_*, or -1 */
} vis_cell_t;

#define CHART(span, over) { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = (span), .numbers = (over) }
#define CHART_AUTO(span, over)                                                                                   \
    { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = (span), .numbers = (over), .min_mode = UI_LIMIT_AUTO, .max_mode = UI_LIMIT_AUTO }
#define SHOW(a) { .as = (a) }

/* The value a cell shows, resolved as a pc.* field would be, in `lang`. */
static inline ui_value_t vis_value(const vis_cell_t *c, const lang_t *lang)
{
    ui_value_t v;
    memset(&v, 0, sizeof(v));
    v.kind = UI_FK_NUMBER;
    v.label = c->label;
    snprintf(v.unit, sizeof(v.unit), "%s", c->unit);
    if (c->lo < c->hi) {
        v.has_range = true;
        v.range_min = c->lo;
        v.range_max = c->hi;
    }
    if (!isnan(c->value)) {
        v.state = UI_VALUE_FRESH;
        v.has_num = true;
        v.num = c->value;
        long scaled = lroundf(c->value * powf(10.0f, (float)c->decimals));
        lang_format_decimal(lang, scaled, c->decimals, v.text, sizeof(v.text));
        if (c->decimals > 0) {
            lang_format_decimal(lang, lroundf(c->value), 0, v.short_text, sizeof(v.short_text));
        }
    }
    return v;
}

/* An empty split dashboard of rows × cols (the status bar, the lines) and its cells. */
static inline void vis_base(gfx_fb_t *fb, int rows, int cols, const lang_t *lang, ui_split_geometry_t *g)
{
    ui_context_t ctx;
    ui_preset_t p;
    fixture_dashboard("home", &ctx, &p);
    ctx.lang = lang;
    uint8_t none[UI_SPLIT_CELLS] = { 0 };
    fixture_grid_split(&p, rows, cols, 0, none, (size_t)(rows * cols));
    ui_draw_dashboard(fb, &ctx, &p, NULL);
    ui_split_layout(p.split, ui_split_area(), g);
}

static inline void vis_sheet(gfx_fb_t *fb, int rows, int cols, const vis_cell_t *cells, int count, const char *lang_code)
{
    const lang_t *lang = lang_get(lang_code);
    ui_split_geometry_t g;
    vis_base(fb, rows, cols, lang, &g);
    for (int i = 0; i < count && i < g.cells; i++) {
        gfx_rect_t c = g.cell[i];
        gfx_rect_t r = { (int16_t)(c.x + 1), (int16_t)(c.y + 1), (int16_t)(c.w - 2), (int16_t)(c.h - 2) };
        ui_value_t v = vis_value(&cells[i], lang);
        ui_visual_draw(fb, r, &v, &cells[i].vis, vis_series(cells[i].series), VIS_NOW_MS, lang);
    }
}

#define N(a) ((int)(sizeof(a) / sizeof((a)[0])))

static inline bool fixture_visual(const char *name, gfx_fb_t *fb)
{
    if (strcmp(name, "visuals_m") == 0) { /* the four, 199×99 */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU load", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, 71, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "GB", 1, 21.3f, 0, 64, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 2, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_s") == 0) { /* 3×3, 132×92 */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { CHART_AUTO(60, false), "CPU", "°C", 0, 72, 0, 0, VIS_S_TEMP },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU", "°C", 0, 71, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "%", 0, 33, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "Fan", "rpm", 0, 1240, 0, 2000, -1 },
            { CHART(3600, true), "Indoor", "°C", 1, 23.8f, 0, 0, VIS_S_ROOM },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "Humidity", "%", 0, 45, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Battery", "%", 0, 87, 0, 100, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 3, 3, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_small") == 0) { /* 4×4, 100×69 */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { CHART(60, true), "GPU", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, VIS_S_GPU },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "CPU", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU", "°C", 0, 71, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "GPU", "°C", 0, 64, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "GB", 1, 21.3f, 0, 64, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "VRAM", "GB", 1, 6.2f, 0, 12, -1 },
            { CHART(3600, true), "Temp", "°C", 1, 23.8f, 0, 0, VIS_S_ROOM },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "Hum", "%", 0, 45, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Bat", "%", 0, 87, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "PV", "kW", 1, 3.4f, 0, 7, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Disk", "%", 0, 71, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "Fan", "%", 0, 38, 0, 100, -1 },
            { CHART_AUTO(60, true), "Net", "MB/s", 0, 12, 0, 0, VIS_S_NET },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "CPU", "%", 0, VIS_NONE, 0, 100, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 4, 4, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "chart_spans") == 0) { /* 10 s, 60 s, 1 h from the values, 24 h from the minutes */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(10, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { CHART(3600, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { CHART(86400, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 2, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "chart_variants") == 0) { /* numbers over it or not, automatic, no value */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, 72, 20, 100, VIS_S_TEMP },
            { CHART(60, false), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, 72, 20, 100, VIS_S_TEMP },
            { CHART_AUTO(60, true), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, 72, 20, 100, VIS_S_TEMP },
            { CHART(60, true), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, VIS_NONE, 20, 100, VIS_S_TEMP },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 2, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_missing") == 0) { /* no value, and a chart without history */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "GPU load", "%", 0, VIS_NONE, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "GB", 1, VIS_NONE, 0, 64, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU load", "%", 0, VIS_NONE, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, VIS_NONE, 20, 100, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 2, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_cs") == 0) { /* Czech: the comma */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(7200, true), "Uvnitř", "°C", 1, 23.8f, 0, 0, VIS_S_ROOM },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Paměť", "GB", 1, 21.3f, 0, 64, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "Vlhkost", "%", 0, 45, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "Výkon FVE", "kW", 1, 3.4f, 0, 7.5f, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 2, k, N(k), "cs");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "chart_gap") == 0) { /* a reconnect, and 20 min of silence in two hours */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, true), "GPU load", "%", 0, 47, 0, 100, VIS_S_GAP },
            { CHART(7200, true), "CPU load", "%", 0, 33, 0, 100, VIS_S_SILENCE },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 2, 1, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_tall") == 0) { /* six columns, 64×277: upright bars */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "GB", 1, 21.3f, 0, 64, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "VRAM", "GB", 1, 6.2f, 0, 12, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU", "°C", 0, 71, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Disk", "%", 0, VIS_NONE, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "Fan", "%", 0, 38, 0, 100, -1 },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 1, 6, k, N(k), "en");
    } else if (strcmp(name, "visuals_rows") == 0) { /* six rows, 398×44: wide and low */
        static const vis_cell_t k[] = {
            { CHART(60, false), "CPU load", "%", 0, 61, 0, 100, VIS_S_CPU },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_GAUGE), "CPU temperature", "°C", 0, 71, 20, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_RING), "GPU load", "%", 0, 94, 0, 100, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "RAM", "GB", 1, 21.3f, 0, 64, -1 },
            { SHOW(UI_VIS_BAR), "Network", "MB/s", 1, VIS_NONE, 0, 100, -1 },
            { CHART_AUTO(60, true), "Network", "MB/s", 0, 12, 0, 0, VIS_S_NET },
        };
        vis_sheet(fb, 6, 1, k, N(k), "en");
    } else {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

static const char *const k_visual_fixtures[] = { "visuals_m",       "visuals_s",   "visuals_small", "chart_spans",
                                                 "chart_variants",  "visuals_missing", "visuals_cs", "chart_gap",
                                                 "visuals_tall",    "visuals_rows" };
```

`test/host/test_ui_visual_golden.c`:

```c
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L /* setenv() in fixture_zone() */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "gfx.h"
#include "unity.h"
#include "visual_fixtures.h"

/* Each sheet must match test/host/golden/vis_<name>.pbm byte for byte (spec §5.3, D44). After an intentional
 * change: build-host/render_visual <name> test/host/golden/vis_<name>.pbm for each, look at the PNGs
 * (python3 tools/render.py), commit. */

static uint8_t s_buf[400 * 300 / 8];
static uint8_t s_pbm[16000];
static uint8_t s_golden[16000];

void setUp(void) {}
void tearDown(void) {}

static void check(const char *name)
{
    gfx_fb_t fb;
    gfx_fb_init(&fb, s_buf, 400, 300);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(fixture_visual(name, &fb), name);
    size_t n = gfx_pbm_encode(&fb, s_pbm, sizeof(s_pbm));
    char path[256];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/vis_%s.pbm", GOLDEN_DIR, name);
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL_MESSAGE(f, path);
    size_t golden = fread(s_golden, 1, sizeof(s_golden), f);
    fclose(f);
    if (golden != n || memcmp(s_golden, s_pbm, n) != 0) {
        char actual[64];
        snprintf(actual, sizeof(actual), "vis_%s.actual.pbm", name);
        FILE *out = fopen(actual, "wb");
        if (out != NULL) {
            fwrite(s_pbm, 1, n, out);
            fclose(out);
        }
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT_MESSAGE((int)n, (int)golden, path);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY_MESSAGE(s_golden, s_pbm, n, path);
}

static void test_every_sheet_matches_its_golden(void)
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(k_visual_fixtures) / sizeof(k_visual_fixtures[0]); i++) {
        check(k_visual_fixtures[i]);
    }
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_every_sheet_matches_its_golden);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```


`test/host/render_visual.c`:

```c
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L /* setenv() in fixture_zone() */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "gfx.h"
#include "visual_fixtures.h"

/* build-host/render_visual <sheet> <out.pbm>: one of the visuals' sheets as PBM (tools/render.py).
 * `--list` prints the sheets' names. */
int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    static uint8_t buf[400 * 300 / 8];
    static uint8_t pbm[16000];
    if (argc == 2 && strcmp(argv[1], "--list") == 0) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(k_visual_fixtures) / sizeof(k_visual_fixtures[0]); i++) {
            printf("%s\n", k_visual_fixtures[i]);
        }
        return 0;
    }
    gfx_fb_t fb;
    gfx_fb_init(&fb, buf, 400, 300);
    if (argc != 3 || !fixture_visual(argv[1], &fb)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "usage: render_visual <sheet> <out.pbm> | --list\n");
        return 2;
    }
    size_t n = gfx_pbm_encode(&fb, pbm, sizeof(pbm));
    FILE *f = fopen(argv[2], "wb");
    if (f == NULL || fwrite(pbm, 1, n, f) != n) {
        return 1;
    }
    fclose(f);
    return 0;
}
```

### Task 8: Visuals in presets and on the dashboard (`ui`)

**Files:**
- Create: `test/host/test_ui_preset_visuals.c`, `test/host/golden/dash_classic_visuals.pbm`, `test/host/golden/dash_split_visuals.pbm`
- Modify: `components/ui/include/ui_fields.h`, `components/ui/include/ui_history.h`, `components/ui/include/ui_preset.h`, `components/ui/ui_dashboard.c`, `components/ui/ui_fields.c`, `components/ui/ui_preset.c`, `components/ui/ui_preset_json.c`, `components/ui/ui_visual.c`, `test/host/CMakeLists.txt`, `test/host/dashboard_fixtures.h`, `test/host/test_ui_preset.c`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ui_visual_t`, `ui_visual_fits()`, `ui_visual_span_ok()`, `ui_visual_draw()` (Task 7); `ui_history_find()` (Task 6); the PC ids (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `UI_VISUALS_MAX` (48), `ui_visual_entry_t` {`preset`, `slot`, `vis`}; in `ui_presets_t` `visual_count` and `visuals[48]`, kept in preset and slot order;
  - `const ui_visual_t *ui_presets_visual(const ui_presets_t *, int preset, int slot)`, `bool ui_presets_set_visual(ui_presets_t *, int preset, int slot, const ui_visual_t *)`;
  - `ui_titles_t` gains `const ui_visual_t *visual[UI_SLOT_MAX]`, filled by `ui_presets_titles()`;
  - in `ui_context_t`: `const ui_history_t *history; uint32_t now_ms;`;
  - `size_t ui_field_name(const char (*pc_ids)[PC_ID_LEN], ui_field_id_t, char *, size_t)`, `bool ui_field_numeric(ui_field_id_t)`.

Spec §5.4's Visuals: a fixed preset's `"visuals": { "s1": { … } }` beside its titles, a split cell's `"show": { … }`. `as` is chart, bar, ring or gauge; `min` and `max` numbers in the field's base unit (a power's in kW), left out for the field's own range, `"auto"` for a chart's; `span_s` and `numbers` a chart's (numbers on unless turned off). Refused with the reason: a visual on a field that isn't a number (or an empty slot), an unknown `as`, `min` not below `max`, a span outside its ranges, `auto` on another visual, a bound that isn't a number, more than 48 in the file. The dashboard draws a slot's visual where its field is a number and the visual fits, its value otherwise; the stale policy applies as the widgets apply it. The visuals reach the renderer with the titles, so `ui_draw_dashboard()` keeps its signature.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** The file's visuals, their round trip, the refusals, the pool, the dashboard drawing a visual or the value, and a chart reading its field's history:

`test/host/test_ui_preset_visuals.c`:

```c
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L /* setenv() in fixture_zone() */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "dashboard_fixtures.h"
#include "gfx.h"
#include "ui_dashboard.h"
#include "ui_history.h"
#include "ui_preset.h"
#include "ui_visual.h"
#include "unity.h"

/* Show as in presets.json (spec §5.4, M7, D44): a fixed slot's `visuals`, a split cell's `show`, their shared pool of
 * 48, and the dashboard drawing them where they fit. */

static ui_presets_t s_p;
static char s_err[UI_PRESET_ERR_LEN];
static char s_json[UI_PRESETS_JSON_MAX];
static uint8_t s_buf[400 * 300 / 8], s_other[400 * 300 / 8];

void setUp(void)
{
    memset(&s_p, 0, sizeof(s_p));
    s_err[0] = '\0';
}

void tearDown(void) {}

static const char k_file[] =
    "{\"schema\":1,\"active\":\"grid\",\"presets\":["
    "{\"id\":\"grid\",\"name\":\"Grid\",\"layout\":\"grid\","
    "\"slots\":{\"g1\":\"env.temp\",\"g2\":\"env.hum\",\"g3\":\"bat.level\",\"g4\":\"pc.cpu_load\"},"
    "\"visuals\":{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"chart\",\"min\":\"auto\",\"max\":\"auto\",\"span_s\":7200,\"numbers\":false},"
    "\"g2\":{\"as\":\"ring\"},\"g3\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":10,\"max\":90.5},"
    "\"g4\":{\"as\":\"gauge\",\"max\":100}}},"
    "{\"id\":\"cells\",\"name\":\"Cells\",\"layout\":\"split\",\"split\":{\"split\":\"columns\",\"ratio\":\"1/2\","
    "\"a\":{\"field\":\"pc.cpu_load\",\"show\":{\"as\":\"chart\",\"span_s\":60}},\"b\":{\"field\":\"env.temp\"}}}]}";

static void test_the_file_names_each_slots_visual(void)
{
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(k_file, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(5, s_p.visual_count);
    const ui_visual_t *g1 = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(g1);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VIS_CHART, g1->as);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_AUTO, g1->min_mode);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_AUTO, g1->max_mode);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT32(7200, g1->span_s);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(g1->numbers);
    const ui_visual_t *g2 = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 1);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VIS_RING, g2->as);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_FIELD, g2->min_mode); /* left out: the field's own */
    const ui_visual_t *g3 = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 2);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_VALUE, g3->min_mode);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(10.0f, g3->min);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_FLOAT(90.5f, g3->max);
    const ui_visual_t *g4 = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 3);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_FIELD, g4->min_mode);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_VALUE, g4->max_mode);
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 4)); /* g5: its value */
    const ui_visual_t *a = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 1, 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VIS_CHART, a->as);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT32(60, a->span_s);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(a->numbers); /* a chart's numbers are on unless the file turns them off */
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 1, 1));
    ui_titles_t own;
    ui_presets_titles(&s_p, 0, &own);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_PTR(g1, own.visual[0]);
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(own.visual[4]);
}

static void test_the_visuals_are_written_back_as_they_came(void)
{
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(k_file, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_to_json(&s_p, s_json, sizeof(s_json)) > 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL_MESSAGE(
        strstr(s_json, "\"visuals\":{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"chart\",\"min\":\"auto\",\"max\":\"auto\",\"span_s\":7200,"
                       "\"numbers\":false},\"g2\":{\"as\":\"ring\"},\"g3\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":10,\"max\":90.5},"
                       "\"g4\":{\"as\":\"gauge\",\"max\":100}}"),
        s_json);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL_MESSAGE(strstr(s_json, "\"show\":{\"as\":\"chart\",\"span_s\":60,\"numbers\":true}"), s_json);
    ui_presets_t back;
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(s_json, &back, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(&s_p, &back, sizeof(back));
}

static void test_a_bound_keeps_its_digits(void)
{
    static const char k[] = "{\"schema\":1,\"presets\":[{\"id\":\"g\",\"layout\":\"grid\",\"slots\":{\"g1\":\"env.temp\"},"
                            "\"visuals\":{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":-0.1,\"max\":23.7}}}]}";
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(k, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    ui_presets_to_json(&s_p, s_json, sizeof(s_json));
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL_MESSAGE(strstr(s_json, "{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":-0.1,\"max\":23.7}"), s_json);
}

static void refused(const char *visual, const char *why)
{
    char file[512];
    snprintf(file, sizeof(file),
             "{\"schema\":1,\"presets\":[{\"id\":\"g\",\"layout\":\"grid\",\"slots\":{\"g1\":\"env.temp\","
             "\"g2\":\"date.day\"},\"visuals\":%s}]}",
             visual);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(file, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), visual);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING_MESSAGE(why, s_err, visual);
}

static void test_a_visual_beyond_the_rules_is_refused(void)
{
    refused("{\"g2\":{\"as\":\"bar\"}}", "preset \"g\": slot g2 shows no number");
    refused("{\"g3\":{\"as\":\"bar\"}}", "preset \"g\": slot g3 shows no number"); /* an empty slot */
    refused("{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"pie\"}}", "preset \"g\": a visual is chart, bar, ring or gauge");
    refused("{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":5,\"max\":5}}", "preset \"g\": a visual's min must be below its max");
    refused("{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"chart\",\"span_s\":5400}}",
            "preset \"g\": a chart's span is 10-3600 s, or whole hours up to 24 h");
    refused("{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"min\":\"auto\"}}", "preset \"g\": only a chart's min and max can be auto");
    refused("{\"g1\":{\"as\":\"bar\",\"max\":\"high\"}}", "preset \"g\": a visual's min and max are numbers");
    refused("{\"g9\":{\"as\":\"bar\"}}", "preset \"g\": layout grid has no slot \"g9\"");
    refused("[]", "preset \"g\": visuals must be an object of slot names");
    refused("{\"g1\":3}", "preset \"g\": a visual must be an object");
}

static void test_the_pool_holds_48_visuals(void)
{
    /* sixteen grid presets of six numbers: 96 slots, the 49th visual refused */
    char *const end = s_json + sizeof(s_json);
    char *p = s_json + snprintf(s_json, sizeof(s_json), "{\"schema\":1,\"presets\":[");
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_PRESET_MAX; i++) {
        p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "%s{\"id\":\"p%d\",\"layout\":\"grid\",\"slots\":{", i ? "," : "", i);
        for (int g = 1; g <= 6; g++) {
            p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "%s\"g%d\":\"env.temp\"", g > 1 ? "," : "", g);
        }
        p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "},\"visuals\":{");
        for (int g = 1; g <= 6; g++) {
            p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "%s\"g%d\":{\"as\":\"ring\"}", g > 1 ? "," : "", g);
        }
        p += snprintf(p, (size_t)(end - p), "}}");
    }
    strcpy(p, "]}");
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_from_json(s_json, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("preset \"p8\": 48 visuals at most in all presets", s_err);
    /* and in the API */
    memset(&s_p, 0, sizeof(s_p));
    ui_visual_t ring = { .as = UI_VIS_RING };
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_VISUALS_MAX; i++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, i / 6, i % 6, &ring));
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 15, 0, &ring));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 0, 0, &ring)); /* one already there is replaced */
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 0, 0, NULL));  /* and cleared */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_VISUALS_MAX - 1, s_p.visual_count);
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 0, 0));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 15, 0, &ring));
    for (int i = 1; i < s_p.visual_count; i++) { /* kept in order, so a file reads back the same */
        int a = s_p.visuals[i - 1].preset * UI_SLOT_MAX + s_p.visuals[i - 1].slot;
        int b = s_p.visuals[i].preset * UI_SLOT_MAX + s_p.visuals[i].slot;
        TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(a < b);
    }
    ui_visual_t value = { .as = UI_VIS_VALUE };
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 15, 0, &value)); /* "as the value" is none */
    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(ui_presets_visual(&s_p, 15, 0));
}

static void draw(gfx_fb_t *fb, uint8_t *buf, const ui_context_t *ctx, const ui_preset_t *p, const ui_titles_t *own)
{
    gfx_fb_init(fb, buf, 400, 300);
    ui_draw_dashboard(fb, ctx, p, own);
}

static void test_the_dashboard_draws_a_visual_where_it_fits_and_the_value_where_not(void)
{
    ui_context_t ctx = fixture_context();
    ui_preset_t p = fixture_preset("indoor"); /* Grid: g2 is env.hum */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_ENV_HUM, p.slots[1]);
    ui_visual_t ring = { .as = UI_VIS_RING };
    ui_titles_t own;
    memset(&own, 0, sizeof(own));
    gfx_fb_t a, b;
    draw(&a, s_buf, &ctx, &p, &own);
    own.visual[1] = &ring;
    draw(&b, s_other, &ctx, &p, &own);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(memcmp(s_buf, s_other, sizeof(s_buf)) != 0); /* the ring */
    /* the same visual drawn by hand in the slot's rectangle, the slot otherwise empty */
    ui_value_t v;
    ui_resolve(&ctx, UI_FIELD_ENV_HUM, &v);
    gfx_rect_t r = ui_layout(UI_LAYOUT_GRID)->slots[1].rect;
    ui_preset_t empty = p;
    empty.slots[1] = UI_FIELD_NONE;
    memset(&own, 0, sizeof(own));
    draw(&a, s_buf, &ctx, &empty, &own);
    ui_visual_draw(&a, r, &v, &ring, NULL, 0, ctx.lang);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(s_buf, s_other, sizeof(s_buf));
    /* a cell too small for the visual, or a field that isn't a number: the value as before */
    ui_visual_t chart = { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = 60 };
    uint8_t cells[24] = { UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP, UI_FIELD_ENV_HUM };
    fixture_grid_split(&p, 4, 6, 0, cells, 2); /* 66×69 cells: no room for a chart */
    memset(&own, 0, sizeof(own));
    draw(&a, s_buf, &ctx, &p, &own);
    own.visual[0] = &chart;
    draw(&b, s_other, &ctx, &p, &own);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(s_buf, s_other, sizeof(s_buf));
    p = fixture_preset("home"); /* Classic: main is the clock */
    memset(&own, 0, sizeof(own));
    draw(&a, s_buf, &ctx, &p, &own);
    own.visual[0] = &ring;
    draw(&b, s_other, &ctx, &p, &own);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(s_buf, s_other, sizeof(s_buf));
}

static void test_a_chart_reads_its_fields_history(void)
{
    ui_series_t *mem = calloc(UI_HIST_SERIES, sizeof(ui_series_t));
    ui_history_t h;
    ui_history_init(&h, mem);
    const char *const names[] = { "env.temp" };
    ui_history_track(&h, names, 1);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        ui_series_push(ui_history_find(&h, "env.temp"), 1000 + i * 1000, 20.0f + (float)(i % 7));
    }
    ui_context_t ctx = fixture_context();
    ui_preset_t p = fixture_preset("indoor"); /* g1: env.temp */
    ui_visual_t chart = { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = 60, .numbers = true };
    ui_titles_t own;
    memset(&own, 0, sizeof(own));
    own.visual[0] = &chart;
    gfx_fb_t a, b;
    draw(&a, s_buf, &ctx, &p, &own); /* no history in the context: an empty chart */
    ctx.history = &h;
    ctx.now_ms = 60 * 1000;
    draw(&b, s_other, &ctx, &p, &own);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(memcmp(s_buf, s_other, sizeof(s_buf)) != 0);
    free(mem);
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_the_file_names_each_slots_visual);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_visuals_are_written_back_as_they_came);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_bound_keeps_its_digits);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_visual_beyond_the_rules_is_refused);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_pool_holds_48_visuals);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_dashboard_draws_a_visual_where_it_fits_and_the_value_where_not);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_chart_reads_its_fields_history);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```

`components/ui/ui_preset_json.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/ui/ui_preset_json.c
+++ b/components/ui/ui_preset_json.c
@@ -1,5 +1,7 @@
+#include <math.h>
 #include <stdarg.h>
 #include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
 #include <string.h>
 
 #include "cJSON.h"
@@ -110,6 +112,128 @@ static bool parse_titles(const cJSON *titles, const ui_layout_t *layout, ui_pres
     return true;
 }
 
+static const char *const k_visual_names[UI_VIS_COUNT] = {
+    [UI_VIS_CHART] = "chart", [UI_VIS_BAR] = "bar", [UI_VIS_RING] = "ring", [UI_VIS_GAUGE] = "gauge",
+};
+
+/* A visual's bound (spec §5.4, M7): left out for the field's own, a number, or "auto" for a chart's. */
+static bool parse_bound(const cJSON *b, uint8_t as, uint8_t *mode, float *out, const char *id, char *err, size_t size)
+{
+    if (b == NULL || cJSON_IsNull(b)) {
+        *mode = UI_LIMIT_FIELD;
+        return true;
+    }
+    if (cJSON_IsString(b) && strcmp(b->valuestring, "auto") == 0) {
+        if (as != UI_VIS_CHART) {
+            return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": only a chart's min and max can be auto", id);
+        }
+        *mode = UI_LIMIT_AUTO;
+        return true;
+    }
+    if (!cJSON_IsNumber(b) || !isfinite((float)b->valuedouble)) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": a visual's min and max are numbers", id);
+    }
+    *mode = UI_LIMIT_VALUE;
+    *out = (float)b->valuedouble;
+    return true;
+}
+
+/* How slot `slot` (named `what` in a reason) shows its field, which must be a number (spec §5.4, M7, D44). */
+static bool parse_visual(const cJSON *o, ui_presets_t *all, int index, int slot, const char *what, char *err,
+                         size_t size)
+{
+    const char *id = all->presets[index].id;
+    if (!cJSON_IsObject(o)) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": a visual must be an object", id);
+    }
+    if (!ui_field_numeric((ui_field_id_t)all->presets[index].slots[slot])) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": %s shows no number", id, what);
+    }
+    const cJSON *as = cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(o, "as");
+    ui_visual_t vis = { .as = UI_VIS_VALUE, .span_s = UI_SPAN_DEFAULT_S };
+    for (int a = UI_VIS_CHART; a < UI_VIS_COUNT && cJSON_IsString(as); a++) {
+        if (strcmp(as->valuestring, k_visual_names[a]) == 0) {
+            vis.as = (uint8_t)a;
+        }
+    }
+    if (vis.as == UI_VIS_VALUE) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": a visual is chart, bar, ring or gauge", id);
+    }
+    if (!parse_bound(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(o, "min"), vis.as, &vis.min_mode, &vis.min, id, err, size) ||
+        !parse_bound(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(o, "max"), vis.as, &vis.max_mode, &vis.max, id, err, size)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (vis.min_mode == UI_LIMIT_VALUE && vis.max_mode == UI_LIMIT_VALUE && !(vis.min < vis.max)) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": a visual's min must be below its max", id);
+    }
+    if (vis.as == UI_VIS_CHART) {
+        const cJSON *span = cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(o, "span_s");
+        if (span != NULL && !cJSON_IsNull(span)) {
+            double s = cJSON_IsNumber(span) ? span->valuedouble : -1.0;
+            if (s != floor(s) || s < 0 || s > UI_SPAN_MAX_S || !ui_visual_span_ok((uint32_t)s)) {
+                return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": a chart's span is 10-3600 s, or whole hours up to 24 h", id);
+            }
+            vis.span_s = (uint32_t)s;
+        }
+        vis.numbers = optional_bool(o, "numbers", true);
+    }
+    if (!ui_presets_set_visual(all, index, slot, &vis)) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": %d visuals at most in all presets", id, UI_VISUALS_MAX);
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+static bool parse_visuals(const cJSON *visuals, const ui_layout_t *layout, ui_presets_t *all, int index, char *err,
+                          size_t size)
+{
+    const char *id = all->presets[index].id;
+    if (visuals == NULL || cJSON_IsNull(visuals)) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    if (!cJSON_IsObject(visuals)) {
+        return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": visuals must be an object of slot names", id);
+    }
+    const cJSON *visual;
+    cJSON_ArrayForEach(visual, visuals)
+    {
+        int slot = ui_slot_by_name(layout, visual->string);
+        if (slot < 0) {
+            return fail(err, size, "preset \"%s\": layout %s has no slot \"%s\"", id, layout->id, visual->string);
+        }
+        char what[24];
+        snprintf(what, sizeof(what), "slot %s", layout->slots[slot].name);
+        if (!parse_visual(visual, all, index, slot, what, err, size)) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+/* A bound as the file writes it: a float's own digits, not its double's. */
+static void add_bound(cJSON *o, const char *key, uint8_t mode, float v)
+{
+    if (mode == UI_LIMIT_AUTO) {
+        cJSON_AddStringToObject(o, key, "auto");
+    } else if (mode == UI_LIMIT_VALUE) {
+        char digits[24];
+        snprintf(digits, sizeof(digits), "%.7g", (double)v);
+        cJSON_AddNumberToObject(o, key, strtod(digits, NULL));
+    }
+}
+
+static cJSON *visual_json(const ui_visual_t *v)
+{
+    cJSON *o = cJSON_CreateObject();
+    cJSON_AddStringToObject(o, "as", v->as < UI_VIS_COUNT && k_visual_names[v->as] ? k_visual_names[v->as] : "bar");
+    add_bound(o, "min", v->min_mode, v->min);
+    add_bound(o, "max", v->max_mode, v->max);
+    if (v->as == UI_VIS_CHART) {
+        cJSON_AddNumberToObject(o, "span_s", v->span_s);
+        cJSON_AddBoolToObject(o, "numbers", v->numbers);
+    }
+    return o;
+}
+
 /* "22:30" -> minutes after midnight. */
 static bool parse_hhmm(const cJSON *item, uint16_t *out)
 {
@@ -214,6 +338,14 @@ static bool parse_node(tree_t *t, const cJSON *node)
```

```diff
+/* A slot's or cell's visual (spec §5.3, M7, D44), where its field is a number and the visual fits; the stale policy
+ * as the widgets take it. True if it is the visual's place, drawn or hidden. */
+static bool draw_visual(gfx_fb_t *fb, gfx_rect_t r, const ui_context_t *ctx, const ui_value_t *v,
+                        const ui_visual_t *vis, ui_stale_policy_t policy)
+{
+    if (vis == NULL || vis->as == UI_VIS_VALUE || !ui_field_numeric(v->field) ||
+        !ui_visual_fits((ui_vis_as_t)vis->as, r.w, r.h)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (ui_widget_hidden(v, policy)) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    ui_value_t shown = *v;
+    if (shown.state == UI_VALUE_STALE && policy != UI_STALE_STALE) {
+        shown.state = UI_VALUE_MISSING;
+    }
+    const ui_series_t *series = NULL;
+    char name[UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
+    if (vis->as == UI_VIS_CHART && ctx->history != NULL &&
+        ui_field_name(ctx->pc_ids, v->field, name, sizeof(name)) > 0) {
+        series = ui_history_find(ctx->history, name);
+    }
+    ui_visual_draw(fb, r, &shown, vis, series, ctx->now_ms, ctx->lang);
+    return true;
+}
```

```diff
+#define FIX_HISTORY_MS 7200000u /* the history's clock: two hours after the start */
+
+/* M7: two hours of the indoor temperature, every 5 min, for the charts. */
+static inline const ui_history_t *fixture_history(void)
+{
+    static ui_history_t h;
+    static ui_series_t *mem;
+    if (mem == NULL) {
+        mem = calloc(UI_HIST_SERIES, sizeof(ui_series_t));
+        ui_history_init(&h, mem);
+        const char *const names[] = { "env.temp" };
+        ui_history_track(&h, names, 1);
+        uint32_t seed = 7;
+        for (int i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
+            seed = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+            float jitter = (float)((seed >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f * 0.3f;
+            ui_series_push(ui_history_find(&h, "env.temp"), FIX_HISTORY_MS - (uint32_t)(24 - i) * 300000,
+                           21.2f + 0.09f * (float)i + jitter);
+        }
+    }
+    return &h;
+}

         preset->slots[0] = UI_FIELD_TIME_CLOCK;
```

### Task 9: The PC layout and preset, the monitor, "PC asleep" (`ui`)

**Files:**
- Create: `test/host/test_ui_pc_layout.c`, `test/host/golden/dash_pc_session.pbm`, `dash_pc_asleep.pbm`, `dash_pc_asleep_cs.pbm`, `dash_pc_new.pbm`
- Modify: `components/ui/include/ui_layout.h`, `components/ui/ui_layout.c`, `components/ui/include/ui_pc.h`, `components/ui/ui_pc.c`, `components/ui/include/ui_preset.h`, `components/ui/ui_preset.c`, `components/ui/ui_preset_json.c`, `components/ui/ui_dashboard.c`, `components/ui/ui_status.c`, `components/ui/ui_visual.c`, `components/locale/include/lang.h`, `components/locale/lang_en.c`, `components/locale/lang_cs.c`, `test/host/CMakeLists.txt`, `test/host/dashboard_fixtures.h`, `test/host/test_ui_catalog.c`, `test/host/test_ui_preset.c`, `test/host/golden/vis_visuals_missing.pbm`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 5–8.
- Produces:
  - `UI_LAYOUT_PC` (last, so stored layouts keep their numbers), slots `c1`–`c4` (199×99, M) and `b1`–`b3` (132×77, S), the spike's rectangles and lines;
  - `UI_OFFERED_PC` and the built-in preset `pc` ("PC"), outside the cycle, with the status clock and its slots empty;
  - `bool ui_presets_fill_pc(ui_presets_t *, const pc_catalog_t *)`: the first catalogue's first four readings as 60 s charts with their numbers, the next three as bars, only into a `pc` preset on the PC layout whose slots are all empty;
  - `int ui_presets_chart_fields(const ui_presets_t *, char names[UI_HIST_SERIES][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN])`: the fields the charts show, each once;
  - in `struct ui_pc`: `char name[32]` and `time_t since`; `void ui_pc_draw_asleep(gfx_fb_t *, const ui_context_t *)`; `void ui_pc_demo(pc_catalog_t *, pc_values_t *)`, `void ui_pc_demo_history(ui_history_t *, uint32_t now_ms)` (the sample PC: the spike's seven readings and their last minute);
  - `LS_PC_ASLEEP`, `LS_PC_SINCE`.

As approved from the spike's renders: during a session a small monitor after the globe in the status bar; without one "PC asleep" in a box across the layout with "since 18:12 · DESKTOP-PC" (D43's forms of the time, each part left out when unknown), its charts empty under it (their labels and baselines), its bars at "—".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`test/host/test_ui_pc_layout.c`:

```c
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L /* setenv() in fixture_zone() */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "dashboard_fixtures.h"
#include "gfx.h"
#include "ui_dashboard.h"
#include "ui_history.h"
#include "ui_layout.h"
#include "ui_pc.h"
#include "ui_preset.h"
#include "unity.h"

/* The PC layout and preset (spec §5.2, §5.4, M7, D44): four charts and three bars, the built-in PC preset filled
 * from the PC app's first catalogue, the status bar's monitor, and "PC asleep" without a session. */

static ui_presets_t s_p;
static pc_catalog_t s_cat;
static pc_values_t s_vals;
static char s_err[UI_PRESET_ERR_LEN];
static char s_json[UI_PRESETS_JSON_MAX];
static uint8_t s_buf[400 * 300 / 8], s_other[400 * 300 / 8];

void setUp(void)
{
    ui_presets_defaults(&s_p);
    ui_pc_demo(&s_cat, &s_vals);
}

void tearDown(void) {}

static void test_the_pc_layout_has_four_charts_and_three_bars(void)
{
    const ui_layout_t *l = ui_layout(UI_LAYOUT_PC);
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(l);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc", l->id);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(7, l->slot_count);
    static const char *const k_names[] = { "c1", "c2", "c3", "c4", "b1", "b2", "b3" };
    for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING(k_names[i], l->slots[i].name);
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_CHART, l->slots[0].rect.w, l->slots[0].rect.h));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_visual_fits(UI_VIS_BAR, l->slots[4].rect.w, l->slots[4].rect.h));
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(l->slots[0].kinds & UI_KIND(UI_FK_NUMBER));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LAYOUT_PC, ui_layout_by_name("pc"));
}

static void test_the_built_in_pc_preset_waits_for_the_first_catalogue(void)
{
    int i = ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc");
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(8, i);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(9, s_p.count);
    const ui_preset_t *pc = &s_p.presets[i];
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("PC", pc->name);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LAYOUT_PC, pc->layout);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(pc->in_cycle);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(pc->status_clock);
    for (int s = 0; s < 7; s++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, pc->slots[s]);
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0, s_p.visual_count);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(s_p.offered & UI_OFFERED_PC);
}

static void test_the_first_catalogue_fills_its_charts_and_bars(void)
{
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat)); /* changed: save it */
    int i = ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc");
    char name[24];
    static const char *const k_fields[] = { "pc.cpu_load", "pc.cpu_temp", "pc.gpu_load", "pc.gpu_temp",
                                            "pc.ram_used", "pc.vram_used", "pc.net_down" };
    for (int s = 0; s < 7; s++) {
        ui_presets_field_name(&s_p, (ui_field_id_t)s_p.presets[i].slots[s], name, sizeof(name));
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING(k_fields[s], name);
        const ui_visual_t *v = ui_presets_visual(&s_p, i, s);
        TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(v);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(s < 4 ? UI_VIS_CHART : UI_VIS_BAR, v->as);
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_LIMIT_FIELD, v->min_mode);
        if (s < 4) {
            TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT32(60, v->span_s);
            TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(v->numbers);
        }
    }
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat)); /* filled once */
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_to_json(&s_p, s_json, sizeof(s_json)) > 0); /* and the file reads back */
    ui_presets_t back;
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(s_json, &back, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(&s_p, &back, sizeof(back));
}

static void test_a_short_catalogue_fills_what_it_has(void)
{
    s_cat.count = 2;
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat));
    const ui_preset_t *pc = &s_p.presets[ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc")];
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_field_is_pc((ui_field_id_t)pc->slots[1]));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, pc->slots[2]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(2, s_p.visual_count);
    s_cat.count = 0;
    ui_presets_defaults(&s_p);
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat)); /* nothing to fill with */
}

static void test_the_owners_pc_preset_is_left_as_it_is(void)
{
    int i = ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc");
    s_p.presets[i].slots[6] = UI_FIELD_ENV_TEMP; /* the owner put something in it */
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(UI_FIELD_NONE, s_p.presets[i].slots[0]);
    ui_presets_defaults(&s_p);
    s_p.presets[ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc")].layout = UI_LAYOUT_GRID; /* a preset of its own with that id */
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat));
    s_p.count = 8; /* deleted */
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat));
}

static void test_a_file_from_m6d_gains_the_pc_preset_once(void)
{
    static const char k_m6d[] = "{\"schema\":1,\"active\":\"home\",\"offered\":[\"rain\",\"flights\",\"solar\","
                                "\"energy\"],\"presets\":[{\"id\":\"home\",\"layout\":\"classic\"}]}";
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(ui_presets_from_json(k_m6d, &s_p, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_offer_builtins(&s_p));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(2, s_p.count);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(1, ui_presets_find(&s_p, "pc"));
    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(ui_presets_offer_builtins(&s_p));
    ui_presets_to_json(&s_p, s_json, sizeof(s_json));
    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(strstr(s_json, "\"offered\":[\"rain\",\"flights\",\"solar\",\"energy\",\"pc\"]"));
}

static void test_the_charted_fields_are_the_historys_series(void)
{
    ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_p, &s_cat);
    ui_visual_t chart = { .as = UI_VIS_CHART, .span_s = 3600 };
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 1, 0, &chart)); /* Indoor's g1: env.temp */
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_presets_set_visual(&s_p, 0, 2, &chart)); /* Home's s1: env.temp again */
    char names[UI_HIST_SERIES][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
    int n = ui_presets_chart_fields(&s_p, names);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(5, n);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("env.temp", names[0]); /* in preset order, each once */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc.cpu_load", names[1]);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc.gpu_temp", names[4]);
}

static void draw(uint8_t *buf, const ui_context_t *ctx, const ui_preset_t *p)
{
    gfx_fb_t fb;
    gfx_fb_init(&fb, buf, 400, 300);
    ui_draw_dashboard(&fb, ctx, p, NULL);
}

static void test_a_session_shows_a_monitor_in_the_status_bar(void)
{
    ui_context_t ctx = fixture_context();
    ui_preset_t home = fixture_preset("home");
    ui_pc_t pc = { .session = false, .catalog = &s_cat };
    ctx.pc = &pc;
    draw(s_buf, &ctx, &home);
    pc.session = true;
    draw(s_other, &ctx, &home);
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(memcmp(s_buf, s_other, 400 / 8 * 20) != 0); /* the status bar */
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(s_buf + 400 / 8 * 20, s_other + 400 / 8 * 20, sizeof(s_buf) - 400 / 8 * 20);
}

int main(void)
{
    UNITY_BEGIN();
    RUN_TEST(test_the_pc_layout_has_four_charts_and_three_bars);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_built_in_pc_preset_waits_for_the_first_catalogue);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_first_catalogue_fills_its_charts_and_bars);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_short_catalogue_fills_what_it_has);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_owners_pc_preset_is_left_as_it_is);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_file_from_m6d_gains_the_pc_preset_once);
    RUN_TEST(test_the_charted_fields_are_the_historys_series);
    RUN_TEST(test_a_session_shows_a_monitor_in_the_status_bar);
    return UNITY_END();
}
```

`components/ui/ui_preset.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/ui/ui_preset.c
+++ b/components/ui/ui_preset.c
@@ -55,6 +55,7 @@ static const struct {
     { UI_OFFERED_FLIGHTS, "flights", "Flights", UI_LAYOUT_FLIGHTS, true, false },
     { UI_OFFERED_SOLAR, "solar", "Solar", UI_LAYOUT_SOLAR, false, true },

+    { UI_OFFERED_PC, "pc", "PC", UI_LAYOUT_PC, false, false }, /* M7: filled from the first catalogue */
 };
 
 bool ui_presets_offer_builtins(ui_presets_t *p)
@@ -211,6 +212,55 @@ bool ui_presets_set_visual(ui_presets_t *p, int preset, int slot, const ui_visua
     return true;
 }
 
+bool ui_presets_fill_pc(ui_presets_t *p, const pc_catalog_t *cat)
+{
+    int i = ui_presets_find(p, "pc");
+    if (i < 0 || p->presets[i].layout != UI_LAYOUT_PC || cat->count == 0) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    ui_preset_t *pc = &p->presets[i];
+    const ui_layout_t *layout = ui_layout(UI_LAYOUT_PC);
+    for (int s = 0; s < layout->slot_count; s++) {
+        if (pc->slots[s] != UI_FIELD_NONE) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    bool changed = false;
+    for (int s = 0; s < layout->slot_count && s < cat->count; s++) {
+        ui_field_id_t f = ui_presets_pc_field(p, cat->r[s].id);
+        ui_visual_t vis = { .as = s < 4 ? UI_VIS_CHART : UI_VIS_BAR, .span_s = UI_SPAN_DEFAULT_S, .numbers = s < 4 };
+        if (f == UI_FIELD_NONE || !ui_presets_set_visual(p, i, s, &vis)) {
+            break; /* the tables are full: the rest stay empty */
+        }
+        pc->slots[s] = (uint8_t)f;
+        changed = true;
+    }
+    return changed;
+}
+
+int ui_presets_chart_fields(const ui_presets_t *p, char names[UI_HIST_SERIES][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN])
+{
+    int n = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < p->visual_count && n < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
+        const ui_visual_entry_t *e = &p->visuals[i];
+        if (e->vis.as != UI_VIS_CHART || e->preset >= p->count) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        char name[UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
+        if (ui_presets_field_name(p, (ui_field_id_t)p->presets[e->preset].slots[e->slot], name, sizeof(name)) == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        bool seen = false;
+        for (int k = 0; k < n && !seen; k++) {
+            seen = strcmp(names[k], name) == 0;
+        }
+        if (!seen) {
+            memcpy(names[n++], name, sizeof(name));
+        }
+    }
+    return n;
+}
+
 const char *ui_presets_title(const ui_presets_t *p, int preset, int slot)
 {
     int n;
```

`components/ui/ui_pc.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/ui/ui_pc.c
+++ b/components/ui/ui_pc.c
@@ -4,6 +4,7 @@
 #include <stdio.h>
 #include <string.h>
 
+#include "gfx_fonts.h"
 #include "ui_internal.h"
 
 /* The PC's readings as fields (spec §5.1, §12a, D44). */
@@ -60,3 +61,93 @@ bool ui_resolve_pc(const ui_context_t *ctx, ui_field_id_t field, ui_value_t *out
     out->age_s = pc->age_ms / 1000;
     return true;
 }
+
+void ui_pc_draw_asleep(gfx_fb_t *fb, const ui_context_t *ctx)
+{
+    const ui_pc_t *pc = ctx->pc;
+    gfx_rect_t box = { 100, 140, 200, 60 };
+    gfx_fill_rect(fb, box, GFX_WHITE);
+    gfx_rect(fb, box, GFX_BLACK);
+    gfx_text_in_rect(fb, &gfx_font_sans_bold_28, (gfx_rect_t){ 100, 142, 200, 34 }, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER,
+                     lang_str(ctx->lang, LS_PC_ASLEEP), GFX_BLACK);
+    char since[32] = "", line[80];
+    if (pc != NULL && pc->since != 0 && ctx->time_valid) {
+        char when[24];
+        ui_sync_time_text(ctx, pc->since, false, when, sizeof(when));
+        ui_fill(lang_str(ctx->lang, LS_PC_SINCE), when, since, sizeof(since));
+    }
+    const char *name = pc != NULL ? pc->name : "";
+    snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%s%s%s", since, since[0] && name[0] ? " · " : "", name);
+    char fit[80];
+    if (line[0] && gfx_text_ellipsize(&gfx_font_sans_12, line, box.w - 8, fit, sizeof(fit)) > 0) {
+        gfx_text_in_rect(fb, &gfx_font_sans_12, (gfx_rect_t){ 100, 176, 200, 20 }, GFX_ALIGN_CENTER, fit, GFX_BLACK);
+    }
+}
+
+/* ---- the sample PC ---- */
+
+#define DEMO_N 60
+
+static const struct {
+    const char *id, *label, *unit;
+    uint8_t decimals;
+    float min, max, value; /* `value` for the readings without a history */
+} k_demo[] = {
+    { "cpu_load", "CPU load", "%", 0, 0, 100, 0 },     { "cpu_temp", "CPU", "°C", 0, 20, 100, 0 },
+    { "gpu_load", "GPU load", "%", 0, 0, 100, 0 },     { "gpu_temp", "GPU", "°C", 0, 20, 100, 0 },
+    { "ram_used", "RAM", "GB", 1, 0, 64, 21.3f },      { "vram_used", "VRAM", "GB", 1, 0, 12, 6.2f },
+    { "net_down", "Network", "MB/s", 1, 0, 100, 12.4f },
+};
+
+/* The charted readings' last minute, as the spike made them up. */
+static void demo_series(float out[4][DEMO_N])
+{
+    uint32_t seed = 12345;
+    for (int i = 0; i < DEMO_N; i++) {
+        float t = (float)i, r[4];
+        for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
+            seed = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+            r[k] = (float)((seed >> 8) & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0f;
+        }
+        out[0][i] = 18.0f + 10.0f * sinf(t / 6.0f) + 8.0f * r[0] + (i > 40 ? 35.0f : 0.0f);
+        out[1][i] = 52.0f + 6.0f * sinf(t / 9.0f) + 2.0f * r[1] + (i > 40 ? 14.0f : 0.0f);
+        out[2][i] = i < 20 ? 3.0f + 2.0f * r[2] : 88.0f + 10.0f * r[2];
+        out[3][i] = i < 20 ? 41.0f + r[3] : 41.0f + (t - 20.0f) * 0.6f + r[3];
+    }
+}
+
+void ui_pc_demo(pc_catalog_t *cat, pc_values_t *vals)
+{
+    float series[4][DEMO_N];
+    demo_series(series);
+    memset(cat, 0, sizeof(*cat));
+    memset(vals, 0, sizeof(*vals));
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(k_demo) / sizeof(k_demo[0]); i++) {
+        pc_reading_t *r = &cat->r[i];
+        snprintf(r->id, sizeof(r->id), "%s", k_demo[i].id);
+        snprintf(r->label, sizeof(r->label), "%s", k_demo[i].label);
+        snprintf(r->unit, sizeof(r->unit), "%s", k_demo[i].unit);
+        r->decimals = k_demo[i].decimals;
+        r->has_min = r->has_max = true;
+        r->min = k_demo[i].min;
+        r->max = k_demo[i].max;
+        vals->has[i] = true;
+        vals->v[i] = i < 4 ? series[i][DEMO_N - 1] : k_demo[i].value;
+    }
+    cat->count = (uint8_t)(sizeof(k_demo) / sizeof(k_demo[0]));
+    vals->seq = 812;
+}
+
+void ui_pc_demo_history(ui_history_t *h, uint32_t now_ms)
+{
+    float series[4][DEMO_N];
+    demo_series(series);
+    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
+        char name[UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
+        snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "pc.%s", k_demo[k].id);
+        ui_series_t *s = ui_history_find(h, name);
+        for (int i = 0; s != NULL && i < DEMO_N; i++) {
+            ui_series_push(s, now_ms - (uint32_t)(DEMO_N - 1 - i) * 1000u, series[k][i]);
+        }
+    }
+}
```

```diff
+/* M7: the built-in presets with the PC preset filled from the sample PC's catalogue, its view and its last minute. */
+static ui_presets_t s_fix_pc_presets;
+static pc_catalog_t s_fix_pc_cat;
+static pc_values_t s_fix_pc_vals;
+static ui_pc_t s_fix_pc;
+
+static inline void fixture_pc(ui_context_t *ctx, ui_preset_t *preset, bool session, bool filled)
+{
+    ui_presets_defaults(&s_fix_pc_presets);
+    ui_pc_demo(&s_fix_pc_cat, &s_fix_pc_vals);
+    if (filled) {
+        ui_presets_fill_pc(&s_fix_pc_presets, &s_fix_pc_cat);
+    }
+    *preset = s_fix_pc_presets.presets[ui_presets_find(&s_fix_pc_presets, "pc")];
+    static ui_history_t h;
+    static ui_series_t *mem;
+    if (mem == NULL) {
+        mem = calloc(UI_HIST_SERIES, sizeof(ui_series_t));
+    }
+    ui_history_init(&h, mem);
+    char names[UI_HIST_SERIES][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
+    const char *list[UI_HIST_SERIES];
+    int n = ui_presets_chart_fields(&s_fix_pc_presets, names);
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+        list[i] = names[i];
+    }
+    ui_history_track(&h, list, n);
+    ui_pc_demo_history(&h, FIX_HISTORY_MS);
+    s_fix_pc = (ui_pc_t){ .session = session, .catalog = &s_fix_pc_cat, .values = &s_fix_pc_vals, .age_ms = 300,
+                          .interval_ms = 1000, .name = "DESKTOP-PC", .since = session ? 0 : FIX_NOW - 2 * 3600 - 36 * 60 };
+    ctx->pc = &s_fix_pc;
+    ctx->pc_ids = s_fix_pc_presets.pc_ids;
+    ctx->history = &h;
+    ctx->now_ms = FIX_HISTORY_MS;
+}
+
 /* Each fixture: a context and a preset. */

 {
@@ -421,6 +458,17 @@ static inline bool fixture_dashboard(const char *name, ui_context_t *ctx, ui_pre
         fixture_solar_ctx(ctx, true, false);
         ctx->history = fixture_history();
         ctx->now_ms = FIX_HISTORY_MS;
+    } else if (strcmp(name, "pc_session") == 0) { /* M7: the sample PC, its charts' last minute */
+        fixture_pc(ctx, preset, true, true);
+    } else if (strcmp(name, "pc_asleep") == 0) { /* its session ended at 18:12 */
+        fixture_pc(ctx, preset, false, true);
+    } else if (strcmp(name, "pc_asleep_cs") == 0) {
+        fixture_pc(ctx, preset, false, true);
+        ctx->lang = lang_get("cs");
+    } else if (strcmp(name, "pc_new") == 0) { /* before the first catalogue: no PC seen since the boot */
+        fixture_pc(ctx, preset, false, false);
+        s_fix_pc.since = 0;
+        s_fix_pc.name[0] = '\0';
     } else if (strcmp(name, "grid_clock_12h") == 0) { /* a clock in a grid cell, 12-hour */

@@ -536,6 +584,11 @@ static inline const ui_titles_t *fixture_titles(const char *name)
```

### Task 10: The PC's settings, its token and the PC step (`storage`, `ui`, `main`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `components/storage/include/settings.h`, `components/storage/settings.c`, `components/ui/include/ui_menu.h`, `components/ui/ui_menu.c`, `components/locale/include/lang.h`, `components/locale/lang_en.c`, `components/locale/lang_cs.c`, `main/app_menu.c`, `main/app_ui.c`, `test/host/test_settings.c`, `test/host/test_ui_menu.c`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - in `settings_t`: `pc_enabled`, `pc_host`, `pc_port`, `pc_interval_ms`, `pc_auto_always`, `pc_preset`; `SETTINGS_PC_PORT` (47123), `SETTINGS_PC_INTERVAL_MIN_MS`/`MAX_MS`, `SETTINGS_PC_PRESET_LEN`; `void settings_pc_defaults(settings_t *)`;
  - `SETTINGS_STEP_PC` (bit 5), `SETTINGS_STEPS_ALL` 0x3F, the step's name "pc";
  - `SETTINGS_SECRET_PC_TOKEN`, NVS key `pc_token`, JSON `pc.token`: letters, digits, dashes and spaces, 63 at most, kept as typed;
  - `UI_MI_STEP_PC` in Menu ▸ Sync ▸ Steps, `LS_SYNC_STEP_PC`.

Spec §12a.1. Each bad value keeps its default, as the other blocks do. A steps list from before M7 never names "pc", so a file without a `pc` object runs the PC step; M7 writes the object at every save, after which the list says it.

```diff
+static void test_the_pc_defaults_are_the_specs(void)
+{
+    settings_t d;
+    memset(&d, 0xAA, sizeof(d));
+    settings_pc_defaults(&d); /* spec §12a.1 */
+    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(d.pc_enabled);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", d.pc_host);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(47123, d.pc_port);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(1000, d.pc_interval_ms);
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(d.pc_auto_always);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc", d.pc_preset);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc", settings_step_name(SETTINGS_STEP_PC));
+}
+
+static void test_the_pc_settings_parse_clamp_and_round_trip(void)
+{
+    const char *json = "{\"schema\":1,\"pc\":{\"enabled\":true,\"host\":\"desktop-pc.local\",\"port\":47200,"
+                       "\"interval_ms\":100,\"auto_always\":false,\"preset\":\"gaming\"}}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(json, &s_defaults, &s_out, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(s_out.pc_enabled);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("desktop-pc.local", s_out.pc_host);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(47200, s_out.pc_port);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(250, s_out.pc_interval_ms); /* clamped to 250 ms-10 s */
+    TEST_ASSERT_FALSE(s_out.pc_auto_always);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("gaming", s_out.pc_preset);
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(settings_to_json(&s_out, NULL, s_json, sizeof(s_json)) > 0);
+    settings_t again;
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(s_json, &s_defaults, &again, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_MEMORY(&s_out, &again, sizeof(again));
+    /* one bad value at a time takes its default; "" is no preset */
+    json = "{\"schema\":1,\"pc\":{\"host\":\"bad host/\",\"port\":0,\"interval_ms\":60000,\"preset\":\"\"}}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(json, &s_defaults, &s_out, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", s_out.pc_host);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(47123, s_out.pc_port);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT16(10000, s_out.pc_interval_ms);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", s_out.pc_preset);
+    json = "{\"schema\":1,\"pc\":{\"host\":\"192.168.1.20\",\"preset\":\"Not An Id\"}}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(json, &s_defaults, &s_out, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("192.168.1.20", s_out.pc_host);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc", s_out.pc_preset);
+}
+
+/* A steps list from before M7 never names the PC step: a file without a "pc" object runs it. */
+static void test_an_older_files_steps_run_the_pc_step(void)
+{
+    const char *json = "{\"schema\":1,\"sync\":{\"steps\":[\"weather\",\"energy\"]}}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(json, &s_defaults, &s_out, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT8(SETTINGS_STEP_WEATHER | SETTINGS_STEP_ENERGY | SETTINGS_STEP_PC, s_out.sync_steps);
+    json = "{\"schema\":1,\"pc\":{},\"sync\":{\"steps\":[\"weather\",\"energy\"]}}"; /* M7's own: as it says */
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE_MESSAGE(settings_from_json(json, &s_defaults, &s_out, s_err, sizeof(s_err)), s_err);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_UINT8(SETTINGS_STEP_WEATHER | SETTINGS_STEP_ENERGY, s_out.sync_steps);
+    s_out.sync_steps |= SETTINGS_STEP_PC;
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(settings_to_json(&s_out, NULL, s_json, sizeof(s_json)) > 0);
+    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(strstr(s_json, "\"steps\":\t[\"weather\", \"energy\", \"pc\"]"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(strstr(s_json, "\"pc\":\t{"));
+}
+
 static void test_the_largest_settings_fit_the_file_buffer(void)
 {
     settings_t s = s_defaults;

     TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(n > 0);
```

```diff
+void settings_pc_defaults(settings_t *out)
+{
+    out->pc_enabled = false;
+    out->pc_host[0] = '\0';
+    out->pc_port = SETTINGS_PC_PORT;
+    out->pc_interval_ms = 1000;
+    out->pc_auto_always = true;
+    snprintf(out->pc_preset, sizeof(out->pc_preset), "%s", "pc");
+}
+
+/* A preset id's characters: a-z, 0-9, - and _ (ui_preset_json.c), 15 at most. */
+static bool preset_id(const char *s)
+{
+    size_t n = strlen(s);
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+        char c = s[i];
+        if (!((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '-' || c == '_')) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    return n < SETTINGS_PC_PRESET_LEN;
+}
+
+/* pc (spec §12a.1): each bad value keeps its default. */
+static void read_pc(const cJSON *pc, settings_t *out)
+{
+    read_bool(pc, "enabled", &out->pc_enabled);
+    const cJSON *host = child(pc, "host");
+    if (cJSON_IsString(host) && (host->valuestring[0] == '\0' || host_name(host->valuestring))) {
+        snprintf(out->pc_host, sizeof(out->pc_host), "%s", host->valuestring);
+    }
+    const cJSON *port = child(pc, "port");
+    if (cJSON_IsNumber(port) && port->valuedouble >= 1 && port->valuedouble <= 65535) {
+        out->pc_port = (uint16_t)port->valuedouble;
+    }
+    out->pc_interval_ms = (uint16_t)read_scaled(pc, "interval_ms", out->pc_interval_ms, 1, SETTINGS_PC_INTERVAL_MIN_MS,
+                                                SETTINGS_PC_INTERVAL_MAX_MS);
+    read_bool(pc, "auto_always", &out->pc_auto_always);
+    const cJSON *preset = child(pc, "preset");
+    if (cJSON_IsString(preset) && preset_id(preset->valuestring)) {
+        snprintf(out->pc_preset, sizeof(out->pc_preset), "%s", preset->valuestring);
+    }
+}
```

### Task 11: The PC step in the sync, and the stream's WebSocket (`pcstats`, `sync`, `main`)

**Files:**
- Create: `components/pcstats/idf_component.yml`, `components/pcstats/include/pc_ws.h`, `components/pcstats/pc_ws.c`
- Modify: `components/pcstats/CMakeLists.txt`, `components/pcstats/include/pc_msg.h`, `components/pcstats/pc_msg.c`, `components/sync/CMakeLists.txt`, `components/sync/include/sync.h`, `components/sync/include/sync_plan.h`, `components/sync/sync.c`, `components/sync/sync_plan.c`, `main/app.c`, `main/app_sync.c`, `test/host/test_pcstats.c`, `test/host/test_sync_plan.c`, `dependencies.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pc_parse_info()` (Task 1), the PC settings and token (Task 10), `fetch_get()` with its bearer (M6d).
- Produces:
  - `pc_probe_t` (`PC_PROBE_FOUND`, `_OFF`, `_REFUSED`) and `pc_probe_t pc_probe_result(int status, const char *body, size_t len, pc_info_t *info, char *detail, size_t size)`;
  - `pc_close_t` (`PC_CLOSE_WAIT`, `PC_CLOSE_END`) and `pc_close_t pc_close_action(int close_code, const char *reason, int http_status, char *why, size_t size)`: what a closed stream means for its session;
  - `SYNC_STEP_PC` (after the Energy step), which like it never fails a sync; `sync_step_name()` "pc";
  - `sync_pc_req_t` {`enabled`, `host`, `port`, `token`} in `sync_request_t` (a full sync's), `pc_info_t pc` in `sync_report_t`;
  - `pc_ws.h`: `pc_ws_event_t` (`PC_WS_CONNECTED`, `_FRAME`, `_CLOSED`, `_TOO_BIG`); `pc_ws_msg_t` {`ev`, `gen`, `frame` (PSRAM, the callee's to free), `len`, `close_code`, `http_status`, `reason[32]`}; `typedef void (*pc_ws_fn)(const pc_ws_msg_t *msg)`; `esp_err_t pc_ws_open(const char *host, uint16_t port, const char *token, pc_ws_fn fn, uint32_t *gen)`, `esp_err_t pc_ws_send(const char *text, size_t len)`, `void pc_ws_close(void)`, which never waits;
  - `SNAP_VERSION` 17.

Spec §9.3 and §12a.2: with PC stats on and an address set, a full sync asks `GET http://<host>:<port>/v1/info` with the token and 2 s, after the others. Its outcome is pure logic (`pc_probe_result()`): found (a session may start; "HWiNFO …" when HWiNFO isn't well), off (no answer: the PC asleep, no failure), or refused ("wrong token", "HTTP 503", "protocol 2", "not reflbo-stats"), which fails the step and never the sync. A closed stream's meaning is pure logic too (`pc_close_action()`): the PC app's Quit (1001), its refusals (1008 with a reason: "new token", "protocol 2", "no hello", "too deep") and a refused upgrade (401 "wrong token", any other status as "HTTP 503") end the session at once, as the PC app's spec §8 promises its Quit does; a connection lost without a word, or the app's 1001 "no pong" when the board's answers to its pings were lost, waits for the silence rule (Task 12), whose one reconnect may bring it back. The stream (§12a.3) is `espressif/esp_websocket_client` 1.8.0, without its own reconnects (the session reconnects once, Task 12). Each connection has its own buffer in PSRAM: a message is put together from its fragments, at most 8 KB, before it leaves the client's task, and one over 8 KB is reported for the session to close; a close is told once, with its code and reason or the upgrade's status. `pc_ws_close()` returns at once: a task of its own (`pc_ws_close`, 3 KB of stack, priority 2) closes the connection and destroys the client, which may take seconds against a PC that went to sleep, so the app task never waits on it; an old connection's late events carry its generation, which the session ignores.

`test/host/test_pcstats.c`:

```diff
--- a/test/host/test_pcstats.c
+++ b/test/host/test_pcstats.c
@@ -174,6 +174,56 @@ static void test_a_number_no_float_holds_is_no_value(void)
     TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("protocol 2147483647", s_err);
 }
 
+/* The PC step's answer (spec §9.3, §12a.2): found, asleep, or refused with the reason. */
+static void test_the_probe_reads_the_pcs_answer(void)
+{
+    pc_info_t info;
+    char detail[32];
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_OFF, pc_probe_result(0, "", 0, &info, detail, sizeof(detail))); /* no answer */
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("off", detail);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_REFUSED, pc_probe_result(401, "", 0, &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("wrong token", detail);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_REFUSED, pc_probe_result(503, "", 0, &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("HTTP 503", detail);
+    const char *ok = "{\"app\":\"reflbo-stats\",\"protocol\":1,\"name\":\"DESKTOP-PC\",\"hwinfo\":\"ok\"}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_FOUND, pc_probe_result(200, ok, strlen(ok), &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", detail);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("DESKTOP-PC", info.name);
+    const char *stale = "{\"app\":\"reflbo-stats\",\"protocol\":1,\"name\":\"PC\",\"hwinfo\":\"not running\"}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_FOUND, pc_probe_result(200, stale, strlen(stale), &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("HWiNFO not running", detail); /* a session starts and says why its values are missing */
+    const char *v2 = "{\"app\":\"reflbo-stats\",\"protocol\":2}";
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_REFUSED, pc_probe_result(200, v2, strlen(v2), &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("protocol 2", detail);
+    const char *other = "<html>router</html>";
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_PROBE_REFUSED, pc_probe_result(200, other, strlen(other), &info, detail, sizeof(detail)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("not reflbo-stats", detail);
+}
+
+/* A stream that closes (spec §12a.3, the PC app's §6): the app quitting or turning the board away ends the session at
+ * once, as a refused upgrade does; a lost connection waits for the silence rule. */
+static void test_a_closed_stream_ends_its_session_or_waits(void)
+{
+    char why[48];
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(1001, "quit", 0, why, sizeof(why)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("the PC app quit", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(1008, "new token", 0, why, sizeof(why)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("new token", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(1008, "protocol 2", 0, why, sizeof(why)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("protocol 2", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(1008, "", 0, why, sizeof(why)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("refused", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(0, "", 401, why, sizeof(why)));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("wrong token", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_END, pc_close_action(0, "", 503, why, sizeof(why))); /* eight boards already */
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("HTTP 503", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_WAIT, pc_close_action(1001, "no pong", 0, why, sizeof(why))); /* its pings lost */
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("no pong", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_WAIT, pc_close_action(1006, "", 0, why, sizeof(why))); /* lost */
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("closed", why);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(PC_CLOSE_WAIT, pc_close_action(0, "", 0, why, sizeof(why)));
+}
+
 static void test_the_catalogue_survives_its_file(void)
 {
     parse(k_catalog, NULL);
@@ -199,6 +249,8 @@ int main(void)
     RUN_TEST(test_long_labels_and_units_are_cut_at_a_character);
     RUN_TEST(test_info_names_the_app_its_protocol_and_hwinfo);
     RUN_TEST(test_a_number_no_float_holds_is_no_value);
+    RUN_TEST(test_the_probe_reads_the_pcs_answer);
+    RUN_TEST(test_a_closed_stream_ends_its_session_or_waits);
     RUN_TEST(test_the_catalogue_survives_its_file);
     return UNITY_END();
 }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The probe's outcome, the close's meaning and the step's verdict.**

`components/pcstats/include/pc_msg.h`:

```diff
--- a/components/pcstats/include/pc_msg.h
+++ b/components/pcstats/include/pc_msg.h
@@ -74,6 +74,25 @@ size_t pc_catalog_json(const pc_catalog_t *cat, char *out, size_t size);
 /* A reading's id: 1-15 characters of a-z, 0-9 and _ (spec §12a.3); presets.json's `pc.<id>` keeps the same rule. */
 bool pc_id_valid(const char *id);
 
+/* What the PC step found (spec §9.3, §12a.2). */
+typedef enum {
+    PC_PROBE_FOUND,   /* a session may start; the detail names HWiNFO's trouble, "" when it is fine */
+    PC_PROBE_OFF,     /* no answer: the PC asleep, no failure */
+    PC_PROBE_REFUSED, /* a wrong token, another protocol, another app: the step fails */
+} pc_probe_t;
+
+/* GET /v1/info's outcome: its HTTP status (0 for no reply) and body; `info` filled when found, the step's detail in
+ * `detail`: "off", "wrong token", "HTTP 503", "protocol 2", "not reflbo-stats", "HWiNFO stale" or "". */
+pc_probe_t pc_probe_result(int status, const char *body, size_t len, pc_info_t *info, char *detail, size_t size);
+
+/* What a closed stream means for its session (spec §12a.3, the PC app's spec §6): the app quitting (close 1001) or
+ * turning the board away (1008: a new token, another protocol) ends it at once, as a refused upgrade does (HTTP 401,
+ * 503 with eight boards already); anything else waits for the silence rule: a lost connection, or the app's 1001
+ * "no pong" when the board's answers to its pings were lost. `why` gets the reason: "the PC app quit", "new token",
+ * "wrong token", "HTTP 503", "no pong", "closed". */
+typedef enum { PC_CLOSE_WAIT, PC_CLOSE_END } pc_close_t;
+pc_close_t pc_close_action(int close_code, const char *reason, int http_status, char *why, size_t size);
+
 /* The index of the reading with `id`, or -1. */
 int pc_catalog_find(const pc_catalog_t *cat, const char *id);
```

`components/pcstats/pc_msg.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/pcstats/pc_msg.c
+++ b/components/pcstats/pc_msg.c
@@ -196,6 +196,58 @@ bool pc_parse_info(const char *json, size_t len, pc_info_t *info, char *err, siz
     return ok;
 }
 
+pc_probe_t pc_probe_result(int status, const char *body, size_t len, pc_info_t *info, char *detail, size_t size)
+{
+    memset(info, 0, sizeof(*info));
+    if (status == 0) {
+        snprintf(detail, size, "off");
+        return PC_PROBE_OFF;
+    }
+    if (status != 200) {
+        if (status == 401) {
+            snprintf(detail, size, "wrong token");
+        } else {
+            snprintf(detail, size, "HTTP %d", status);
+        }
+        return PC_PROBE_REFUSED;
+    }
+    if (!pc_parse_info(body, len, info, detail, size)) {
+        return PC_PROBE_REFUSED;
+    }
+    if (info->hwinfo == PC_HW_OK) {
+        detail[0] = '\0';
+    } else {
+        snprintf(detail, size, "HWiNFO %s", pc_hwinfo_name(info->hwinfo));
+    }
+    return PC_PROBE_FOUND;
+}
+
+pc_close_t pc_close_action(int close_code, const char *reason, int http_status, char *why, size_t size)
+{
+    if (http_status == 401) {
+        snprintf(why, size, "wrong token");
+        return PC_CLOSE_END;
+    }
+    if (http_status != 0 && http_status != 101) {
+        snprintf(why, size, "HTTP %d", http_status);
+        return PC_CLOSE_END;
+    }
+    if (close_code == 1001 && reason != NULL && strcmp(reason, "no pong") == 0) { /* its pings lost: reconnect */
+        snprintf(why, size, "no pong");
+        return PC_CLOSE_WAIT;
+    }
+    if (close_code == 1001) { /* going away: the PC app quit */
+        snprintf(why, size, "the PC app quit");
+        return PC_CLOSE_END;
+    }
+    if (close_code == 1008) { /* policy: a new token, another protocol, no hello */
+        snprintf(why, size, "%s", reason != NULL && reason[0] ? reason : "refused");
+        return PC_CLOSE_END;
+    }
+    snprintf(why, size, "closed");
+    return PC_CLOSE_WAIT;
+}
+
 size_t pc_catalog_json(const pc_catalog_t *cat, char *out, size_t size)
 {
     cJSON *root = cJSON_CreateObject();
```

`components/sync/sync.c`:

```diff
--- a/components/sync/sync.c
+++ b/components/sync/sync.c
@@ -31,6 +31,7 @@ static const char *TAG = "sync";
 #define BODY_MAX (32 * 1024) /* the largest reply, Solcast's 72 h, is about 18 KB (M6d) */
 #define RADAR_STEP_MS 10000 /* spec §9.3 */
 #define REFRESH_MAX_MS 30000 /* a radar-only refresh, the last hour's 12 frames at most (D28) */
+#define PC_INFO_MS 2000      /* spec §12a.2 */
 
 static volatile bool s_running;
 static volatile uint8_t s_step = SYNC_STEP_COUNT;
@@ -592,6 +593,43 @@ static void step_energy(void)
     }
 }
 
+/* The PC app (spec §12a.2, M7): GET /v1/info with the token. No answer is the PC asleep, no failure; a wrong token
+ * or another protocol fails the step, never the sync (sync_report_failed()). */
+static void step_pc(void)
+{
+    if (!wanted(SYNC_STEP_PC, SETTINGS_STEP_PC)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const sync_pc_req_t *q = &s_req.pc;
+    if (!q->enabled || q->host[0] == '\0') {
+        skipped(SYNC_STEP_PC, q->enabled ? "no address" : "no PC");
+        return;
+    }
+    int budget = sync_budget_ms(esp_timer_get_time(), s_deadline_us, PC_INFO_MS);
+    if (budget == 0) {
+        skipped(SYNC_STEP_PC, "timeout"); /* the radio's 45 s are up */
+        return;
+    }
+    char url[SETTINGS_HOST_LEN + 32];
+    snprintf(url, sizeof(url), "http://%s:%u/v1/info", q->host, (unsigned)q->port);
+    fetch_session_t session = { .bearer = q->token };
+    size_t len = 0;
+    int status = 0;
+    fetch_get(&session, url, s_body, sizeof(s_body), &len, budget, &status); /* status 0: no reply */
+    fetch_close(&session);
+    char detail[SYNC_DETAIL_LEN];
+    pc_probe_t found = pc_probe_result(status, s_body, len, &s_report.pc, detail, sizeof(detail));
+    if (found == PC_PROBE_FOUND) {
+        s_report.result[SYNC_STEP_PC] = SYNC_STEP_OK;
+        util_json_text(s_report.detail[SYNC_STEP_PC], SYNC_DETAIL_LEN, detail);
+        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "pc: found %s%s%s", s_report.pc.name, detail[0] ? ", " : "", detail);
+    } else if (found == PC_PROBE_OFF) {
+        skipped(SYNC_STEP_PC, detail);
+    } else {
+        failed(SYNC_STEP_PC, detail);
+    }
+}
+
 /* Sync mode `always`: the radar, the house's reading or both, on the network Wi-Fi is on already (D23: never
  * joining). */
 static void refresh_task(int64_t start)
@@ -644,6 +682,8 @@ static void sync_task(void *arg)
         step_solar();
         s_step = SYNC_STEP_ENERGY;
         step_energy();
+        s_step = SYNC_STEP_PC;
+        step_pc();
     } else {
         const char *why = err == ESP_ERR_NOT_FOUND ? "no network saved" : err == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE ? "Wi-Fi busy"
                                                                                                     : "not joined";
@@ -695,6 +735,6 @@ sync_step_t sync_step(void)
 const char *sync_step_name(sync_step_t step)
 {
     static const char *const k_names[SYNC_STEP_COUNT] = { "wifi", "time", "weather", "air", "radar", "solar",
-                                                          "energy" };
+                                                          "energy", "pc" };
     return (unsigned)step < SYNC_STEP_COUNT ? k_names[step] : "";
 }
```
