- [ ] **Step 5: The stream.**

`components/pcstats/idf_component.yml`:

```yaml
dependencies:
  espressif/esp_websocket_client: "==1.8.0"
```


`components/pcstats/include/pc_ws.h`:

```c
#pragma once

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "esp_err.h"

/*
 * The stream to the PC app (spec §12a.3, M7, D44): a WebSocket to ws://<host>:<port>/v1/stream with the token, on
 * esp_websocket_client's task. Each event goes to the callback on that task, which hands it on without blocking.
 * One stream at a time; it doesn't reconnect by itself, as the session decides (spec §12a.3). Device only.
 */

typedef enum {
    PC_WS_CONNECTED, /* say hello */
    PC_WS_FRAME,     /* a text message, whole */
    PC_WS_CLOSED,    /* closed, refused or lost */
    PC_WS_TOO_BIG,   /* a message over PC_FRAME_MAX: close it (spec §12a.3) */
} pc_ws_event_t;

typedef struct {
    pc_ws_event_t ev;
    uint32_t gen;    /* one opening's events from the next one's */
    char *frame;     /* PC_WS_FRAME: NUL-terminated, `len` bytes, in PSRAM; the callee's to free() */
    size_t len;
    int close_code;  /* PC_WS_CLOSED: the PC app's close code (1001 quit, 1008 refused), 0 for none */
    int http_status; /* PC_WS_CLOSED: the upgrade's status when it was refused (401), 0 otherwise */
    char reason[32]; /* PC_WS_CLOSED: the close's reason ("new token") */
} pc_ws_msg_t;

typedef void (*pc_ws_fn)(const pc_ws_msg_t *msg);

/* Opens the stream, closing the one before; the generation of its events in *gen. */
esp_err_t pc_ws_open(const char *host, uint16_t port, const char *token, pc_ws_fn fn, uint32_t *gen);
/* A text frame: the hello. */
esp_err_t pc_ws_send(const char *text, size_t len);
/* Closes it without waiting: a task of its own closes the connection and frees it. Its events may still come, with
 * its generation. */
void pc_ws_close(void);
```


`components/pcstats/pc_ws.c`:

```c
#include "pc_ws.h"

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_websocket_client.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "pc_msg.h"

/* The stream to the PC app (spec §12a.3). A message is taken whole, at most 8 KB, in PSRAM, before it leaves the
 * client's task; each connection has its own buffer, as a closing one's task can still run beside the next. */

static const char *TAG = "pc_ws";

#define TASK_STACK 4096
#define TASK_PRIORITY 3 /* as the sync's: below netmgr (4) and the app (5) */
#define CLOSER_STACK 3072
#define CLOSER_PRIORITY 2
#define NETWORK_TIMEOUT_MS 5000
#define CLOSE_WAIT_MS 1000
#define OP_CONTINUATION 0x00
#define OP_TEXT 0x01
#define OP_CLOSE 0x08

typedef struct {
    esp_websocket_client_handle_t client;
    uint32_t gen;
    pc_ws_fn fn;
    char *buf; /* the message being taken, PC_FRAME_MAX + 1 */
    size_t have;
    bool taking, too_big, told;
    int close_code, http_status;
    char reason[32];
} conn_t;

static conn_t *s_conn; /* the open one; the app task's */
static uint32_t s_gen;

static void tell(conn_t *c, pc_ws_event_t ev, char *frame, size_t len)
{
    pc_ws_msg_t m = { .ev = ev, .gen = c->gen, .frame = frame, .len = len };
    if (ev == PC_WS_CLOSED) {
        m.close_code = c->close_code;
        m.http_status = c->http_status;
        snprintf(m.reason, sizeof(m.reason), "%s", c->reason);
    }
    c->fn(&m);
}

/* A data event: a close's code and reason, or a text message's part. A message is a text frame and its continuations;
 * a frame longer than the client's buffer comes in parts too (`payload_offset`). */
static void take(conn_t *c, const esp_websocket_event_data_t *d)
{
    if (d->op_code == OP_CLOSE) {
        if (d->data_len >= 2) {
            c->close_code = ((uint8_t)d->data_ptr[0] << 8) | (uint8_t)d->data_ptr[1];
            int n = d->data_len - 2 < (int)sizeof(c->reason) - 1 ? d->data_len - 2 : (int)sizeof(c->reason) - 1;
            memcpy(c->reason, d->data_ptr + 2, (size_t)n);
            c->reason[n] = '\0';
        }
        return;
    }
    if (d->op_code == OP_TEXT && d->payload_offset == 0) {
        c->taking = true; /* a new message */
        c->have = 0;
        c->too_big = false;
    } else if (d->op_code != OP_CONTINUATION && d->op_code != OP_TEXT) {
        return; /* pings and pongs: the client answers them */
    }
    if (!c->taking) {
        return; /* a continuation of nothing */
    }
    if (!c->too_big && c->have + (size_t)d->data_len > PC_FRAME_MAX) {
        c->too_big = true;
    }
    if (!c->too_big) {
        memcpy(c->buf + c->have, d->data_ptr, (size_t)d->data_len);
        c->have += (size_t)d->data_len;
    }
    if (!d->fin || d->payload_offset + d->data_len < d->payload_len) {
        return; /* more of it to come */
    }
    c->taking = false;
    if (c->too_big) {
        tell(c, PC_WS_TOO_BIG, NULL, 0);
        return;
    }
    char *copy = heap_caps_malloc(c->have + 1, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
    if (copy == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "no memory for a message of %u bytes", (unsigned)c->have);
        return;
    }
    memcpy(copy, c->buf, c->have);
    copy[c->have] = '\0';
    tell(c, PC_WS_FRAME, copy, c->have);
}

static void handler(void *arg, esp_event_base_t base, int32_t id, void *data)
{
    (void)base;
    conn_t *c = arg;
    const esp_websocket_event_data_t *d = data;
    switch (id) {
    case WEBSOCKET_EVENT_CONNECTED:
        c->taking = c->told = false;
        c->close_code = c->http_status = 0;
        c->reason[0] = '\0';
        tell(c, PC_WS_CONNECTED, NULL, 0);
        break;
    case WEBSOCKET_EVENT_DATA:
        take(c, d);
        break;
    case WEBSOCKET_EVENT_ERROR:
        if (d->error_handle.error_type == WEBSOCKET_ERROR_TYPE_HANDSHAKE) {
            c->http_status = d->error_handle.esp_ws_handshake_status_code; /* 401: a wrong token */
        }
        if (!c->told) {
            c->told = true;
            tell(c, PC_WS_CLOSED, NULL, 0);
        }
        break;
    case WEBSOCKET_EVENT_DISCONNECTED:
    case WEBSOCKET_EVENT_CLOSED:
        if (c->close_code == 0) {
            c->close_code = d->close_status_code;
        }
        if (!c->told) {
            c->told = true;
            tell(c, PC_WS_CLOSED, NULL, 0);
        }
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

static void conn_free(conn_t *c)
{
    if (c->client != NULL) {
        if (esp_websocket_client_is_connected(c->client)) {
            esp_websocket_client_close(c->client, pdMS_TO_TICKS(CLOSE_WAIT_MS));
        }
        esp_websocket_client_destroy(c->client); /* stops its task: no event after this */
    }
    heap_caps_free(c->buf);
    free(c);
}

static void closer(void *arg) /* a task of its own, so the app task never waits on a connection */
{
    conn_free(arg);
    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

void pc_ws_close(void)
{
    conn_t *c = s_conn;
    if (c == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    s_conn = NULL;
    if (xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(closer, "pc_ws_close", CLOSER_STACK, c, CLOSER_PRIORITY, NULL, 0) != pdPASS) {
        conn_free(c); /* no task for it: here, then */
    }
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "closing");
}

esp_err_t pc_ws_open(const char *host, uint16_t port, const char *token, pc_ws_fn fn, uint32_t *gen)
{
    pc_ws_close();
    conn_t *c = calloc(1, sizeof(*c));
    char *buf = heap_caps_malloc(PC_FRAME_MAX + 1, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
    if (c == NULL || buf == NULL) {
        free(c);
        heap_caps_free(buf);
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }
    char uri[96];
    snprintf(uri, sizeof(uri), "ws://%s:%u/v1/stream", host, (unsigned)port);
    char headers[96]; /* the token as typed: up to 63 characters (spec §12a.1) */
    snprintf(headers, sizeof(headers), "Authorization: Bearer %s\r\n", token);
    esp_websocket_client_config_t cfg = {
        .uri = uri,
        .headers = headers,
        .disable_auto_reconnect = true, /* the session reconnects once, then ends (spec §12a.3) */
        .network_timeout_ms = NETWORK_TIMEOUT_MS,
        .task_stack = TASK_STACK,
        .task_prio = TASK_PRIORITY,
        .buffer_size = 1024,
    };
    *c = (conn_t){ .gen = ++s_gen, .fn = fn, .buf = buf };
    c->client = esp_websocket_client_init(&cfg);
    if (c->client == NULL) {
        conn_free(c);
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }
    esp_err_t err = esp_websocket_register_events(c->client, WEBSOCKET_EVENT_ANY, handler, c);
    if (err == ESP_OK) {
        err = esp_websocket_client_start(c->client);
    }
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        conn_free(c);
        return err;
    }
    s_conn = c;
    *gen = c->gen;
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "opening %s:%u", host, (unsigned)port);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t pc_ws_send(const char *text, size_t len)
{
    if (s_conn == NULL || !esp_websocket_client_is_connected(s_conn->client)) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    }
    int n = esp_websocket_client_send_text(s_conn->client, text, (int)len, pdMS_TO_TICKS(CLOSE_WAIT_MS));
    return n == (int)len ? ESP_OK : ESP_FAIL;
}
```

### Task 12: The session on the device (`main`)

**Files:**
- Create: `main/app_pc.c`
- Modify: `main/CMakeLists.txt`, `main/app_internal.h`, `main/app.c`, `main/app_ui.c`, `main/app_sync.c`, `main/app_menu.c`, `main/app_cmds.c`, `components/ui/include/ui_menu.h`, `components/ui/ui_menu.c`, `components/ui/include/ui_pc.h`, `components/ui/ui_pc.c`, `components/locale/include/lang.h`, `components/locale/lang_en.c`, `components/locale/lang_cs.c`, `test/host/test_ui_menu.c`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything before.
- Produces (`app_internal.h`): `app_pc_status_t`; `app_pc_init()`, `app_pc_presets_changed()`, `app_pc_report(const sync_report_t *)`, `app_pc_tick()`, `app_pc_deadline_ms()`, `app_pc_end(const char *why)`, `app_pc_session()`, `app_pc_keeps_wifi()`, `app_pc_pauses_cycle()`, `app_pc_feed(bool sample)`, `app_pc_ui()`, `app_pc_history()`, `app_pc_status()`, `app_pc_demo(bool)`, `app_pc_state_text()`; `uint8_t app_pc_saved_mode(uint8_t mode)`, `const char *app_pc_saved_preset(void)`, `void app_pc_defer_night(time_t until)`; `void app_ui_begin_night(time_t until)` (`app_ui.c`); `ui_pc_since_text()` (`ui`); `UI_MI_SYNC_PC`, `UI_MI_PC_STATS`, `UI_MI_PC_SESSION`; the console's `pc status | demo on | demo off`.

Spec §12a.4 on the board, with the rules of Task 2:
- **Start**: a sync's PC step that found the PC, before the sync lets Wi-Fi go; the mode and the preset switched as asked; the stream opened, the hello sent when it connects and again when the interval changes.
- **While it runs**: a catalogue replaces the last, is kept in `/fs/state/pc.json` and fills the built-in PC preset once; each readings message is heard, fills the PC's series (a `null` as a break) and redraws a preset that shows a PC value; the auto-cycle waits while the session's preset is on screen; quiet hours keep Wi-Fi (`always_wanted()`); a schedule's night entry waits for the session's end (`app_pc_defer_night()`); the app loop looks at the silence every second.
- **The files keep the owner's own** (Task 2's `pc_session_saved_mode()` and `_preset()`): while a session runs, `settings.json` is written with the sync mode before it if the session set `always`, and `presets.json` names the preset before it as active while the session's preset is on screen. A reboot during a session, an update or a power cycle, comes back as the owner left it: restarts are cold boots, which read the files.
- **End**: the silence (after its one reconnect), the PC app's Quit or refusal (`pc_close_action()`, at once), PC stats off or a new address, a critical battery, the console's `night`; what comes back as Task 2 decides, the PC's series broken; then a schedule's night that came during the session starts, if its end is still ahead (`app_ui_begin_night()`, with the toast "Night until 06:00").
- **History**: its 761 KB of PSRAM when the presets first chart a field, once the board stays awake (`app_pc_presets_changed()` from `come_alive()` and at each change of the presets), never on a routine deep-sleep wake; the indoor values and the battery at each sample, the rest at each sync and each refresh of the house's reading.
- **The PC step's report** is applied before the sync lets a new one start (`app_sync.c`), so a session started from it can't run a nested sync that overwrites the report.
- **`pc demo on`**: the sample PC for screenshots, never a session; an empty PC preset takes its readings for the while and gives them back.

- [ ] **Step 3: The session.** It runs on the app task, which owns the presets, the display and storage; the stream's task only hands its events over (`app_post()`), and a stale stream's are dropped by their generation.

`main/app_pc.c`:

```c
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "app_internal.h"
#include "esp_attr.h"
#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "pc_session.h"
#include "pc_ws.h"
#include "storage.h"
#include "ui_history.h"

/* The PC's sensors on the device (spec §12a, M7, D44): the session a sync's PC step starts, the stream's messages,
 * the catalogue kept in /fs/state/pc.json, and the charts' history. All of it belongs to the app task. */

static const char *TAG = "app_pc";

#define CACHE_PATH "/fs/state/pc.json"
#define CHECK_MS 1000 /* how often a session looks at its silence */

_Static_assert(PC_SESSION_ALWAYS == SETTINGS_SYNC_ALWAYS, "pc_session's `always` is the settings'");

static pc_session_t s_sess;
static pc_catalog_t s_cat; /* the session's catalogue, or the cached one */
static bool s_cat_known;
static pc_values_t s_vals; /* the last readings message's */
static bool s_have_vals;
static int64_t s_heard_ms; /* when it came */
static pc_hwinfo_t s_hw;
static pc_info_t s_info; /* the last /v1/info */
static time_t s_since;   /* when the last session ended (UTC) */
static uint32_t s_gen;   /* the open stream's events */
static bool s_connected;
static char s_why[64]; /* why the stream last closed, or a frame was refused */
static bool s_demo, s_demo_filled;
static bool s_cache_tried; /* a routine wake mounts no storage: the file is read once it is up */
static struct {
    bool enabled;
    char host[SETTINGS_HOST_LEN];
    uint16_t port, interval_ms;
} s_seen; /* the settings the session runs with */
static ui_pc_t s_view;
static ui_history_t s_hist;
static ui_series_t *s_series; /* PSRAM, from the first chart */

typedef pc_ws_msg_t ws_event_t; /* a copy, posted to the app task */

/* ---- the history ---- */

/* The series of the charts every preset shows (spec §5.3): kept, the rest freed; the memory from the first. */
void app_pc_presets_changed(void)
{
    char names[UI_HIST_SERIES][UI_HIST_NAME_LEN];
    const char *list[UI_HIST_SERIES];
    int n = ui_presets_chart_fields(app_presets(), names);
    if (n == 0 && s_series == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    if (s_series == NULL) {
        s_series = heap_caps_calloc(UI_HIST_SERIES, sizeof(ui_series_t), MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
        if (s_series == NULL) {
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "no PSRAM for the charts' history");
            return;
        }
        ui_history_init(&s_hist, s_series);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        list[i] = names[i];
    }
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "history: %d series", ui_history_track(&s_hist, list, n));
}

static bool is_pc_name(const char *name)
{
    return strncmp(name, "pc.", 3) == 0;
}

/* The PC's readings, each series from the message just taken. */
static void feed_pc(uint32_t now_ms)
{
    for (int i = 0; s_series != NULL && i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        ui_series_t *s = &s_series[i];
        if (!s->name[0] || !is_pc_name(s->name)) {
            continue;
        }
        int k = pc_catalog_find(&s_cat, s->name + 3);
        if (k >= 0 && s_vals.has[k]) {
            ui_series_push(s, now_ms, s_vals.v[k]);
        } else {
            ui_series_break(s, now_ms); /* HWiNFO sends none: the line stops */
        }
    }
}

void app_pc_feed(bool sample)
{
    if (s_series == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    ui_context_t ctx;
    app_ui_context(&ctx);
    for (int i = 0; i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        ui_series_t *s = &s_series[i];
        bool local = strncmp(s->name, "env.", 4) == 0 || strncmp(s->name, "bat.", 4) == 0;
        if (!s->name[0] || is_pc_name(s->name) || local != sample) { /* each at its source's own rate */
            continue;
        }
        ui_value_t v;
        ui_resolve(&ctx, ui_field_by_name(s->name), &v);
        if (v.has_num && v.state == UI_VALUE_FRESH) {
            ui_series_push(s, ctx.now_ms, v.num);
        }
    }
}

/* ---- the catalogue's file ---- */

static bool parse_cache(const char *text, void *ctx)
{
    (void)ctx;
    pc_hwinfo_t hw;
    char err[64];
    return pc_parse(text, strlen(text), &s_cat, &s_vals, &hw, err, sizeof(err)) == PC_MSG_CATALOG;
}

static void save_cache(void)
{
    EXT_RAM_BSS_ATTR static char text[PC_FRAME_MAX];
    size_t n = pc_catalog_json(&s_cat, text, sizeof(text));
    esp_err_t err = n > 0 && storage_ready() ? storage_write_atomic(CACHE_PATH, text, n) : ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "%s: %s", CACHE_PATH, esp_err_to_name(err));
    }
}

/* The last catalogue from its file, so the editor lists the PC's fields while the PC is off (spec §12a.3). */
static void load_cache(void)
{
    EXT_RAM_BSS_ATTR static char text[PC_FRAME_MAX + 1];
    s_cache_tried = storage_ready();
    bool from_backup = false;
    s_cat_known = storage_ready() && storage_load(CACHE_PATH, text, sizeof(text), parse_cache, NULL,
                                                  &from_backup) == ESP_OK;
    if (s_cat_known) {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "catalogue: %d readings from %s", s_cat.count, CACHE_PATH);
    }
}

void app_pc_init(void)
{
    pc_session_init(&s_sess);
    load_cache(); /* the history's memory waits until the board stays awake (app_pc_presets_changed()) */
}

/* ---- the session ---- */

static const char *shown_id(void)
{
    const ui_presets_t *p = app_presets();
    return p->presets[p->active].id;
}

static bool shows_pc(void)
{
    const ui_presets_t *p = app_presets();
    const ui_preset_t *active = &p->presets[p->active];
    for (int i = 0; i < ui_preset_slots(active); i++) {
        if (ui_field_is_pc((ui_field_id_t)active->slots[i])) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static void set_mode(uint8_t mode)
{
    settings_t *set = &app_state()->settings;
    if (mode == SETTINGS_SYNC_ALWAYS) {
        settings_toggle_always(set); /* as BOOT double does: the mode before is kept */
    } else {
        set->sync_mode = mode;
    }
    app_ui_save_settings();
    app_clock_moved(0); /* `always` takes Wi-Fi at once; leaving it, it goes */
}

static void select_id(const char *id)
{
    int i = id[0] ? ui_presets_find(app_presets(), id) : -1;
    if (i >= 0) {
        app_ui_select(i, false); /* the session's switch isn't saved, as an auto-cycle switch isn't */
    }
}

static void on_ws(const pc_ws_msg_t *msg); /* on the stream's task */

static void open_stream(void)
{
    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
    char token[SETTINGS_SECRET_LEN];
    app_secret_get(SETTINGS_SECRET_PC_TOKEN, token, sizeof(token));
    s_connected = false;
    s_gen = 0; /* the last stream's events, which may still come, are dropped */
    esp_err_t err = pc_ws_open(set->pc_host, set->pc_port, token, on_ws, &s_gen);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        snprintf(s_why, sizeof(s_why), "%s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "the stream: %s", s_why);
    }
}

static void end_session(const char *why)
{
    if (!s_sess.active) {
        return;
    }
    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
    int64_t night = pc_session_take_night(&s_sess, time(NULL)); /* a schedule's night that came meanwhile */
    pc_end_t e = pc_session_end(&s_sess, set->sync_mode == SETTINGS_SYNC_ALWAYS, shown_id());
    pc_ws_close();
    s_gen = 0;
    s_connected = false;
    s_have_vals = false;
    s_since = time(NULL);
    uint32_t now_ms = (uint32_t)app_uptime_ms();
    for (int i = 0; s_series != NULL && i < UI_HIST_SERIES; i++) {
        if (s_series[i].name[0] && is_pc_name(s_series[i].name)) {
            ui_series_break(&s_series[i], now_ms); /* the next session's line starts apart */
        }
    }
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "session ends: %s%s%s", why, e.restore_mode ? ", the mode back" : "",
             e.select[0] ? ", the preset back" : "");
    if (e.restore_mode) {
        set_mode((uint8_t)e.mode);
    }
    if (e.select[0]) {
        select_id(e.select);
    } else {
        app_ui_render();
    }
    if (night != 0) {
        app_ui_begin_night((time_t)night); /* spec §12a.4: it waited for the session */
    }
}

static void start_session(void)
{
    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
    pc_session_options_t o = { .auto_always = set->pc_auto_always };
    snprintf(o.preset, sizeof(o.preset), "%s", set->pc_preset);
    bool always = set->sync_mode == SETTINGS_SYNC_ALWAYS;
    if (!pc_session_may_start(&o, always)) {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "%s found; a session needs sync mode always", s_info.name);
        return;
    }
    pc_start_t a = pc_session_start(&s_sess, &o, always, set->sync_mode, shown_id(), app_uptime_ms());
    s_seen.enabled = set->pc_enabled;
    snprintf(s_seen.host, sizeof(s_seen.host), "%s", set->pc_host);
    s_seen.port = set->pc_port;
    s_seen.interval_ms = set->pc_interval_ms;
    s_hw = s_info.hwinfo;
    s_why[0] = '\0';
    s_demo = false;
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "session starts with %s%s%s", s_info.name, a.to_always ? ", sync mode always" : "",
             a.select[0] ? ", its preset" : "");
    if (a.to_always) {
        set_mode(SETTINGS_SYNC_ALWAYS);
    }
    open_stream();
    if (a.select[0]) {
        select_id(a.select);
    } else {
        app_ui_render();
    }
}

void app_pc_report(const sync_report_t *r)
{
    if (r->result[SYNC_STEP_PC] != SYNC_STEP_OK || app_state()->critical) {
        return;
    }
    s_info = r->pc;
    if (!s_sess.active) {
        start_session();
    }
}

static void hello(void)
{
    char text[96];
    size_t n = pc_hello(text, sizeof(text), app_settings()->pc_interval_ms);
    if (n > 0 && pc_ws_send(text, n) != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "hello not sent");
    }
}

/* A frame, whole (spec §12a.3). */
static void take_frame(char *frame, size_t len)
{
    char err[64];
    pc_msg_kind_t kind = pc_parse(frame, len, &s_cat, &s_vals, &s_hw, err, sizeof(err));
    int64_t now_ms = app_uptime_ms();
    if (kind == PC_MSG_INVALID) {
        snprintf(s_why, sizeof(s_why), "%s", err);
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "a frame refused: %s; closing", err);
        pc_ws_close(); /* the silence brings the reconnect */
        s_gen = 0;
        s_connected = false;
        return;
    }
    pc_session_heard(&s_sess, now_ms);
    if (kind == PC_MSG_CATALOG) {
        s_cat_known = true;
        s_have_vals = false; /* the values follow the catalogue's order */
        save_cache();
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "catalogue: %d readings", s_cat.count);
        if (ui_presets_fill_pc(app_presets(), &s_cat)) { /* the built-in PC preset's first readings */
            app_ui_save_presets();
            app_pc_presets_changed();
        }
        app_ui_render();
    } else if (kind == PC_MSG_READINGS) {
        s_have_vals = true;
        s_heard_ms = now_ms;
        feed_pc((uint32_t)now_ms);
        if (shows_pc()) {
            app_ui_render(); /* spec §12a.4: a redraw on each message */
        }
    } else if (kind == PC_MSG_STATUS) {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "HWiNFO: %s", pc_hwinfo_name(s_hw));
        app_ui_render();
    }
}

static void apply_ws(void *arg) /* on the app task */
{
    ws_event_t *e = arg;
    if (e->gen == s_gen && s_sess.active) {
        switch (e->ev) {
        case PC_WS_CONNECTED:
            s_connected = true;
            hello();
            break;
        case PC_WS_FRAME:
            take_frame(e->frame, e->len);
            break;
        case PC_WS_TOO_BIG:
            snprintf(s_why, sizeof(s_why), "a frame over 8 KB");
            ESP_LOGW(TAG, "%s; closing", s_why);
            pc_ws_close();
            s_gen = 0;
            s_connected = false;
            break;
        case PC_WS_CLOSED: {
            char why[48];
            bool end = pc_close_action(e->close_code, e->reason, e->http_status, why, sizeof(why)) == PC_CLOSE_END;
            snprintf(s_why, sizeof(s_why), "%s", why);
            ESP_LOGI(TAG, "the stream closed: %s", why);
            s_connected = false;
            if (end) { /* the app quit or turned the board away: at once, not after the silence (spec §12a.3) */
                end_session(why);
            }
            break;
        }
        }
    }
    free(e->frame);
    free(e);
}

static void on_ws(const pc_ws_msg_t *msg)
{
    ws_event_t *e = malloc(sizeof(*e));
    if (e == NULL) {
        free(msg->frame);
        return;
    }
    *e = *msg;
    if (app_post(apply_ws, e) != ESP_OK) { /* the app is busy: the next message comes within the interval */
        free(e->frame);
        free(e);
    }
}

void app_pc_tick(void)
{
    if (!s_cache_tried && !s_cat_known && storage_ready()) {
        load_cache(); /* after a warm wake, for the editor's PC fields */
    }
    if (!s_sess.active) {
        return;
    }
    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
    if (!set->pc_enabled || strcmp(set->pc_host, s_seen.host) != 0 || set->pc_port != s_seen.port) {
        end_session(set->pc_enabled ? "a new address" : "PC stats off");
        return;
    }
    if (set->pc_interval_ms != s_seen.interval_ms) {
        s_seen.interval_ms = set->pc_interval_ms;
        hello(); /* spec §12a.3: sent again when the interval changes */
    }
    switch (pc_session_check(&s_sess, set->pc_interval_ms, app_uptime_ms())) {
    case PC_RECONNECT:
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "silence: reconnecting");
        open_stream();
        break;
    case PC_END:
        end_session("silence");
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

int64_t app_pc_deadline_ms(void)
{
    return s_sess.active ? app_uptime_ms() + CHECK_MS : 0;
}

void app_pc_end(const char *why)
{
    end_session(why);
}

bool app_pc_session(void)
{
    return s_sess.active;
}

bool app_pc_keeps_wifi(void)
{
    return pc_session_keeps_wifi(&s_sess);
}

bool app_pc_pauses_cycle(void)
{
    return pc_session_pauses_cycle(&s_sess, shown_id());
}

uint8_t app_pc_saved_mode(uint8_t mode)
{
    return (uint8_t)pc_session_saved_mode(&s_sess, mode);
}

const char *app_pc_saved_preset(void)
{
    return pc_session_saved_preset(&s_sess, shown_id());
}

void app_pc_defer_night(time_t until)
{
    pc_session_defer_night(&s_sess, until);
}

/* ---- the views ---- */

const ui_pc_t *app_pc_ui(void)
{
    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
    int64_t now_ms = app_uptime_ms();
    s_view = (ui_pc_t){ .session = s_sess.active || s_demo, .catalog = s_cat_known ? &s_cat : NULL,
                        .values = s_have_vals ? &s_vals : NULL,
                        .age_ms = s_demo ? 0 : (uint32_t)(now_ms - s_heard_ms), .interval_ms = set->pc_interval_ms,
                        .since = s_since };
    snprintf(s_view.name, sizeof(s_view.name), "%s", s_info.name);
    return &s_view;
}

const ui_history_t *app_pc_history(void)
{
    return s_series != NULL ? &s_hist : NULL;
}

void app_pc_status(app_pc_status_t *out)
{
    *out = (app_pc_status_t){ .session = s_sess.active, .connected = s_connected, .demo = s_demo,
                              .hwinfo = s_hw, .readings = s_cat_known ? s_cat.count : 0, .since = s_since,
                              .age_ms = s_have_vals ? (uint32_t)(app_uptime_ms() - s_heard_ms) : 0,
                              .set_always = s_sess.set_always };
    snprintf(out->name, sizeof(out->name), "%s", s_info.name);
    snprintf(out->why, sizeof(out->why), "%s", s_why);
    snprintf(out->preset, sizeof(out->preset), "%s", s_sess.preset);
}

/* `pc demo on|off` (spec §12a.4): the sample PC for screenshots, never a session. An empty PC preset takes its
 * readings for the while, and gives them back. */
void app_pc_demo(bool on)
{
    ui_presets_t *p = app_presets();
    int i = ui_presets_find(p, "pc");
    if (on && !s_sess.active) {
        ui_pc_demo(&s_cat, &s_vals);
        s_cat_known = s_have_vals = s_demo = true;
        snprintf(s_info.name, sizeof(s_info.name), "%s", "DEMO-PC");
        s_demo_filled = ui_presets_fill_pc(p, &s_cat);
        app_pc_presets_changed();
        if (s_series != NULL) {
            ui_pc_demo_history(&s_hist, (uint32_t)app_uptime_ms());
        }
    } else if (!on && s_demo) {
        s_demo = s_have_vals = false;
        if (s_demo_filled && i >= 0) {
            for (int k = 0; k < ui_preset_slots(&p->presets[i]); k++) {
                p->presets[i].slots[k] = UI_FIELD_NONE;
                ui_presets_set_visual(p, i, k, NULL);
            }
            app_ui_save_presets();
            app_pc_presets_changed();
        }
        s_demo_filled = false;
        load_cache(); /* the PC's own catalogue again */
    }
    app_ui_render();
}

void app_pc_state_text(char *out, size_t size)
{
    const lang_t *lang = lang_get(app_settings()->language);
    if (s_demo) {
        snprintf(out, size, "%s (demo)", s_info.name);
    } else if (!app_settings()->pc_enabled) {
        snprintf(out, size, "%s", lang_str(lang, LS_PC_OFF));
    } else if (s_sess.active) {
        uint32_t age_s = s_have_vals ? (uint32_t)(app_uptime_ms() - s_heard_ms) / 1000 : 0;
        if (s_have_vals) {
            snprintf(out, size, "%s, %lu s", s_info.name, (unsigned long)age_s);
        } else {
            snprintf(out, size, "%s, %s", s_info.name, s_connected ? "\xE2\x80\xA6" : "\xE2\x80\x94");
        }
    } else if (s_since != 0) {
        ui_context_t ctx;
        app_ui_context(&ctx);
        ui_pc_since_text(&ctx, s_since, out, size);
    } else {
        const app_sync_state_t *sync = &app_state()->sync;
        bool told = sync->last_at != 0 && sync->last_detail[SYNC_STEP_PC][0] != '\0';
        snprintf(out, size, "%s", told ? sync->last_detail[SYNC_STEP_PC] : lang_str(lang, LS_PC_NONE));
    }
}
```


`main/app_internal.h`:

```diff
--- a/main/app_internal.h
+++ b/main/app_internal.h
@@ -17,6 +17,7 @@
 #include "sync_plan.h"
 #include "ui_fields.h"
 #include "ui_menu.h"
+#include "ui_pc.h"
 #include "ui_preset.h"
 #include "ui_radar.h"
 #include "ui_solar.h"
@@ -115,6 +116,7 @@ void app_ui_toast_expire(void);
 
 /* Night sleep (spec §9.1): starts now and ends at `until` (UTC, on a minute). */
 void app_ui_start_night(time_t until);
+void app_ui_begin_night(time_t until); /* a schedule's night: started, with its toast "Night until 06:00" */
 void app_ui_end_night(void);
 bool app_ui_night(void);
 
@@ -244,6 +246,41 @@ void app_flights_tick(bool shown);
 void app_flights_fill(ui_radar_t *ui); /* the last good poll into the view's context */
 void app_flights_status(app_flights_status_t *out);
 
+/* The PC's sensors (main/app_pc.c, spec §12a, M7, D44): the session, its stream and the charts' history. */
+typedef struct {
+    bool session;          /* a session runs */
+    bool connected;        /* its stream is up */
+    bool demo;             /* `pc demo on` */
+    bool set_always;       /* the session switched to sync mode `always` */
+    pc_hwinfo_t hwinfo;    /* HWiNFO's state as the app last said */
+    uint8_t readings;      /* the catalogue's, the cached one included */
+    time_t since;          /* when the last session ended (UTC); 0 = none since the boot */
+    uint32_t age_ms;       /* since the last readings; 0 = none */
+    char name[32];         /* the PC's, from /v1/info */
+    char why[64];          /* why the stream last closed, or a frame was refused */
+    char preset[UI_PRESET_ID_LEN]; /* the preset the session showed; "" for none */
+} app_pc_status_t;
+void app_pc_init(void);            /* the cached catalogue, and the history for the presets' charts */
+void app_pc_presets_changed(void); /* the presets' charts may have changed: their series follow */
+void app_pc_report(const sync_report_t *r); /* a sync's PC step: a session may start (before Wi-Fi goes) */
+void app_pc_tick(void);            /* its silence, its settings; call from the app loop */
+int64_t app_pc_deadline_ms(void);  /* app_uptime_ms() of its next look; 0 without a session */
+void app_pc_end(const char *why);  /* a night, a critical battery */
+bool app_pc_session(void);
+bool app_pc_keeps_wifi(void);      /* quiet hours don't end it (spec §12a.4) */
+bool app_pc_pauses_cycle(void);    /* the session's preset is on screen */
+/* What the files keep while a session runs (spec §12a.4): the mode from before for `mode`, while it is still the
+ * session's `always`; the preset from before while the session's is on screen (NULL: as it is). */
+uint8_t app_pc_saved_mode(uint8_t mode);
+const char *app_pc_saved_preset(void);
+void app_pc_defer_night(time_t until); /* a schedule's night during a session: it starts at the session's end */
+void app_pc_feed(bool sample);     /* the charts' other fields: at a sample (`env.*`, `bat.*`) or a sync (the rest) */
+const ui_pc_t *app_pc_ui(void);    /* what a render draws now */
+const ui_history_t *app_pc_history(void); /* NULL until a chart needs it */
+void app_pc_status(app_pc_status_t *out);
+void app_pc_demo(bool on);         /* `pc demo on|off` */
+void app_pc_state_text(char *out, size_t size); /* Menu ▸ Sync ▸ PC ▸ Session: "DESKTOP-PC, 1 s", "since 18:12" */
+
 /* Config mode (main/app_config.c, spec §10.2). */
 esp_err_t app_net_init(void); /* the Wi-Fi manager, started on first use */
 bool app_net_ready(void);     /* it started: netmgr_status() may be called */
```

     return diag_on_owner(sync_body, argc, argv);
 }
 
+/* `pc status | demo on | demo off` (spec §12a.4, §15, M7): never the token. */
+static int pc_body(int argc, char **argv)
+{
+    static const char *const k_usage = "pc status | pc demo on | pc demo off";
+    if (argc == 3 && strcmp(argv[1], "demo") == 0 && (strcmp(argv[2], "on") == 0 || strcmp(argv[2], "off") == 0)) {
+        app_pc_demo(strcmp(argv[2], "on") == 0);
+        printf("pc: the sample PC %s\n", strcmp(argv[2], "on") == 0 ? "shows" : "is gone");
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (argc != 2 || strcmp(argv[1], "status") != 0) {
+        return usage(k_usage);
+    }
+    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
+    app_pc_status_t s;
+    app_pc_status(&s);
+    char state[48];
+    app_pc_state_text(state, sizeof(state));
+    printf("pc: %s, %s:%u every %u ms, token %s\n", set->pc_enabled ? "on" : "off",
+           set->pc_host[0] ? set->pc_host : "(no address)", set->pc_port, set->pc_interval_ms,
+           app_secret_set(SETTINGS_SECRET_PC_TOKEN) ? "set" : "not set");
+    printf("  session: %s%s%s\n", state, s.session && s.connected ? ", connected" : "",
+           s.set_always ? ", switched to always" : "");
+    if (s.session) {
+        printf("  preset %s, HWiNFO %s, %u readings, the last %lu ms ago\n", s.preset[0] ? s.preset : "(none)",
+               pc_hwinfo_name(s.hwinfo), s.readings, (unsigned long)s.age_ms);
+    } else {
+        printf("  catalogue: %u readings%s\n", s.readings, s.readings ? " (cached)" : "");
+    }
+    if (s.why[0]) {
+        printf("  last trouble: %s\n", s.why);
+    }
+    const app_sync_state_t *sync = &app_state()->sync;
+    static const char *const k_results[] = { "not run", "ok", "failed", "kept" };
+    uint8_t r = sync->last_result[SYNC_STEP_PC];
+    printf("  last PC step: %s%s%s\n", k_results[r < 4 ? r : 0], sync->last_detail[SYNC_STEP_PC][0] ? ": " : "",
+           sync->last_detail[SYNC_STEP_PC]);
+    print_time("  last session ended", s.since);
+    return 0;
+}
+
+static int cmd_pc(int argc, char **argv)
+{
+    return diag_on_owner(pc_body, argc, argv);
+}
+
 /* `radar status | loop` (spec §15, M6). */
 static int radar_body(int argc, char **argv)
 {

git add main components test/host

### Task 13: The PC page and Show as (`web`, `ui`, `main`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `components/ui/ui_catalog.c`, `components/sync/include/sync.h`, `components/sync/sync.c`, `main/app_internal.h`, `main/app_pc.c`, `main/app_sync.c`, `main/app_web.c`, `web/index.html`, `web/app.js`, `web/style.css`, `test/host/test_ui_catalog.c`, `test/web/test_app.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything before.
- Produces:
  - `GET /api/fields`: every field's `numeric` and own range (`min`, `max`), and the PC's readings from the session's or the cached catalogue, as `pc.<id>` in the group `pc`;
  - `GET /api/layouts`: `visuals` (each visual's `min_w` and `min_h`, as `ui_visual_fits()` decides) and `spans`;
  - `GET /api/settings`: `pc.keys.token`; `PATCH` takes `pc.token`;
  - `GET /api/pc`: the session, the catalogue, the last sync's PC step and the last Test;
  - `POST /api/pc/test`: `SYNC_KIND_PC`, the PC step alone (202; 409 with the reason); `app_sync_pc_test()`, `app_pc_tested()`, `app_pc_last_test()`;
  - the page's PC page (`#pc`), Show as in the preset editor, the PC group in its field lists, the PC step on the Sync page.

Spec §10.3. The PC page: PC stats, the address and port, the write-only token, the interval with a warning when it is shorter than the panel's frame time (the refresh rate's period on the Device page), "When the PC is on" (Switch to Always on, Show preset), the session now with the readings, and Test, which asks `/v1/info` at once. Show as offers only the visuals with room in the slot or cell; an empty bound is the field's own, with its range as the placeholder, and a chart's may be "auto"; the span is a list from 10 s to 24 h; a visual goes when its field stops being a number, travels with its field on Split and Join, and goes, with a toast, when a ratio leaves it no room. A slot whose reading the PC no longer sends keeps it in the list as "pc.<id> (not sent now)", with its Show as, so a save never drops what the file holds.

+/* M7 (D44): which fields a visual takes, and the PC's readings from its catalogue, the cached one too. */
+static void test_fields_say_which_are_numbers_and_list_the_pcs_readings(void)
+{
+    ui_context_t ctx = fixture_context();
+    pc_catalog_t cat;
+    pc_values_t vals;
+    ui_pc_demo(&cat, &vals);
+    ui_pc_t pc = { .session = true, .catalog = &cat, .values = &vals, .age_ms = 200, .interval_ms = 1000 };
+    ctx.pc = &pc;
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_catalog_fields_json(&ctx, s_out, sizeof(s_out)) > 0);
+    s_root = cJSON_Parse(s_out);
+    const cJSON *fields = cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(s_root, "fields");
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(UI_FIELD_COUNT - 1 + cat.count, cJSON_GetArraySize(fields));
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(cJSON_IsTrue(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(by_id(fields, "env.temp"), "numeric")));
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(cJSON_IsTrue(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(by_id(fields, "bat.level"), "numeric")));
+    TEST_ASSERT_NULL(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(by_id(fields, "wx.now"), "numeric"));
+    const cJSON *hum = by_id(fields, "env.hum"); /* a field's own range, for the editor's placeholders */
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(0, num(hum, "min"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(100, num(hum, "max"));
+    const cJSON *load = by_id(fields, "pc.cpu_load");
+    TEST_ASSERT_NOT_NULL(load);
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("number", str(load, "kind"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("CPU load", str(load, "label"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("pc", str(load, "group"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("fresh", str(load, "state"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_INT(100, num(load, "max"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(cJSON_IsTrue(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(load, "numeric")));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("21.3 GB", str(by_id(fields, "pc.ram_used"), "value"));
+    cJSON_Delete(s_root);
+    pc.session = false; /* the PC asleep: the cached catalogue still lists them, without a value */
+    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(ui_catalog_fields_json(&ctx, s_out, sizeof(s_out)) > 0);
+    s_root = cJSON_Parse(s_out);
+    fields = cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(s_root, "fields");
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("missing", str(by_id(fields, "pc.cpu_load"), "state"));
+    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL_STRING("", str(by_id(fields, "pc.cpu_load"), "value"));
+}
+
+/* GET /api/layouts publishes the room each visual needs and a chart's spans (spec §5.3), so the editor offers only
+ * what the device takes. */
+static void test_layouts_publish_the_visuals_rules(void)
+{

+esp_err_t app_sync_pc_test(void)
+{
+    const settings_t *set = app_settings();
+    if (app_state()->critical || s_active) {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    if (!set->pc_enabled || set->pc_host[0] == '\0') {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG; /* nothing to ask */
+    }
+    if (!networks_saved()) {
+        return ESP_ERR_NOT_FOUND;
+    }
+    if (app_config_active()) {
+        netmgr_status_t ns;
+        netmgr_status(&ns);
+        if (ns.state != NETMGR_STATION) {
+            return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE; /* only the device's own network: nothing to reach */
+        }
+    }
+    EXT_RAM_BSS_ATTR static sync_request_t req;
+    req = (sync_request_t){ .kind = SYNC_KIND_PC, .steps = SETTINGS_STEPS_ALL,
+                            .pc = { .enabled = true, .port = set->pc_port } };
+    snprintf(req.pc.host, sizeof(req.pc.host), "%s", set->pc_host);
+    app_secret_get(SETTINGS_SECRET_PC_TOKEN, req.pc.token, sizeof(req.pc.token));
+    esp_err_t err = sync_start(&req, done);
+    if (err == ESP_OK) {
+        s_active = true;
+        s_refresh = true; /* not a sync: no mark in the status bar, no history */
+        s_manual = false;
+        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "PC test starts");
+    }
+    return err;
+}
+

+        cJSON_AddStringToObject(e, "unit", r->unit);

+    const app_sync_state_t *sync = &app_state()->sync;
+    cJSON *step = cJSON_AddObjectToObject(o, "step");

+    cJSON_AddStringToObject(step, "result", step_result_name(sync->last_result[SYNC_STEP_PC]));
+    cJSON_AddStringToObject(step, "detail", sync->last_detail[SYNC_STEP_PC]);

+    cJSON_AddStringToObject(test, "result", step_result_name(t->result));

+                     layout: 'pc' }], cycle: { enabled: false, interval_s: 60 }, schedule: { enabled: false, entries: [] } };
+const PC_STATE = { enabled: false, session: false, connected: false, demo: false, set_always: false, name: '',
+                   hwinfo: 'unknown', preset: '', since: 0, age_ms: 0, trouble: '', catalog: [],
+                   step: { at: 0, result: 'not run', detail: '' }, test: { at: 0, result: 'not run', detail: '' },
+                   testing: false };
+
+function pcDevice(patches, settings = PC_SETTINGS, states = [PC_STATE], extra = {}) {
+  let n = 0;
+  return {
+    'GET /api/settings': () => reply(200, settings),
+    'GET /api/presets': () => reply(200, PC_PRESETS),
+    'GET /api/pc': () => reply(200, states[Math.min(n++, states.length - 1)]),
+    'PATCH /api/settings': (init) => { patches.push(JSON.parse(init.body)); return reply(200, settings); },
+    ...extra,
+  };
+}
+
+test('the PC page saves the address, the port, the interval and what a session switches', async () => {
+  const patches = [];
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(pcDevice(patches));
+  await ctx.pcPage();
+  checkNamed(main, 'On').checked = true;
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'Address'), ' 192.168.1.20 ');
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'Port'), '47200');
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'A reading every (ms)'), '2000');
+  checkNamed(main, 'Switch to Always on').checked = false;
+  await change(control(main, 'Show preset'), '');
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  assert.deepEqual(patches.at(-1), { pc: { enabled: true, host: '192.168.1.20', port: 47200, interval_ms: 2000,
+                                           auto_always: false, preset: '' } });
+  assert.deepEqual(options(control(main, 'Show preset')), ['', 'home', 'pc']); /* none, or any preset */
+});
+
+test('the PC\'s token is write-only: the page says whether one is set and sends only what you type', async () => {
+  const patches = [];
+  const settings = { ...PC_SETTINGS, pc: { ...PC_SETTINGS.pc, keys: { token: true } } };
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(pcDevice(patches, settings));
+  await ctx.pcPage();
+  const token = inputNamed(main, 'Token');
+  assert.equal(token.attrs.type, 'password');
+  assert.match(text(main), /A key is set/);
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  assert.equal(patches.at(-1).pc.token, undefined); /* nothing typed: it stays */
+  await type(token, 'ABCDE-FGHIJ-KLMNO-PQRST-UVWXY-Z');
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  assert.equal(patches.at(-1).pc.token, 'ABCDE-FGHIJ-KLMNO-PQRST-UVWXY-Z'); /* as typed (spec §12a.1) */
+});
+
+test('an interval shorter than the panel\'s frame time shows a warning', async () => {
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(pcDevice([]));
+  await ctx.pcPage();
+  const warning = below(main).find((e) => /frame time/.test(e.textContent));
+  assert.ok(warning, 'the warning is there');
+  assert.equal(warning.hidden, true); /* 1 s at 1 Hz */
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'A reading every (ms)'), '500');
+  assert.equal(warning.hidden, false);
+  assert.match(warning.textContent, /1 s at 1 Hz/);
+  const fast = { ...PC_SETTINGS, display: { lpm_hz: 2 } }; /* 500 ms at 2 Hz */
+  const page = await load(pcDevice([], fast));
+  await page.ctx.pcPage();
+  await type(inputNamed(page.main, 'A reading every (ms)'), '500');
+  assert.equal(below(page.main).find((e) => /frame time/.test(e.textContent)).hidden, true);
+});
+
+test('Test asks the PC at once and says what came back', async () => {
+  const found = { ...PC_STATE, session: true, connected: true, name: 'DESKTOP-PC', hwinfo: 'ok',
+                  test: { at: 1800000000, result: 'ok', detail: '' } };
+  const tested = [];
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(pcDevice([], PC_SETTINGS, [PC_STATE, PC_STATE, found], {
+    'POST /api/pc/test': () => { tested.push(true); return reply(202, { started: true }); },
+  }));
+  ctx.setTimeout = (fn) => { fn(); return 0; }; /* sleep() returns at once */
+  await ctx.pcPage();
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Test').click();
+  await settle();
+  assert.equal(tested.length, 1);
+  assert.match(text(main), /Found DESKTOP-PC\./);
+  assert.match(text(main), /HWiNFO\s*running/);
+  const refused = { ...PC_STATE, test: { at: 1800000000, result: 'failed', detail: 'wrong token' } };
+  const page = await load(pcDevice([], PC_SETTINGS, [PC_STATE, refused], {
+    'POST /api/pc/test': () => reply(202, { started: true }),
+  }));
+  page.ctx.setTimeout = (fn) => { fn(); return 0; };
+  await page.ctx.pcPage();
+  await buttonNamed(page.main, 'Test').click();
+  await settle();
+  assert.match(text(page.main), /Not found: wrong token\./);
+});
+
+test('the PC page shows the session and the readings it knows', async () => {
+  const state = { ...PC_STATE, session: true, connected: true, name: 'DESKTOP-PC', hwinfo: 'stale', age_ms: 400,
+                  set_always: true, catalog: [{ id: 'cpu_load', label: 'CPU load', unit: '%' },
+                                              { id: 'ram_used', label: 'RAM', unit: 'GB' }] };
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(pcDevice([], PC_SETTINGS, [state]));
+  await ctx.pcPage();
+  const all = text(main);
+  assert.match(all, /Session\s*running/);
+  assert.match(all, /HWiNFO\s*not updating/);
+  assert.match(all, /400 ms ago/);
+  assert.match(all, /Always on, until the session ends/);
+  assert.match(all, /Readings \(2\)/);
+  assert.match(all, /CPU load pc\.cpu_load · %/);
+});
+
+/* The device's rules for the visuals (GET /api/layouts, ui_catalog.c). */
+const VISUALS = { visuals: { chart: { min_w: 80, min_h: 40 }, bar: { min_w: 60, min_h: 16 },
+                             ring: { min_w: 40, min_h: 40 }, gauge: { min_w: 60, min_h: 40 } },
+                  spans: { min_s: 10, values_max_s: 3600, hours_min_s: 7200, max_s: 86400 } };
+const SHOW_FIELDS = { fields: [{ id: 'time.clock', kind: 'time', label: 'Time', value: '12:15' },
+                               { id: 'env.temp', kind: 'number', label: 'Temperature', value: '23.7 °C', numeric: true },
+                               { id: 'env.hum', kind: 'number', label: 'Humidity', value: '45 %', numeric: true,
+                                 min: 0, max: 100 },
+                               { id: 'pc.cpu_load', kind: 'number', label: 'CPU load', value: '55 %', numeric: true,
+                                 min: 0, max: 100, group: 'pc' }] };
+
+function showDevice(saved, slots, tree) {
+  const catalogue = { ...SPLIT_CATALOGUE, ...VISUALS, layouts: [{ id: 'classic', slots }, { id: 'split', slots: [] }] };
+  const home = tree ? { id: 'home', name: 'Home', layout: 'split', in_cycle: true, split: tree, options: {} }
+                    : { id: 'home', name: 'Home', layout: 'classic', in_cycle: true, slots: { s1: 'env.temp' },
+                        options: {} };
+  const doc = { schema: 1, active: 'home', presets: [home], cycle: { enabled: false, interval_s: 60 },
+                schedule: { enabled: false, entries: [] } };
+  return {
+    'GET /api/layouts': () => reply(200, catalogue),
+    'GET /api/presets': () => reply(200, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(doc))),
+    'GET /api/fields': () => reply(200, SHOW_FIELDS),
+    'POST /api/preview.bmp': () => reply(200, 'BM', 'image/bmp'),
+    'PUT /api/presets': (init) => { saved.push(JSON.parse(init.body)); return reply(200, JSON.parse(init.body)); },
+  };
+}
+const BIG = [{ id: 's1', x: 0, y: 172, w: 200, h: 128, size: 'S', kinds: ['number', 'time'] }];
+
+test('a number offers Show as, with only the visuals that fit its slot', async () => {
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice([], BIG));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  assert.deepEqual(options(control(main, 'Show as')), ['', 'chart', 'bar', 'ring', 'gauge']);
+  const small = [{ id: 's1', x: 0, y: 172, w: 70, h: 30, size: 'XS', kinds: ['number', 'time'] }];
+  const page = await load(showDevice([], small));
+  await page.ctx.presetsPage();
+  assert.deepEqual(options(control(page.main, 'Show as')), ['', 'bar']); /* 70×30: a bar alone */
+});
+
+test('a chart is saved in the preset\'s visuals with its span, bounds and numbers', async () => {
+  const saved = [];
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice(saved, BIG));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  await change(control(main, 'Show as'), 'chart');
+  await change(control(main, 'Span'), '7200');
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'Minimum'), 'auto');
+  await type(inputNamed(main, 'Maximum'), '30');
+  checkNamed(main, 'Numbers over the chart').checked = false;
+  await Promise.all(checkNamed(main, 'Numbers over the chart').listeners.change.map((fn) => fn({ target: { checked: false } })));
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  await settle();
+  assert.deepEqual(saved.at(-1).presets[0].visuals, { s1: { as: 'chart', span_s: 7200, numbers: false, min: 'auto',
+                                                           max: 30 } });
+});
+
+test('a field\'s own range shows where a bound is left empty', async () => {
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice([], BIG));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  const select = below(main).find((e) => e.tag === 'select' && options(e).includes('env.hum'));
+  await change(select, 'env.hum');
+  await change(control(main, 'Show as'), 'gauge');
+  assert.equal(inputNamed(main, 'Minimum').attrs.placeholder, '0');
+  assert.equal(inputNamed(main, 'Maximum').attrs.placeholder, '100');
+  await change(select, 'env.temp'); /* no range of its own: the page says what empty means */
+  await change(control(main, 'Show as'), 'bar');
+  assert.match(text(main), /no range of its own/);
+});
+
+test('a field that isn\'t a number takes its slot\'s visual away', async () => {
+  const saved = [];
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice(saved, BIG));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  await change(control(main, 'Show as'), 'ring');
+  const select = below(main).find((e) => e.tag === 'select' && options(e).includes('time.clock'));
+  await change(select, 'time.clock');
+  assert.ok(!below(main).some((e) => e.tag === 'label' && text(e) === 'Show as'), 'no Show as for a time');
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  await settle();
+  assert.equal(saved.at(-1).presets[0].visuals, undefined);
+});
+
+test('a cell\'s visual goes with its field on Split, and stays out where it has no room', async () => {
+  const saved = [];
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice(saved, BIG, { field: 'env.temp', show: { as: 'chart', span_s: 60,
+                                                                                   numbers: true } }));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Split into rows').click(); /* 400×139: the chart still fits */
+  let p = await savedTree(main, saved);
+  assert.deepEqual(p.split.a, { field: 'env.temp', show: { as: 'chart', span_s: 60, numbers: true } });
+  const page = await load(showDevice(saved, BIG, { field: 'env.temp', show: { as: 'gauge' } }));
+  await page.ctx.presetsPage();
+  const ratio = { split: 'columns', ratio: '1/2', line: true, a: { field: 'env.temp', show: { as: 'gauge' } }, b: {} };
+  const narrow = await load(showDevice(saved, BIG, ratio));
+  await narrow.ctx.presetsPage();
+  await change(ratioSelects(narrow.main)[0], '1/4'); /* 100 px: a gauge's 60 px still fit */
+  p = await savedTree(narrow.main, saved);
+  assert.deepEqual(p.split.a.show, { as: 'gauge' });
+});
+
+test('the field lists group the PC\'s readings', async () => {
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(showDevice([], BIG));
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  const groups = below(main).filter((e) => e.tag === 'optgroup').map((g) => g.attrs.label);
+  assert.ok(groups.includes('PC'), groups.join());
+  assert.ok(below(main).filter((e) => e.tag === 'option').map(text).includes('CPU load — 55 %'));
+});
+
+test('the Sync page switches the PC step too', async () => {
+  const patches = [];
+  const settings = { ...STEP_SETTINGS, sync: { ...STEP_SETTINGS.sync, steps: ['weather', 'pc'] } };
+  const { ctx, main } = await load({
+    'GET /api/settings': () => reply(200, settings),
+    'GET /api/status': () => reply(200, syncStatus({ mode: 'times', running: false })),
+    'PATCH /api/settings': (init) => { patches.push(JSON.parse(init.body)); return reply(200, settings); },
+  });
+  await ctx.syncPage();
+  const box = (name) => below(main).find((e) => e.tag === 'label' && text(e) === name).children[0];
+  assert.equal(box('PC').checked, true);
+  box('PC').checked = false;
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save steps').click();
+  assert.deepEqual(patches.at(-1), { sync: { steps: ['weather'] } });
+});
+
+test('a slot\'s PC reading the PC no longer sends stays, named, with its visual', async () => {
+  const saved = [];
+  const device = showDevice(saved, BIG);
+  const doc = { schema: 1, active: 'home', presets: [{ id: 'home', name: 'Home', layout: 'classic', in_cycle: true,
+                slots: { s1: 'pc.gone' }, visuals: { s1: { as: 'ring' } }, options: {} }],
+                cycle: { enabled: false, interval_s: 60 }, schedule: { enabled: false, entries: [] } };
+  device['GET /api/presets'] = () => reply(200, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(doc)));
+  const { ctx, main } = await load(device);
+  await ctx.presetsPage();
+  const select = below(main).find((e) => e.tag === 'select' && options(e).includes('pc.gone'));
+  assert.ok(select, 'the slot still names it');
+  assert.ok(select.children.some((o) => o instanceof FakeElement && o.value === 'pc.gone' && o.selected));
+  assert.ok(below(main).filter((e) => e.tag === 'option').map(text).some((t) => /^pc\.gone \(not sent now\)$/.test(t)));
+  assert.equal(control(main, 'Show as').value, 'ring');
+  await buttonNamed(main, 'Save').click();
+  await settle();
+  assert.deepEqual(saved.at(-1).presets[0].visuals, { s1: { as: 'ring' } });
+});
```


Run: `node --test test/web/test_app.mjs 2>&1 | grep -E '^# (pass|fail|cancelled)'`

Expected: the new tests fail (or are cancelled where a page doesn't exist yet).

```text
# pass 73
# fail 13
# cancelled 0
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The page.** Plain JS without a build step; `replaceChildren()` takes elements, never an array, which a browser would print as text; a Show as editor keeps its visual current between its controls, as they change one after another without a redraw.

 function route() {
   const name = location.hash.slice(1) || 'status';

 /* The data steps a sync can leave out (D35): the time always runs, as the clock and its trim need it. */
 const STEP_SWITCHES = SYNC_STEPS.slice(2);
 const SYNC_INTERVALS = [15, 30, 60, 120, 180, 360, 720, 1440];
@@ -945,6 +946,163 @@ async function solarPage() {
       '4.0), Solcast (for personal use only, as its terms say). The house\'s readings: SolaX Cloud.' })));
 }
 
+/* ---- PC (spec §10.3, §12a, M7, D44) ---- */
+
+const HWINFO_STATES = { ok: 'running', 'not running': 'not running', 'shm off': 'running, its Shared Memory Support off',
+                        stale: 'not updating', unknown: '—' };
+/* The panel's frame time (ms) at the refresh rate on the Device page: a reading faster than it can't show. */
+const frameMs = (settings) => Math.round(1000 / ((settings.display || {}).lpm_hz || 1));
+const msLabel = (ms) => (ms < 1000 ? `${ms} ms` : `${+(ms / 1000).toFixed(2)} s`);
+
+async function pcPage() {
+  const [s, doc, pc] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/settings'), api('GET', '/api/presets'), api('GET', '/api/pc')]);
+  const set = s.pc || {}, keys = set.keys || {};
+  const enabled = h('input', { type: 'checkbox', checked: !!set.enabled });
+  const host = h('input', { type: 'text', value: set.host || '', placeholder: '192.168.1.20 or desktop-pc.local',
+                            autocapitalize: 'off', spellcheck: 'false' });
+  const conn = { port: set.port ?? 47123, interval: set.interval_ms ?? 1000 };
+  const port = numberInput(conn, 'port', 1, 65535, 1);
+  const interval = numberInput(conn, 'interval', 250, 10000, 50);
+  const frame = frameMs(s);
+  const warning = h('p', { class: 'bad small' });
+  const warn = () => {
+    const ms = Number(conn.interval);
+    warning.hidden = !(ms < frame);
+    warning.textContent = `Shorter than the panel's frame time (${msLabel(frame)} at ${(s.display || {}).lpm_hz || 1} Hz, ` +
+      'on the Device page): the values in between don\'t show.';
+  };
+  interval.addEventListener('input', warn);
+  warn();
+  const token = secretInput('Token', !!keys.token, 'From the PC app\'s page: ABCDE-FGHIJ-…, with or without the dashes.');
+  const always = h('input', { type: 'checkbox', checked: set.auto_always !== false });
+  const preset = choose([['', 'None'], ...doc.presets.map((p) => [p.id, p.name])], set.preset ?? 'pc');
+  const note = h('p');
+  const save = card(null, note, actions(button('Save', () => busy(save, note, async () => {
+    const out = { pc: { enabled: enabled.checked, host: host.value.trim(), port: Number(conn.port),
+                        interval_ms: Number(conn.interval), auto_always: always.checked, preset: preset.value } };
+    const t = token.value();
+    if (t !== undefined) out.pc.token = t;
+    await api('PATCH', '/api/settings', out);
+    note.className = 'good';
+    note.textContent = 'Saved.';
+    toast('Saved');
+  }), 'primary')));
+
+  const nowBox = h('div');
+  const showNow = (x) => {
+    const age = x.age_ms ? msLabel(x.age_ms) + ' ago' : '—';
+    nowBox.replaceChildren(facts([
+      ['Session', x.demo ? 'the sample PC (pc demo)' : x.session ? (x.connected ? 'running' : 'running, reconnecting')
+        : x.since ? `none since ${when(x.since)}` : 'none yet'],
+      x.name ? ['PC', x.name] : null,
+      x.session ? ['HWiNFO', HWINFO_STATES[x.hwinfo] || x.hwinfo] : null,
+      x.session ? ['Last message', age] : null,
+      x.session && x.set_always ? ['Sync mode', 'Always on, until the session ends'] : null,
+      x.trouble ? ['Last trouble', x.trouble] : null,
+      x.step && x.step.at ? ['Last sync\'s PC step', `${x.step.result}${x.step.detail ? `: ${x.step.detail}` : ''}`] : null,
+      x.test && x.test.at ? ['Last test', `${when(x.test.at)}: ${x.test.result === 'ok' ? 'found' : 'not found'}` +
+        `${x.test.detail ? ` (${x.test.detail})` : ''}`] : null,
+    ]), ...(x.catalog && x.catalog.length ? [h('h3', { text: `Readings (${x.catalog.length})` }),
+      h('ul', { class: 'plain small' }, x.catalog.map((r) => h('li', {}, h('b', { text: r.label || r.id }),
+        ` pc.${r.id}${r.unit ? ` · ${r.unit}` : ''}`)))]
+      : [h('p', { class: 'muted small', text: 'No readings yet: they come with the first session, and stay for the ' +
+        'Presets page while the PC is off.' })]));
+  };
+  showNow(pc);
+  const testNote = h('p');
+  const nowCard = card('Now', nowBox, testNote, actions(button('Test', () => busy(nowCard, testNote, async () => {
+    const before = (pc.test || {}).at || 0;
+    await api('POST', '/api/pc/test');
+    testNote.className = 'muted';
+    testNote.textContent = 'Asking the PC…';
+    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { /* joining Wi-Fi first can take some seconds */
+      await sleep(1000);
+      const now = await api('GET', '/api/pc');
+      if ((now.test || {}).at && now.test.at !== before) {
+        showNow(now);
+        const ok = now.test.result === 'ok';
+        testNote.className = ok ? 'good' : 'bad';
+        testNote.textContent = ok ? `Found ${now.name || 'the PC'}.` : sentence(`Not found: ${now.test.detail || 'no answer'}`);
+        return;
+      }
+    }
+    testNote.textContent = 'No answer yet.';
+  }))));
+
+  main.replaceChildren(h('h1', { text: 'PC' }),
+    card('PC stats', h('label', { class: 'check' }, enabled, 'On'),
+      h('p', { class: 'muted small', text: 'The PC runs reflbo-stats, which reads HWiNFO. A sync looks for it; while it ' +
+        'answers, its readings come live.' }),
+      field('Address', host), field('Port', port), token.el,
+      field('A reading every (ms)', interval, '250 ms to 10 s.'), warning),
+    card('When the PC is on', h('label', { class: 'check' }, always, 'Switch to Always on'),
+      h('p', { class: 'muted small', text: 'Without it, a session starts only in sync mode Always on.' }),
+      field('Show preset', preset, 'When the PC goes quiet, what you haven\'t changed comes back.')),
+    save, nowCard);
+}
+
+/* ---- Show as (spec §5.3, §10.3, M7, D44) ---- */
+
+const SHOW_AS = [['', 'Value'], ['chart', 'History chart'], ['bar', 'Bar'], ['ring', 'Ring'], ['gauge', 'Gauge']];
+const SPANS = [10, 30, 60, 300, 900, 1800, 3600, 7200, 21600, 43200, 86400];
+const spanLabel = (s) => (s % 3600 === 0 ? `${s / 3600} h` : s >= 120 && s % 60 === 0 ? `${s / 60} min` : `${s} s`);
+/* Whether a visual has room in w×h, by the rules GET /api/layouts publishes. */
+function showFits(as, w, hgt) {
+  const v = (catalogue.visuals || {})[as];
+  return !!v && w >= v.min_w && hgt >= v.min_h;
+}
+/* A slot's or a cell's visual, for a field that is a number: the visuals that fit, a minimum and maximum (empty for

+/* The field a slot names: its catalogue's entry, or a PC reading the PC no longer sends, still a number. */

### Task 14: On the board, against the PC app's demo on this Mac, and the docs as built

**Files:**
- Modify (only if the checks find something): whatever they point at, each fix with its own test where one can fail first.
- Modify: `docs/specs/2026-09-25-firmware-design.md` (r54, as built), `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/guide.md`, `tools/docs_images.py`, `docs/images/panel/*` (the PC layout and visuals, from the goldens), `docs/images/web/pc.png`, `docs/images/web/presets-show.png`

**Needs the owner first:** the board plugged into this Mac, and agreement that the checks may replace the presets and the settings for a while (the backup brings them back), run syncs on demand (which restarts M5's RTC-trim count, as M6's checks did), and leave the board on M7 for the acceptance below. Step 1 resets the owner's web password, as the owner allows (memory `web-password-reset-ok`), and Step 12 clears the temporary one. The PC app (`~/reflbo-stats-win`, branch or `main` with its plan run) must build here: `go version` 1.26 or later. Ask, and wait.

Before anything else, confirm the port is this board (`ioreg -p IOUSB -l -w0 | grep 'USB Serial Number'` shows `14:C1:9F:54:BB:94`), and note what it has now:

```bash
tools/idf.sh exec python tools/devlog.py --cmd version --cmd "preset list" --cmd "sync status" -o captures/m7-before.log
grep -E 'elf|auto-cycle|mode' captures/m7-before.log
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Config mode, a temporary web password, and the backup.** As M6e's checks did (AGENTS §6): `btn boot long`, a screenshot for the device's network and its password, Menu ▸ Wi-Fi ▸ Reset web password first if the page asks for the owner's, then join and set a throwaway password, in one script that restores the home Wi-Fi whatever happens:

```bash
networksetup -setairportnetwork en0 reflbo-bb94 '<the password on the screen>'
PW=$(openssl rand -hex 8) # never written down; Step 12 clears it
curl -s -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"password\":\"$PW\"}" http://192.168.4.1/api/auth/setup
curl -s -c captures/m7-jar -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"password\":\"$PW\"}" http://192.168.4.1/api/auth/login
curl -s -b captures/m7-jar http://192.168.4.1/api/backup > captures/m7-backup.json
python3 -c 'import json; b=json.load(open("captures/m7-backup.json")); print(sorted(b["files"]), b["firmware"])'
```

Keep this backup for a rollback: `stable-m6d` refuses a `presets.json` with the PC layout, a `pc.<id>` field or more than its 48 KB, loads its `.bak` (an M7 file after M7's second save) or its built-ins, and its next save overwrites the file, as `stable-m6b` did with M6c's files (AGENTS §6). After any later flash of `stable-m6d`, restore this file.

```bash
mkdir -p captures/stable && cp captures/m7-backup.json captures/stable/before-m7-backup.json
```

Expected: 200 twice, then `['presets.json', 'settings.json']` and the running firmware's version. Config mode also joins the home network (gotcha 43), whose address `wifi status` prints; later calls use it as `$B` (`B=http://<that address>`).

tools/idf.sh exec python tools/devlog.py --cmd reboot --until "reflbo ready" -t 30 -o captures/m7-boot.log
grep -E 'presets.json|app_pc|E \(' captures/m7-boot.log
tools/idf.sh exec python tools/devlog.py --cmd version --cmd "preset list" --cmd "pc status"

(A board left in download mode after flashing: gotcha 22.)

## Owner acceptance

M7 is done (spec §18) once the owner has checked, with the expected results, on the owner's PC with HWiNFO Pro and the PC app from its own plan:

1. **The picker:** the PC app's page lists HWiNFO's sensors; seven picked (CPU load and temperature, GPU load and temperature, RAM, VRAM, the network) reach the board's PC page as its readings.
2. **A session at the next sync after the PC wakes:** with the board in its usual sync mode, the first sync after the PC starts switches to Always on and the PC preset; the charts move each second.
3. **Its end when the PC sleeps or the app quits:** within about 15 s of the PC's sleep, or at once after Quit in the app's tray menu, the board goes back to its mode and its preset, and shows "PC asleep" on the PC preset.
4. **Your hand wins:** a preset chosen with KEY during a session stays at its end.
5. **The visuals on the panel:** the PC layout's charts, numbers and bars read well from the desk, with the chart's line sloping from value to value where the spike drew steps; a ring and a gauge in another preset too.
6. **Auto-start and Quit** (the PC app's own checks, its spec §10).
7. **A session's power:** the USB meter's average over 10 minutes of a session, against sync mode Always on without one (spec §9.4), into `docs/power.md`.

After the acceptance, M7's build may become the stable firmware (`stable-m7`), which M8's MQTT and Home Assistant tests then flash back.
